# First-party live chat (Worker + Durable Objects) — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** One chat room per enabled tracker, served by the existing `watchboard-push` Worker on a new host `chat.watchboard.dev` through SQLite-backed Durable Objects with hibernatable WebSockets; reading is anonymous, writing needs a GitHub session; comments carry no tier and live in a collapsed "Community · unverified · not a source" panel; moderation is built for one person and degrades to read-only when nobody is watching; cost ceiling is 0 USD (Workers Free). Everything ships **disabled** first.

**Architecture:** `worker/index.ts` dispatches on `url.hostname` before its existing path `switch`: `chat.watchboard.dev` goes to an isolated module `worker/chat/router.ts`, every other host keeps today's push/newsletter behaviour byte for byte (regression-tested). The chat router refuses everything with 503 unless the secret `CHAT_ENABLED` is exactly `"true"`, and 404s any room slug not in the `CHAT_ROOMS` var **before** calling `idFromName`. Two SQLite DO classes: `ChatRoom` (one per `tracker:{slug}`; messages, counters, room mode, local ban cache; hibernatable WebSockets, per-socket state in `serializeAttachment`, one `alarm` for retention/attended hours) and `ChatMod` (singleton `global`; bans, reports, decisions, global mode, term list, erasure fan-out). Pure logic (normalisation, filters, rate limits, session tokens, Origin checks) lives in plain modules unit-tested inside the Workers runtime. The wire protocol is a dependency-free parser in `src/lib/community/protocol.ts`, imported by both the Worker and the site island. On the site, `ChatPanel.tsx` mounts only when `PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED === 'true'` at build time **and** the tracker has `community.chat: true`; it opens no socket until the reader opens the panel. A pure client state machine (`chat-client.ts`) owns reconnect/backoff/ack timeouts and is unit-tested in node; Playwright covers the rendered panel.

**Tech Stack:** Cloudflare Workers + Durable Objects (SQLite backend, WebSocket Hibernation API), `wrangler@4.124.0`, `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers@0.22.0`, `vitest@4.1.0`, `@cloudflare/workers-types` — all in a **separate `worker/package.json`** (see Deviations D1). Site: Astro 5 static, React 19 islands, TypeScript, Vitest 4 (node env), Playwright, GitHub Actions.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-live-chat-design.md` (cited below as "spec §N"). Research on alternatives, moderation, DSA, minors, GDPR, CSP and the tier promise is reused from `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-live-comments-design.md` (cited as "09-24 §N"), not redone.

---

## Owner gates

Question numbers are the spec's §14 numbers: **Qn here = Pn in the spec**.
An executor that reaches a task or step marked `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Qn`
stops, reports which question is open, and does not guess a value. No gated
value has a default in code: where a value is needed, the code reads it from
a Worker var/secret or a constant that the gated step writes.

| Task | Gate |
|---|---|
| 1-4 | none — foundations and bug fixes, no chat visible, nothing deployed |
| 5 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q12` (CI deploy token) |
| 6 | ADR-0003 is merged by the owner (the merge is the acceptance; spec §12 step 1) |
| 7 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q1, Q2, Q6-jurisdiction` — before any chat code: the pilot slugs, the success/retirement numbers N and M (spec §12 says what the pilot can and cannot prove), and whether the DOs live in the EU jurisdiction (objects never move after creation, so this must precede the first `idFromName`). The answers go into ADR-0003 first. |
| 8-14 | none beyond Task 7 — dark backend, `CHAT_ENABLED` unset, only room `_canary` |
| 15 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q5` for step 15.5 only (attended hours) |
| 16 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q3` (identity provider + minimum account age) |
| 17 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q13` (who is in `CHAT_ADMIN_IDS`) |
| 18 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q6` (contact address for the notice form) |
| 19-20, 22-25 | none — canary, dark panel, `PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED` unset everywhere; step 19.5 (canary on in production) needs the owner's go-ahead in the conversation |
| 21 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q10` for the Docker default only (step 21.4) |
| 26 | owner runs the rehearsal (spec §12 step 4); `Q8` decides Turnstile on `/notice`; the path-based WAF rule (26.6) is a precondition of Task 27, not optional |
| 27 | `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q5, Q6, Q7, Q11` (pilot; Q1 and Q2 were answered at Task 7) |

Out of scope for this plan (spec "fases posteriores"): Bluesky OAuth, event
references inside a room, Workers AI (Q9), per-locale rooms (Q4 proposal is
one room for all locales; if the owner picks per-locale rooms, Tasks 7 and
20 are re-planned, not improvised).

## Verified facts this plan depends on

Checked on 2026-09-25 while writing this plan; Task 6 re-checks the
Cloudflare figures and copies them into ADR-0003 with the date.

- **Cloudflare Durable Objects, Workers Free** (`https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/platform/pricing/`):
  requests "100,000 / day"; duration "13,000 GB-s / day"; rows read
  "5 million / day"; rows written "100,000 / day"; storage "5 GB (total)";
  "Only Durable Objects with SQLite storage backend are available";
  "If you exceed any one of the free tier limits, further operations of
  that type will fail with an error"; "a 20:1 ratio is applied to incoming
  WebSocket messages". Matches spec §7.1.
- **Local DO testing is viable.** In a scratch project:
  `npm i -D vitest@4.1.0 @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers@0.22.0 wrangler@4.124.0 @cloudflare/workers-types`
  installed cleanly — **because the types package was left unpinned** and
  resolved to `5.x`; a `^4.x` pin fails with ERESOLVE (adversarial review
  #1, re-verified 2026-09-26), so Task 1 pins `^5.20260815.1` (`npm view @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers peerDependencies`
  → `vitest ^4.1.0`, matching the repo's `vitest ^4.1.0`). A test with a
  SQLite DO (`new_sqlite_classes`), `ctx.acceptWebSocket`,
  `serializeAttachment`, `evictDurableObject(stub)` and
  `runInDurableObject(stub, (_, state) => state.storage.sql.exec(...))`
  **passed**: the attachment counter survived eviction and the rows were
  readable afterwards. API is the Vitest-4 one: `cloudflareTest()` plugin
  from `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers`, `env`/`exports` from
  `cloudflare:workers`, helpers from `cloudflare:test`.
- **Compatibility date ceiling:** the workerd bundled with that miniflare
  (`5.20260815.0-alpha`) refuses `compatibility_date = "2026-09-01"`:
  "the newest date supported by this server binary is \"2026-08-22\"".
  This plan pins **`2026-08-22`**.
- **Config must be ESM:** a `vitest.config.ts` in a package without
  `"type": "module"` fails ("This package is ESM only"). `worker/package.json`
  declares `"type": "module"`.
- **Handler return values:** `addEventListener('message', e => arr.push(...))`
  logs "An event handler returned a value"; test listeners use a block body.
- **Repo facts** (this branch): `wrangler.toml` has no DO, `compatibility_date = "2024-09-01"`;
  `worker/index.ts:59-100` routes by path only; root `vitest.config.ts:11`
  includes only `src/`, `tests/`, `scripts/`; `tsconfig.json` extends
  `astro/tsconfigs/strict` with `types: ["node"]`; `.github/workflows/test.yml`
  runs `npm ci && npm test`; `src/lib/live-layers.ts:35` renderer enum has no
  `panel`; `layersForTracker()` (`:275-277`) returns every `scope: []` entry,
  so a panel entry would leak into `IntelMap.tsx:194` and `CesiumGlobe.tsx:749`
  unless it is filtered; `feedUrls()` (`:280-284`) feeds the CSP test;
  `connect-src` in `public/_headers:16` lacks `https://push.watchboard.dev`
  while `src/lib/push-client.ts` fetches it (09-24 P9, spec §1);
  `MobileTabShell.tsx:16` `TAB_ORDER = ['map','feed','data','intel']`;
  `Dockerfile` runs `npm ci` then `npm run build` on `node:22-alpine`;
  translations are `const en/es/fr/pt` objects in `src/i18n/translations.ts`
  typed `Record<keyof typeof en, string>` (a missing key is a type error);
  `docs/runbooks/` does not exist; ADRs are written in Spanish.

## Global Constraints

All values live in `worker/chat/limits.ts` (Worker) or
`src/lib/community/protocol.ts` (shared) — never re-declared elsewhere.

- **Host:** `CHAT_HOST = 'chat.watchboard.dev'`. Routes: `GET /room/{slug}` (WebSocket upgrade), `GET /health`, `GET /auth/github/start`, `GET /auth/github/callback`, `POST /auth/logout`, `GET|POST /notice`, `GET|POST /admin/*`, `POST /me/erase`, `GET /me/export`.
- **Kill switches:** secret `CHAT_ENABLED` — rooms serve only when it is exactly the string `"true"`; absent/anything else → room upgrades get close `4003` (and a plain `GET /room/*` gets `503 {"error":"chat_disabled"}`) before any DO access, and `/auth/github/start` refuses everyone except the admin sign-in (`?return=/__admin`). `/health`, `/admin/*`, `/notice*`, `/me/*`, `/auth/github/callback` and `/auth/logout` keep working while it is off, so the owner can moderate, accept DSA notices and honour erasures during an incident (review #32). Var `CHAT_ROOMS` — comma-separated slugs; initial value `"_canary"`. Build var `PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED` — the panel mounts only when exactly `"true"`.
- **Room ids:** `roomName(slug) = 'tracker:' + slug`; slug regex `/^(_canary|[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63})$/`; `ChatMod` id `idFromName('global')`.
- **Origins:** var `CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, comma-separated, production value `"https://watchboard.dev"`; `wrangler dev` / tests add `http://localhost:4321` through `.dev.vars` / the test config only. Upgrade with a missing or foreign `Origin` → 403 before the DO.
- **Sizes:** `MAX_BODY_CHARS = 500` (after normalisation), `MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 4096` (bigger → close 1009), `HISTORY_LIMIT = 100`, `ACK_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000`.
- **Rate limits (spec §3.7):** burst 1 msg / `8_000` ms (slow mode `60_000` ms); `20` msgs / hour / identity / room; `100` msgs / UTC day / identity (see D3); `10` reports / hour / identity; room `40` msgs / minute → auto `slow` for `15` min + alert; room `1_000` accepted msgs / UTC day → `readonly` reason `daily_budget` until 00:00 UTC (lowered from the spec's 1,500 until Task 13.8 measures rows written per send; see D11); **no daily connection cap** (D10); `300` concurrent sockets per room, of which at most `200` anonymous (over → `hello` + close `4004`); `3` consecutive **malformed** frames → close `4008`; rate-limit/readonly rejections never close the socket.
- **Close codes (client contract):** `1008` banned (no retry); `4002` closed by owner/global (no retry, shows "closed"); `4003` chat disabled by lever 3 (no retry); `4004` room full (no auto-retry); `4008` malformed frames (no retry, "reload the page"); `4009` session revoked (reconnect at once, anonymously). Refusals the router or room knows about are sent **inside** an accepted WebSocket (one `mode`/`err` frame, then the close), because a browser cannot read an HTTP status from a failed handshake (review #36).
- **Protocol version:** `PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1`, sent in `hello.v`. Parsers on both ends **ignore unknown keys** and build their output from known fields only; a field is never removed in the same release that stops using it; the Worker deploys first (review #39).
- **Budget:** `CHAT_MAX_ROOMS = 5` (site build test and Worker startup check); alert at `0.7` of any room budget; per-send ceilings asserted in tests: rows written ≤ 9, rows read ≤ 150 with 10,000 messages in the table; anonymous connect: rows written 0.
- **Identity:** uid format `'gh:' + github numeric id`; handle shown `'gh/' + login`; minimum account age `CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS` (Worker var, **no default**, set by Task 16 from Q3); reserved handles `['watchboard','admin','mod','moderator','reuters','ap','bbc','afp','cnn','aljazeera']` compared case-insensitively on the login.
- **Session:** cookie `__Host-wb_chat` (`Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=2592000`), token `base64url(json) + '.' + base64url(HMAC-SHA256)` with secret `CHAT_SESSION_KEY`, payload `{uid, handle, iat, exp, gen}`; OAuth state cookie `__Host-wb_oauth` (`Max-Age=600`), PKCE S256, scope `read:user` only.
- **Ban keys:** `uidHmac(uid) = hex(HMAC-SHA256(CHAT_BAN_KEY, uid))`; never a public salt (09-24 §7, `:626-632`).
- **Retention (spec §3.8, confirmed by Q11 before the pilot):** visible `30` days; held `72` h, then a content-free stub with its automated statement of reasons for the rest of the 30 days; removed rows keep `body = ''`; reports and decisions `183` days; bans `≤ 365` days; messages under an open report are never deleted by retention and their text moves to `evidence` when the author deletes or erases (spec §6.2); no IP stored anywhere; Worker `observability` stays off for the whole Worker.
- **Modes:** `open | slow | readonly | closed`; reasons `owner | global | daily_budget | auto_slow | filters_unavailable | attended_hours | unreviewed_report`. A `closed` room sends an empty history (so lever 1/2 also disables access to content, TCO art. 3).
- **Error codes (stable, i18n keys `chat.err.{code}`):** `rate_limited`, `too_long`, `empty`, `banned`, `auth_required`, `account_too_new`, `readonly`, `closed`, `bad_frame`, `not_author`, `report_limited`, `duplicate`, `shouting`, `unknown`, `daily_budget`, `room_full`, `chat_disabled`.
- **Hold codes:** `slur | pii | link | duplicate | reports | grave_report | language`.
- **Stub creation:** every `idFromName` goes through `worker/chat/stubs.ts` (`roomStub`, `modStub`), which applies `.jurisdiction('eu')` when the var `CHAT_JURISDICTION = "eu"` (Q6 at Task 7). A root test greps `worker/chat` for any other `idFromName`.
- **Alerts:** only to secret `TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID` via `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`; never message text, handle or uid — only room, category, count and the `/admin` URL. The string `TELEGRAM_CHANNEL_ID` must not appear under `worker/` (test).
- **Tier separation (09-24 §3, spec §5.2):** `ChatMessage` has no `tier|source|sourceTier|pole|contested|media|confidence|verified` key; nothing under `src/lib/schemas.ts`, `scripts/`, `mcp/`, `video/`, `src/pages/api/`, `src/pages/**/rss*`, `src/pages/feeds*` imports `lib/community/`; panel CSS never references `--tier-`, `--accent-red`, `--accent-amber`, `.source-chip`, `.freshness-indicator`, `SourceStatusChip` or a monospace font. Enforced by Task 9 and Task 23.
- **No tracking:** no PostHog call, no `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`/`document.cookie` in any community file (the session cookie is set by the Worker on its own host). Enforced by Task 9.
- **No silent success (`docs/silent-failure-patterns.md`):** every test asserts on the artefact — the SQLite row, the frame another socket received, the header the server sent, the HTML in `dist/`. `ack` is sent only after the SQL write returns; erasure reports the count it re-reads.
- **Type check:** root `npx astro check` OOMs locally; use `npx tsc --noEmit -p . 2>&1 | grep -E 'community|live-layers|tracker-config|MobileTab|translations'` (must print nothing) for the site and `npm --prefix worker run typecheck` for the Worker.
- **Git:** feature branch per task group (`feat/chat-foundations`, `feat/chat-backend`, `feat/chat-panel`), never `main`. Commits end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Paths with `[tracker]` go through `git add --literal-pathspecs -- "src/pages/[tracker]/index.astro"` (memory: pathspec bracket trap).
- **Env-var table:** every task that adds a `process.env.X` / `import.meta.env.X` read ends with `npx tsx scripts/list-env-vars.ts --write docs/self-hosting.md && npx tsx scripts/list-env-vars.ts --check docs/self-hosting.md`.

## Deviations from the spec

Each is recorded again in ADR-0003 (Task 6).

- **D1 — `worker/` becomes its own npm package** instead of adding
  `wrangler` and the pool to the root `devDependencies` (spec §11.2/§12
  step 0). Reason: the root `Dockerfile` runs `npm ci` on `node:22-alpine`;
  `workerd` ships glibc binaries and its postinstall would put ~100 MB and a
  musl risk into the self-hosting image for code the image never runs. The
  Worker's tests therefore run through `npm --prefix worker test`, which
  `npm test` at the root calls (Task 1) so CI cannot skip them.
- **D2 — the protocol parser is dependency-free, not Zod** (spec §3.4 says
  Zod on both ends). Reason: `src/lib/community/protocol.ts` is compiled by
  the site (root `zod@3`) and by the Worker package (no zod). Importing
  `zod` from a file under `src/` would resolve to the root `node_modules`,
  which the Worker's deploy job does not install. Hand-written guards with
  exhaustive tests (Task 8) give the same rejection behaviour.
- **D3 — the per-identity daily cap (100) is counted per room**, not across
  rooms. Reason: a cross-room count needs a `ChatMod` call on every send
  (one extra DO request per message) for a cap that, with ≤ 5 rooms, is at
  most 5× looser. The hourly cap (20/room) still binds first. If the pilot
  shows abuse spread across rooms, this is revisited.
- **D4 — bans are pushed to rooms and reconciled** (`ChatMod` fans out to
  every room in its **registry** — each room registers itself on first use,
  so a room later removed from `CHAT_ROOMS` still receives erasures, bans and
  global modes — and each room keeps a `bans` table) rather than rooms reading
  `ChatMod` per message; same reason as D3. Because a fan-out RPC can fail or
  a room can be new, each room also **pulls** `ChatMod.syncState()` (global
  mode, ban list version, term list) when a socket connects more than 60 s
  after the last pull and in every alarm; if ChatMod is unreachable the room
  refuses writes (`readonly`, reason `global`) until a pull succeeds
  (adversarial review #7, #33).
- **D5 — root `vitest.config.ts` does not include `worker/**`** (spec §11.1
  says it should). Worker tests need the Workers runtime (`cloudflare:test`),
  which the root node environment cannot load; they run in
  `worker/vitest.config.ts` instead and Task 2 adds a guard test that fails
  if `npm test` stops calling them.
- **D6 — no licence-plate pattern in the PII filter** (spec §4.1.5 lists
  "matrículas"). Reason: plate formats differ per country and a generic
  letters-digits pattern matches weapon designations that fill a conflict
  site ("F-16", "S-400", "M142"), holding normal messages all day. Plates
  are handled by reports (`personal_data`) and the term list instead.
- **D7 — the admin queue does not show account age** (spec §4.4 asks for
  it). Reason: `created_at` is checked once at sign-in and never stored;
  storing it only to display it widens the personal data held (spec §6.3).
- **D8 — the panel e2e plays the room with `page.routeWebSocket`** instead
  of a local `wrangler dev` (spec §11.3). Reason: the panel's room URL is
  the production host, and a local Worker would need dev hosts in the CSP
  and a second OAuth app. The Worker is tested in workerd (Tasks 7-19) and
  the real panel↔Worker path in the rehearsal and pilot (Tasks 26-27).
- **D9 — the rehearsal runs against production's `_canary` room with a
  script client** (spec §12 step 4 offers "a test deployment, or preview +
  wrangler dev"). Same reason as D8; `_canary` is never mounted on the site.
- **D10 — anonymous connections have no daily cap** (spec §3.2/§7.3 had
  5,000 connections/day → `readonly`). A hard daily cap that any script can
  reach closes the room to everyone until 00:00 UTC. Instead anonymous
  sockets are capped **concurrently** at `maxAnonSockets` (200 of 300), which
  leaves 100 slots only signed-in readers can take; over the cap an anonymous
  reader gets `hello` with history and a `4004` close (shown as "room full —
  showing the latest messages", no auto-retry). Connections are still counted
  (in memory, flushed hourly) for the owner's log. Adversarial review #4, #28.
- **D11 — the room's hot counters are not one SQL row each.** Per-identity
  usage is one `WITHOUT ROWID` row per uid (one row written per send); room
  counters (messages, connections, the minute window) live in memory and in
  one `room` row written at most once per send. Every SQL statement goes
  through a wrapper that adds `cursor.rowsRead`/`rowsWritten` to per-room
  totals, and Task 13 tests assert ceilings on them (review #2, #3, #27).
- **D12 — the term list is cached in the room's SQLite**, pushed by `ChatMod`
  (`termsChanged(list)`) and pulled by the room on connect/alarm, so `onSend`
  never awaits another object between its checks and its writes (review #6).
- **D13 — grave reports hide the message, not the room** (spec §4.5 said a
  60-minute-old grave report made the room `readonly`). One report, from
  anyone, could silence a room while leaving the reported content visible.
  Now a grave report from a signed-in reader, or an anonymous notice of kind
  `csam`/`terrorism`/`threat`, provisionally holds that message at once
  (rate-limited, restorable); the room goes `readonly` only on volume
  (≥ 3 grave-held messages unreviewed for > 60 min). Review #15, #31.
- **D14 — sign-out signs out everywhere.** Every logout bumps the session
  generation and closes that user's open sockets; there is no per-device
  logout, because a copied 30-day token otherwise stays valid (review #41).

### Task 1: Worker package, local test harness, push regression tests

No gate. Branch `feat/chat-foundations`. Nothing is deployed.

**Files:**
- Create: `worker/package.json`, `worker/tsconfig.json`, `worker/vitest.config.ts`, `worker/test/push-routes.test.ts`, `worker/.gitignore`
- Modify: `wrangler.toml:6` (compatibility date)

**Interfaces:** none new; the test pins today's behaviour of `worker/index.ts` `fetch` for `GET /`, `OPTIONS *`, `GET /nope`, `GET /subscribe`.

- [ ] **1.1 Create the package.** `worker/package.json`:

```json
{
  "name": "watchboard-worker",
  "private": true,
  "type": "module",
  "scripts": {
    "test": "vitest run",
    "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit -p .",
    "deploy": "wrangler deploy --config ../wrangler.toml"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers": "0.22.0",
    "@cloudflare/workers-types": "^5.20260815.1",
    "typescript": "^5.9.3",
    "vitest": "4.1.0",
    "wrangler": "4.124.0"
  }
}
```

The types pin is load-bearing: `npm view wrangler@4.124.0 peerDependencies` → `{ '@cloudflare/workers-types': '^5.20260815.1' }` (peerOptional). With a `^4.x` pin, `npm install` fails with `ERESOLVE … peerOptional @cloudflare/workers-types@"^5.20260815.1" from wrangler@4.124.0` (reproduced 2026-09-26 in a scratch directory with this exact file); with `^5.20260815.1` it resolves. Task 2's guard test pins all three.

`worker/.gitignore`: `node_modules/` and `.dev.vars` (one per line).

`worker/tsconfig.json`:

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "es2022",
    "module": "es2022",
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "strict": true,
    "noEmit": true,
    "skipLibCheck": true,
    "lib": ["es2022"],
    "types": ["@cloudflare/workers-types", "@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers/types"]
  },
  "include": ["**/*.ts", "../src/lib/community/protocol.ts", "../src/lib/community/types.ts"]
}
```

Run: `npm --prefix worker install` → creates `worker/package-lock.json`. Expected: exit 0; `ls worker/node_modules/.bin/wrangler` exists.

- [ ] **1.2 Write the failing regression test.** `worker/test/push-routes.test.ts`:

```ts
import { exports } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

const PUSH = 'https://push.watchboard.dev';
const call = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) =>
  (exports as { default: Fetcher }).default.fetch(PUSH + path, init);

describe('push host keeps today\'s behaviour', () => {
  it('GET / lists the push endpoints', async () => {
    const res = await call('/');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin')).toBe('https://watchboard.dev');
    const body = (await res.json()) as { service: string; endpoints: string[] };
    expect(body.service).toBe('Watchboard Push Notifications');
    expect(body.endpoints).toContain('/subscribe');
    expect(body.endpoints).toContain('/newsletter/subscribe');
  });

  it('OPTIONS answers 204 with CORS', async () => {
    const res = await call('/subscribe', { method: 'OPTIONS' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(204);
    expect(res.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Methods')).toContain('POST');
  });

  it('unknown path is 404 JSON', async () => {
    const res = await call('/nope');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'Not found' });
  });

  it('GET /subscribe returns the VAPID public key binding', async () => {
    const res = await call('/subscribe');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.text()).toContain('test-vapid-public');
  });

  // The compat-date bump (2024-09-01 → 2026-08-22) must not break the paths push really uses (spec §3.1).
  it('POST /subscribe stores the subscription and its reverse index in KV', async () => {
    const sub = { endpoint: 'https://push.example/abc', keys: { p256dh: 'p', auth: 'a' } };
    const res = await call('/subscribe', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ subscription: sub, trackers: ['cdmx'] }) });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const { subKey } = (await res.json()) as { subKey: string };
    const kv = (env as unknown as { PUSH_SUBSCRIPTIONS: KVNamespace }).PUSH_SUBSCRIPTIONS;
    expect(JSON.parse((await kv.get(subKey))!)).toMatchObject({ endpoint: sub.endpoint, trackers: ['cdmx'] });
    expect(JSON.parse((await kv.get('tracker-subs:cdmx'))!)).toContain(subKey);
  });

  it('the cron path fetches the RSS and records the seen GUIDs (network mocked)', async () => {
    const rss = '<rss><channel><item><title>T</title><link>https://watchboard.dev/cdmx/</link><guid>g-1</guid><description>d</description></item></channel></rss>';
    const spy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockImplementation(async (input) =>
      String(input) === 'https://watchboard.dev/rss.xml' ? new Response(rss) : new Response('{}', { status: 404 }));
    const res = await handleCron(env as never);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(spy.mock.calls.some(([u]) => String(u) === 'https://watchboard.dev/rss.xml')).toBe(true);
    const kv = (env as unknown as { PUSH_SUBSCRIPTIONS: KVNamespace }).PUSH_SUBSCRIPTIONS;
    expect(JSON.parse((await kv.get('last-seen-guids'))!)).toContain('g-1');
    spy.mockRestore();
  });
});
```

Add to the imports: `import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';`, `vi` from `vitest`, and `import { handleCron } from '../handlers/cron';`. If `parseRssItems` in `worker/handlers/cron.ts` needs other tags to accept an item, read it and extend the fixture — the test pins today's behaviour, it does not change it.

Run: `npm --prefix worker test`. Expected: FAIL — "Could not find config file" / no `vitest.config.ts`.

- [ ] **1.3 Add the harness.** `worker/vitest.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
import { cloudflareTest } from '@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers';

// Runs inside workerd via miniflare: real DOs, real SQLite, real WebSockets.
// Secrets that production sets with `wrangler secret put` are test values here.
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    cloudflareTest({
      wrangler: { configPath: '../wrangler.toml' },
      miniflare: {
        bindings: {
          VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY: 'test-vapid-public',
          VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY: 'test-vapid-private',
        },
      },
    }),
  ],
  test: { include: ['test/**/*.test.ts', 'chat/**/*.test.ts'] },
});
```

If the second passes, storage is isolated per test: write "isolated per test" in the file's header comment. If it fails, change the assertion to `'1'`, write "shared within a file" in the header comment, and from Task 13 on every DO test restores what it changed in `afterEach` (Task 13.1's helpers provide `resetChat()` for that). Either way the result is recorded in ADR-0003 (Task 6), not assumed.

No gate. Same branch.

- [ ] **2.1 Failing guard test.** `tests/ci/worker-tests-wired.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

const ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '../..');
const read = (p: string) => readFileSync(resolve(ROOT, p), 'utf8');

describe('Worker tests are wired into CI', () => {
  it('root npm test also runs the worker package tests', () => {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(read('package.json')) as { scripts: Record<string, string> };
    expect(pkg.scripts.test).toContain('npm --prefix worker test');
  });
  it('test.yml installs the worker package before npm test', () => {
    const yml = read('.github/workflows/test.yml');
    const ci = yml.indexOf('npm --prefix worker ci');
    const test = yml.indexOf('npm test');
    expect(ci).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    expect(ci).toBeLessThan(test);
  });
  it('the worker package pins the pool that was verified', () => {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(read('worker/package.json')) as { devDependencies: Record<string, string> };
    expect(pkg.devDependencies['@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers']).toBe('0.22.0');
    expect(pkg.devDependencies.wrangler).toBe('4.124.0');
    // wrangler 4.124.0 declares peerOptional @cloudflare/workers-types ^5.20260815.1; a 4.x pin fails npm install.
    expect(pkg.devDependencies['@cloudflare/workers-types']).toBe('^5.20260815.1');
  });
  it('the Docker build context never ships the worker toolchain', () => {
    expect(read('.dockerignore').split('\n').map((l) => l.trim())).toContain('worker/node_modules');
  });
});
```

Run: `npx vitest run tests/ci/worker-tests-wired.test.ts`. Expected: 3 FAIL (scripts.test, test.yml, .dockerignore), 1 PASS.

- [ ] **2.2 Wire it.** Root `package.json`: `"test": "vitest run && npm run test:worker"` and `"test:worker": "test -d worker/node_modules || { echo 'worker deps missing: run npm --prefix worker ci' >&2; exit 1; }; npm --prefix worker test"` (so `npm test` and `make test` fail with a clear hint instead of a module-not-found trace; change the guard test's first assertion to `expect(pkg.scripts.test).toContain('npm run test:worker'); expect(pkg.scripts['test:worker']).toContain('npm --prefix worker test');`). `.dockerignore`: add the line `worker/node_modules` next to `video/node_modules` (Docker's `.dockerignore` patterns are not recursive: the existing `node_modules` line covers only the root, which is why `video/` and `mcp/` are listed). Root `tsconfig.json`: `"exclude": ["functions", "worker"]`. In `.github/workflows/test.yml`, between `- run: npm ci` and `- run: npm test`, add `- run: npm --prefix worker ci`, and add `cache-dependency-path: |\n package-lock.json\n worker/package-lock.json` under `setup-node.with`.

Expected: the second command prints **two** `Tests` lines, the worker one with `4 passed`. Paste both lines in the PR description.

No gate. Spec §1 "fallo silencioso nuevo". Branch `feat/chat-foundations`.

```ts
it('POST /newsletter/send never claims a send without a provider', async () => {
  const res = await call('/newsletter/send', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer test-newsletter', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ html: '<p>x</p>' }),
  });

  expect(body.ok).not.toBe(true);
```

Seed one subscriber first inside the test with `await env.PUSH_SUBSCRIPTIONS.put(...)` using the key format `getAllSubscribers` lists (read `worker/handlers/newsletter.ts:100-125` for the prefix) so the code path reaches the placeholder. Run `npm --prefix worker test`. Expected: FAIL (`ok: true`).

- [ ] **4.2 Fix.** Replace the placeholder block (from `// Placeholder: log the intent` to the closing `});` of that `Response.json`) with:

```ts
  // No email provider is wired. Saying "queued" here was a silent success:
  // the weekly digest step printed OK while nobody received anything.
  return Response.json(
    { error: 'email_provider_not_configured', subscriberCount: subscribers.length, prepared: personalizedHtmls.length },
    { status: 501 },
  );
```

Run `npm --prefix worker test`. Expected: PASS. `.github/workflows/weekly-digest.yml:168-172` uses `curl -sf … || echo "⚠️ Newsletter send failed (non-fatal)"`, so the digest workflow now prints the warning instead of nothing — the honest outcome; no workflow change.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

const yml = readFileSync(resolve(__dirname, '../../.github/workflows/deploy-worker.yml'), 'utf8');

describe('deploy-worker.yml', () => {
  it('tests before deploying', () => {
    expect(yml.indexOf('npm --prefix worker test')).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    expect(yml.indexOf('npm --prefix worker test')).toBeLessThan(yml.indexOf('wrangler deploy'));

  });
```

Run `npx vitest run tests/ci/deploy-worker.test.ts`. Expected: FAIL (ENOENT).

- [ ] **5.2 Workflow.** `.github/workflows/deploy-worker.yml`:

```yaml
name: Deploy Worker

# The Worker was deployed by hand from a laptop: nobody could say which
# version ran in production (spec 2026-09-26 §10). This job is the only
# deploy path; a deploy whose smoke test fails is red.

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths: ['worker/**', 'wrangler.toml', 'src/lib/community/protocol.ts', 'src/lib/community/types.ts']

concurrency:
  group: deploy-worker
  cancel-in-progress: false

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: 22
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: worker/package-lock.json
      - run: npm --prefix worker ci
      - run: npm --prefix worker test
      - name: Deploy
        id: deploy
        env:
          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
        run: npx --prefix worker wrangler deploy --config wrangler.toml --var CHAT_VERSION:${{ github.sha }}
      - name: Smoke — push host answers as before
        run: |
          for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do
            BODY=$(curl -s --max-time 15 https://push.watchboard.dev/ || true)
            echo "$BODY" | grep -q 'Watchboard Push Notifications' && exit 0
            sleep 10
          done
          echo "::error::push.watchboard.dev did not answer after deploy"; exit 1
      # A red job is not enough: without this step production stays on the broken version.
      - name: Roll back when a smoke test failed
        if: failure() && steps.deploy.outcome == 'success'
        env:
          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
        run: |
          npx --prefix worker wrangler rollback --config wrangler.toml --message "auto-rollback: smoke failed in run ${{ github.run_id }}" -y
          curl -s --max-time 15 https://push.watchboard.dev/ | grep -q 'Watchboard Push Notifications' \
            || { echo "::error::push still down after rollback"; exit 1; }
      - name: Alert on failure
        if: failure()
        env:
          TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN }}
          TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID: ${{ secrets.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID }}
        run: |
          [ -n "$TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN" ] && [ -n "$TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID" ] || exit 0
          curl -s --max-time 15 "https://api.telegram.org/bot${TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}/sendMessage" \
            --data-urlencode "chat_id=${TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID}" \
            --data-urlencode "text=Worker deploy failed: ${{ github.server_url }}/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}"
```

Run `npx vitest run tests/ci/deploy-worker.test.ts`. Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **6.2 Write the ADR** (Spanish, the repo's ADR language; template `docs/adr/0000-template.md`). Required content, in this order:
  1. **Estado:** Propuesto (the owner's merge flips it; edit to "Aceptado" in the same PR on request). **Fecha**, **Autores**.
  2. **Contexto:** decision of 2026-09-25; reverses `2026-09-21-radio-layer-and-community-design.md:26-30`; supersedes the recommendations of 09-24; cites spec §1.
  3. **Decisión — contrato "sin tier":** the three layers (visual, structural, ingestion) from 09-24 §3 (`:169-246`) as binding rules, each with the test that enforces it (Task 9, Task 23 `separation.test.ts`, Task 25 e2e).
  4. **Decisión — estado compartido nuevo:** host `chat.watchboard.dev`, `ChatRoom`/`ChatMod` SQLite DOs, schemas of spec §3.3, `renderer: 'panel'`, `CHAT_ROOMS` allowlist, `CHAT_MAX_ROOMS = 5`.
  5. **Límites y techo:** the 6.1 table with dates; 0 USD ceiling (Free fails instead of billing); internal per-room budget and why (shared account quota with push, spec §7.2).
  6. **Retención y datos personales:** spec §3.8 and §6.3 tables verbatim; processors Cloudflare + GitHub.
  7. **Desviaciones del spec:** D1-D14 of this plan.
  8. **Consecuencias** and **Alternativas descartadas** (link 09-24 §4 and spec §9; do not repeat them).
  9. Link to `docs/runbooks/chat.md`.
  10. **Riesgos aceptados** (spec §7.2, §10; adversarial review): an anonymous script can spend the account-wide Workers/DO request quota that push shares — the Free WAF rule (1 rule, 10 s window, 10 s block, path only) slows a burst but does not stop a low-rate script; the only full isolation is a separate Cloudflare account. Observability is per Worker, so it is off for the whole `watchboard-push` Worker (push included). The Worker's storage-isolation mode in tests (Task 1.3 result).
  11. **Excepción de supresión (RGPD art. 17(3)(b) y (e)):** content under an open report or grave hold is kept in the room's `evidence` table until decided (spec §6.2), then deleted or moved to `decisions.facts` with the 183-day retention.
  12. **TCO (Reglamento (UE) 2021/784):** removal-order contact point (Q6), the one-hour path (spec §6.2 bis), and the conflict-room precondition.
  13. **Claves:** `CHAT_BAN_KEY` is never rotated (bans, reporter trust and session generations are keyed by it; the stored fingerprint makes a rotation fail closed, Task 14); `CHAT_SESSION_KEY` may be rotated (signs everyone out).
  14. **Jurisdicción:** the Q6 answer on EU jurisdiction, recorded **before** Task 7 creates any object (spec §6.3).

- [ ] **6.3 Write the runbook** `docs/runbooks/chat.md` (English, operational). Sections and exact commands:

```markdown
# Chat runbook

## Kill switches (fastest first; spec §8)
| # | Lever | Command | Expected within | Verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Room mode | https://chat.watchboard.dev/admin → room → readonly/closed | seconds | panel shows the reason |
| 2 | Global mode | /admin → Global → closed | ≤ 30 s | `curl -s https://chat.watchboard.dev/health \| jq .globalMode` = "closed" |
| 3 | Worker chat off | Phone: GitHub app → Actions → "Chat kill switch" → Run with `enabled=false` (Task 5.4). Laptop fallback: `cd worker && npx wrangler secret put CHAT_ENABLED --config ../wrangler.toml` then type `false` | 1-2 min | the workflow's "Verify by value" step is green; `curl -s https://chat.watchboard.dev/health \| jq .enabled` = false; `node worker/tools/chat-cli.mjs` prints `{"closed":4003}` without a `hello`; `/admin`, `/notice`, `/me/*` keep answering; push unchanged |
| 4 | Panel off in site | `gh variable set PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED --body false && gh workflow run deploy.yml` | build time | `curl -s https://watchboard.dev/cdmx/ \| grep -c 'data-chat-root'` = 0 |
| 5 | Nuclear | First disable deploy-worker (`gh workflow disable deploy-worker.yml`), then Cloudflare dashboard → Workers → watchboard-push → Domains → remove chat.watchboard.dev; within a day, a PR removes the `chat.watchboard.dev` route from wrangler.toml before deploy-worker is re-enabled | minutes | DNS for chat. no longer answers; push unchanged. Without the first step the next push to `worker/**` re-attaches the domain (`custom_domain = true`) |
After any lever, set the intended state for the canary: `gh variable set CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED --body false` (or `true` when re-enabling). A mismatch fails the daily credential canary (Task 19.4), which is the reminder that the chat is still off.
Measured times from the rehearsal (Task 26): fill in the table's last column after the drill.

## Remove one tracker's room
0. Immediate, from a phone: /admin → that room → `closed` (lever 1). Closed rooms serve no history.
1. Remove the slug from `CHAT_ROOMS` in wrangler.toml, merge → deploy-worker.yml. The room stays in ChatMod's registry, so erasure, bans and retention still reach it until its data expires.
2. Set `community.chat: false` in trackers/{slug}/tracker.json, merge, then `gh workflow run deploy.yml` (a merge under trackers/** does not deploy: deploy.yml paths-ignore).

## Alerts (TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID only)
One line per alert code from worker/chat/alerts.ts: meaning, first action.

## Secrets
- `CHAT_BAN_KEY`: never rotate. Bans, reporter trust and session generations are keyed by HMAC(CHAT_BAN_KEY, uid); ChatMod stores its fingerprint and refuses every identity if it changes (writes stop, reads continue). If it leaked, re-plan: rotation needs a re-keying migration.
- `CHAT_SESSION_KEY`: rotating it signs every reader out; safe at any time.
- `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`: exists twice — as a GitHub secret and as a Worker secret. Rotate both in the same sitting; `/health.alertsOk` (probed hourly by ChatMod) turns false if the Worker copy is stale.

## Erasure / access requests (GDPR)
Reader self-service: panel → "Delete my account" / "Download my messages".
By email: /admin → User → paste GitHub login → Erase; the confirmation shows the re-read count, per room of ChatMod's registry (rooms removed from CHAT_ROOMS included).
Messages under an open report are hidden at once but kept as evidence until the report is decided (GDPR art. 17(3)(b)/(e)); tell the requester so.

## Illegal content (DSA art. 16-18) and terrorist content (TCO, Regulation (EU) 2021/784)
Remove + 12-month ban from /admin; tick "preserve" only if Q6 requires it; authority contact: filled in by Task 27 from Q6.
A TCO removal order must be executed within one hour of receipt: open /admin on the phone, remove the item (or set the room `closed`, which hides history), then record the order reference in the decision.
Notice status for notifiers: https://chat.watchboard.dev/notice/status?ref= followed by the reference number the notice page showed.
```

```ts
// worker/chat/limits.ts — every value from Global Constraints, exported as const
export const CHAT_HOST = 'chat.watchboard.dev';
export const CHAT_MAX_ROOMS = 5;
export const SLUG_RE = /^(_canary|[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63})$/;
export const MAX_BODY_CHARS = 500;
export const MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 4096;
export const HISTORY_LIMIT = 100;
export const LIMITS = {
  burstMs: 8_000, slowBurstMs: 60_000, perHour: 20, perDay: 100, reportsPerHour: 10,
  roomPerMinute: 40, autoSlowMs: 15 * 60_000, roomMsgsPerDay: 1_000,
  maxSockets: 300, maxAnonSockets: 200, maxConsecutiveBadFrames: 3, budgetAlertRatio: 0.7,
  syncEveryMs: 60_000,                       // room pulls ChatMod.syncState() at most this often (D4)
  anonGraveHoldsPerRoomHour: 5,              // anonymous csam/terrorism/threat notices that may auto-hold per room per hour (D13)
  graveHeldForReadonly: 3, graveUnreviewedMs: 60 * 60_000,   // room-level readonly only on volume (D13)
  graveAlertEveryMs: 15 * 60_000,            // at most one grave-report alert per room per window, with a count
} as const;
// CLOSE and PROTOCOL_VERSION are declared in src/lib/community/protocol.ts (Task 8) because the client maps them;
// until Task 8 exists, declare CLOSE here and move it (re-export) in 8.2.
export const CLOSE = { banned: 1008, closed: 4002, disabled: 4003, full: 4004, badFrames: 4008, revoked: 4009 } as const;
export const RETENTION = { visibleMs: 30 * 86_400_000, heldMs: 72 * 3_600_000, modRecordsMs: 183 * 86_400_000, maxBanMs: 365 * 86_400_000 } as const;
export const RESERVED_HANDLES = ['watchboard', 'admin', 'mod', 'moderator', 'reuters', 'ap', 'bbc', 'afp', 'cnn', 'aljazeera'] as const;
export const roomName = (slug: string) => `tracker:${slug}`;

// worker/chat/env.ts
export interface ChatEnv {
  CHAT_ROOM: DurableObjectNamespace;
  CHAT_MOD: DurableObjectNamespace;
  CHAT_ENABLED?: string; CHAT_ROOMS?: string; CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS?: string; CHAT_VERSION?: string;
  CHAT_DEV_HOST?: string; CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS?: string; CHAT_JURISDICTION?: string;
  CHAT_SESSION_KEY?: string; CHAT_BAN_KEY?: string; CHAT_ADMIN_IDS?: string;
  GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID?: string; GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET?: string;
  TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN?: string; TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID?: string;
}
export function chatEnabled(env: ChatEnv): boolean;          // env.CHAT_ENABLED === 'true'
export function parseRooms(raw: string | undefined): Set<string>;   // trims, drops invalid slugs, throws if > CHAT_MAX_ROOMS + 1 (_canary)
export function parseOrigins(raw: string | undefined): Set<string>;
export function isChatHost(url: URL, env: ChatEnv): boolean; // hostname === CHAT_HOST || (env.CHAT_DEV_HOST && url.host === env.CHAT_DEV_HOST)

// worker/chat/stubs.ts — the ONLY module that calls idFromName (review #24)
export function roomNs(env: ChatEnv): DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>;   // env.CHAT_ROOM, or .jurisdiction('eu') when CHAT_JURISDICTION === 'eu'
export function modNs(env: ChatEnv): DurableObjectNamespace<ChatMod>;
export function roomStub(env: ChatEnv, slug: string): DurableObjectStub<ChatRoom>;   // roomNs(env).get(roomNs(env).idFromName(roomName(slug)))
export function modStub(env: ChatEnv): DurableObjectStub<ChatMod>;                   // idFromName('global')

// worker/chat/router.ts
export async function handleChat(request: Request, env: ChatEnv, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response>;
/** Accept a WebSocket only to tell the browser why it is refused: frames, then an application close code (review #36). */
export function refuseSocket(frames: ServerFrame[], code: number, reason: string): Response;

// worker/chat/http.ts
export function chatJson(data: unknown, status?: number, extra?: HeadersInit): Response; // no CORS; Cache-Control: no-store
```

```ts
import { exports } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { handleChat } from '../chat/router';
import type { ChatEnv } from '../chat/env';

const ctx = { waitUntil() {}, passThroughOnException() {} } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
function fakeEnv(over: Partial<ChatEnv> = {}) {
  const ns = () => ({ idFromName: vi.fn(() => ({})), get: vi.fn(() => ({ fetch: vi.fn(async () => new Response('do')) })) });
  return { CHAT_ROOM: ns(), CHAT_MOD: ns(), CHAT_ENABLED: 'true', CHAT_ROOMS: '_canary,cdmx',
    CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: 'https://watchboard.dev', CHAT_VERSION: 'abc123', ...over } as unknown as ChatEnv & {
    CHAT_ROOM: ReturnType<typeof ns>; };
}
const ws = (path: string, origin = 'https://watchboard.dev') =>
  new Request('https://chat.watchboard.dev' + path, { headers: { Upgrade: 'websocket', Origin: origin } });

describe('chat router', () => {
  it('disabled: /health says so without touching a DO', async () => {
    const env = fakeEnv({ CHAT_ENABLED: undefined });
    const res = await handleChat(new Request('https://chat.watchboard.dev/health'), env, ctx);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ enabled: false, version: 'abc123' });
    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it.each(['false', 'TRUE', '1', ''])('CHAT_ENABLED=%j → the browser learns why (close 4003), no DO touched', async (v) => {
    const env = fakeEnv({ CHAT_ENABLED: v });
    const res = await handleChat(ws('/room/cdmx'), env, ctx);
    expect(res.status).toBe(101);
    const sock = res.webSocket!;
    const got: string[] = []; let code = 0;
    sock.addEventListener('message', (e) => { got.push(e.data as string); });
    sock.addEventListener('close', (e) => { code = e.code; });
    sock.accept();
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
    expect(got.map((s) => JSON.parse(s))).toEqual([{ t: 'err', code: 'chat_disabled' }]);
    expect(code).toBe(4003);
    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('CHAT_ENABLED off: a plain GET on a room is 503 chat_disabled', async () => {
    const res = await handleChat(new Request('https://chat.watchboard.dev/room/cdmx'), fakeEnv({ CHAT_ENABLED: 'false' }), ctx);
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: 'chat_disabled' });
  });
  it('slug outside CHAT_ROOMS → 404 without idFromName', async () => {
    const env = fakeEnv();
    for (const p of ['/room/iran-conflict', '/room/..%2Fx', '/room/CDMX', '/room/']) {
      expect((await handleChat(ws(p), env, ctx)).status).toBe(404);
    }
    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('foreign or missing Origin → 403 without idFromName', async () => {
    const env = fakeEnv();
    expect((await handleChat(ws('/room/cdmx', 'https://evil.example'), env, ctx)).status).toBe(403);
    const noOrigin = new Request('https://chat.watchboard.dev/room/cdmx', { headers: { Upgrade: 'websocket' } });
    expect((await handleChat(noOrigin, env, ctx)).status).toBe(403);
    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it('non-upgrade GET on a room → 426', async () => {
    expect((await handleChat(new Request('https://chat.watchboard.dev/room/cdmx'), fakeEnv(), ctx)).status).toBe(426);
  });
  it('valid upgrade reaches the room named tracker:{slug}', async () => {
    const env = fakeEnv();
    await handleChat(ws('/room/cdmx'), env, ctx);
    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName).toHaveBeenCalledWith('tracker:cdmx');
  });
  it('the real worker sends the chat host to the chat router, not the push switch', async () => {
    const res = await (exports as { default: Fetcher }).default.fetch('https://chat.watchboard.dev/');
    expect(res.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin')).toBeNull();
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { runInDurableObject } from 'cloudflare:test';
import { it, expect } from 'vitest';

const tables = (stub: DurableObjectStub) =>
  runInDurableObject(stub, (_i, state) =>
    state.storage.sql.exec("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name").toArray().map((r) => r.name));

it('ChatRoom creates its tables on first use', async () => {
  const ns = (env as unknown as { CHAT_ROOM: DurableObjectNamespace }).CHAT_ROOM;
  expect(await tables(roomStub(env as never, '_canary'))).toEqual(['bans', 'evidence', 'holds', 'messages', 'room', 'usage']);
});
it('ChatMod creates its tables on first use', async () => {
  expect(await tables(modStub(env as never))).toEqual(['bans', 'decisions', 'reports', 'room_stats', 'rooms', 'settings', 'users']);
});
it('the hot paths are indexed (review #2): no full scan for duplicates, own history or retention', async () => {
  const plan = (q: string) => runInDurableObject(roomStub(env as never, '_canary'), (_i, s) =>
    s.storage.sql.exec(`EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN ${q}`).toArray().map((r) => String(r.detail)).join(' | '));
  expect(await plan("SELECT uid, body FROM messages WHERE body_hash = 'x' AND ts > 0")).toMatch(/USING INDEX messages_hash/);
  expect(await plan("SELECT body FROM messages WHERE uid = 'gh:1' AND ts > 0")).toMatch(/USING INDEX messages_uid/);
  expect(await plan("SELECT id FROM messages WHERE state = 'visible' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100")).not.toMatch(/TEMP B-TREE/);
});
```

```ts
import { roomStub, modStub } from '../chat/stubs';

const ns = () => { const inner = { idFromName: vi.fn(() => ({})), get: vi.fn(() => ({})) };

describe('stubs', () => {

    expect(env.CHAT_ROOM.jurisdiction).toHaveBeenCalledWith('eu');
    expect(env.CHAT_MOD.jurisdiction).toHaveBeenCalledWith('eu');
    expect(env.CHAT_MOD.inner.idFromName).toHaveBeenCalledWith('global');
  });
  it('an unknown jurisdiction value is refused, not ignored', () => {
    expect(() => modStub({ CHAT_ROOM: ns(), CHAT_MOD: ns(), CHAT_JURISDICTION: 'europe' } as never)).toThrow(/CHAT_JURISDICTION/);
  });
});
```

`worker/chat/stubs.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';
import { roomName } from './limits';
import type { ChatRoom } from './room';
import type { ChatMod } from './mod';

function scoped<T extends Rpc.DurableObjectBranded | undefined>(ns: DurableObjectNamespace<T>, env: ChatEnv): DurableObjectNamespace<T> {
  if (env.CHAT_JURISDICTION === undefined || env.CHAT_JURISDICTION === '') return ns;
  if (env.CHAT_JURISDICTION !== 'eu') throw new Error(`CHAT_JURISDICTION must be "eu" or unset, got ${env.CHAT_JURISDICTION}`);
  return ns.jurisdiction('eu');
}
export const roomNs = (env: ChatEnv) => scoped(env.CHAT_ROOM as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>, env);
export const modNs = (env: ChatEnv) => scoped(env.CHAT_MOD as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatMod>, env);
export function roomStub(env: ChatEnv, slug: string) { const n = roomNs(env); return n.get(n.idFromName(roomName(slug))); }
export function modStub(env: ChatEnv) { const n = modNs(env); return n.get(n.idFromName('global')); }
```

- [ ] **7.2 Schema.** `worker/chat/schema.ts` (spec §3.3, revised after the adversarial review: every hot query has an index, text-keyed tables are `WITHOUT ROWID` so a write is one row rather than a row plus an autoindex entry — "every row update of an index counts as an additional row", Cloudflare SQLite API — and there is no `counters` table; see D11):

```ts
export const ROOM_SCHEMA = [
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
     id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ts INTEGER NOT NULL, uid TEXT NOT NULL, handle TEXT NOT NULL,
     body TEXT NOT NULL, body_hash TEXT NOT NULL, lang TEXT, moderator INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
     state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('visible','held','removed','deleted')),
     hold_code TEXT, reason TEXT, reported INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, nonce TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE)`,
  // No index on state: "last 100 visible" walks the rowid backwards and stops after 100 hits.
  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS messages_uid ON messages(uid, ts)`,          // own recent bodies, own held/removed, erasure
  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS messages_hash ON messages(body_hash, ts)`,   // brigade duplicates (review #2)
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS holds (msg_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ts INTEGER NOT NULL, grave INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)`,  // small: held messages only
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence (msg_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ts INTEGER NOT NULL, uid TEXT NOT NULL, handle TEXT NOT NULL,
     body TEXT NOT NULL, saved_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,                       // text kept under an open report (spec §6.2, review #16)
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS usage (uid TEXT PRIMARY KEY, last INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, hour TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
     hour_n INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, day TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', day_n INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
     rep_hour TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', rep_n INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0) WITHOUT ROWID`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS room (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID`,   // mode, global, slug, terms, stats:<day>, sync
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bans (uid_hmac TEXT PRIMARY KEY, until INTEGER NOT NULL, ground TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID`,
] as const;

export const MOD_SCHEMA = [
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bans (uid_hmac TEXT PRIMARY KEY, until INTEGER NOT NULL, ground TEXT NOT NULL, decision_id INTEGER) WITHOUT ROWID`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reports (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room TEXT NOT NULL, msg_id INTEGER NOT NULL, ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
     category TEXT NOT NULL, reporter_uid_hmac TEXT, notice_json TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open')`,
  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS reports_msg ON reports(room, msg_id)`,
  `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS reports_open ON reports(status, ts)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS decisions (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, ts INTEGER NOT NULL, room TEXT NOT NULL, msg_id INTEGER,
     action TEXT NOT NULL, ground TEXT NOT NULL, automated INTEGER NOT NULL, facts TEXT, facts_until INTEGER)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (uid_hmac TEXT PRIMARY KEY, gen INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, dismissed_reports INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0) WITHOUT ROWID`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rooms (slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, first_seen INTEGER NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID`,   // registry of every room ever created (review #7)
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS room_stats (slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, at INTEGER NOT NULL, json TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID`,  // pushed by each room's alarm; feeds public /health (review #9)
] as const;

export function applySchema(sql: SqlStorage, stmts: readonly string[]): void {
  for (const s of stmts) sql.exec(s);
}
```

- [ ] **7.3 Env + HTTP helpers.** `worker/chat/env.ts` implements the interface above:

```ts
import { CHAT_HOST, CHAT_MAX_ROOMS, SLUG_RE } from './limits';
export function chatEnabled(env: ChatEnv): boolean { return env.CHAT_ENABLED === 'true'; }
export function parseRooms(raw: string | undefined): Set<string> {
  const rooms = new Set((raw ?? '').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => SLUG_RE.test(s)));
  const real = [...rooms].filter((s) => s !== '_canary');
  if (real.length > CHAT_MAX_ROOMS) throw new Error(`CHAT_ROOMS has ${real.length} rooms; max ${CHAT_MAX_ROOMS}`);
  return rooms;
}
export function parseOrigins(raw: string | undefined): Set<string> {
  return new Set((raw ?? '').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => /^https?:\/\/[^/]+$/.test(s)));
}
export function isChatHost(url: URL, env: ChatEnv): boolean {
  return url.hostname === CHAT_HOST || (!!env.CHAT_DEV_HOST && url.host === env.CHAT_DEV_HOST);
}
```

`worker/chat/http.ts`:

```ts
export function chatJson(data: unknown, status = 200, extra: HeadersInit = {}): Response {
  const h = new Headers({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
  for (const [k, v] of new Headers(extra)) h.set(k, v);          // callers may override the defaults (e.g. /health caching)
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { status, headers: h });
}
```

- [ ] **7.4 Router.** `worker/chat/router.ts`:

```ts
import { chatEnabled, parseOrigins, parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { chatJson } from './http';
import { CLOSE, SLUG_RE } from './limits';
import { roomStub } from './stubs';
import { encode, type ServerFrame } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';

export function refuseSocket(frames: ServerFrame[], code: number, reason: string): Response {
  const pair = new WebSocketPair();
  const [client, server] = [pair[0], pair[1]];
  server.accept();
  for (const f of frames) server.send(encode(f));
  server.close(code, reason);
  return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
}

export async function handleChat(request: Request, env: ChatEnv, _ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const path = url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
  if (path === '/health' && !chatEnabled(env)) {
    return chatJson({ enabled: false, version: env.CHAT_VERSION ?? 'unknown' });
  }
  // Routes that must keep working while CHAT_ENABLED is off (/admin, /notice, /me, callback, logout) are added
  // ABOVE the room match by Tasks 16-18; CHAT_ENABLED gates only room upgrades and non-admin sign-in (review #32).

  const m = /^\/room\/([^/]*)$/.exec(path);
  if (m) {
    let slug: string;
    try { slug = decodeURIComponent(m[1]); } catch { return chatJson({ error: 'not_found' }, 404); }
    if (!SLUG_RE.test(slug) || !parseRooms(env.CHAT_ROOMS).has(slug)) return chatJson({ error: 'not_found' }, 404);
    const upgrade = request.headers.get('Upgrade')?.toLowerCase() === 'websocket';
    if (!chatEnabled(env) && !upgrade) return chatJson({ error: 'chat_disabled' }, 503);
    if (!upgrade) return chatJson({ error: 'upgrade_required' }, 426);
    const origin = request.headers.get('Origin');
    if (!origin || !parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS).has(origin)) return chatJson({ error: 'forbidden_origin' }, 403);
    if (!chatEnabled(env)) return refuseSocket([{ t: 'err', code: 'chat_disabled' }], CLOSE.disabled, 'chat disabled');
    const fwd = new Request(request, { headers: new Headers(request.headers) });
    fwd.headers.set('X-Chat-Room', slug);
    return roomStub(env, slug).fetch(fwd);
  }
  return chatJson({ error: 'not_found' }, 404);
}
```

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// src/lib/community/types.ts — NO tier/source/pole/contested/media keys, ever (Task 9)
export type ChatMode = 'open' | 'slow' | 'readonly' | 'closed';
export type ChatModeReason = 'owner' | 'global' | 'daily_budget' | 'auto_slow' | 'filters_unavailable' | 'attended_hours' | 'unreviewed_report';
export type HoldCode = 'slur' | 'pii' | 'link' | 'duplicate' | 'reports' | 'grave_report' | 'language';
export type ReportCategory = 'illegal' | 'harassment' | 'personal_data' | 'spam' | 'misinformation' | 'other';
export type ChatErrorCode = 'rate_limited' | 'too_long' | 'empty' | 'banned' | 'auth_required' | 'account_too_new'
  | 'readonly' | 'closed' | 'bad_frame' | 'not_author' | 'report_limited' | 'duplicate' | 'shouting' | 'unknown'
  | 'daily_budget' | 'room_full' | 'chat_disabled';
export interface ChatMessage {
  id: number; ts: number; handle: string; body: string; lang?: string;
  state: 'visible' | 'held' | 'removed';
  mine?: boolean; holdCode?: HoldCode; reason?: string; moderator?: boolean;
}

// src/lib/community/protocol.ts
export const MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 4096;
export const MAX_BODY_CHARS = 500;
export const MAX_RAW_BODY_CHARS = 2000;           // before normalisation; normalise() then trims to MAX_BODY_CHARS
export const PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1;
export const CLOSE = { banned: 1008, closed: 4002, disabled: 4003, full: 4004, badFrames: 4008, revoked: 4009 } as const;
export const REPORT_CATEGORIES: readonly ReportCategory[];
export type ClientFrame =
  | { t: 'send'; body: string; nonce: string; lang?: string }
  | { t: 'report'; id: number; category: ReportCategory }
  | { t: 'delete'; id: number };
export interface Sanction { until: number; ground: string }       // statement of reasons for a ban (DSA art. 17, review #20)
export type ServerFrame =
  | { t: 'hello'; v: number; mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number; history: ChatMessage[];
      me?: { handle: string; canWrite: boolean; cooldownMs: number; banned?: Sanction } }
  | { t: 'ack'; nonce: string; id: number; state: 'visible' | 'held'; holdCode?: HoldCode }
  | { t: 'msg'; m: ChatMessage }
  | { t: 'remove'; id: number; reason?: string }
  | { t: 'mode'; mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number }
  | { t: 'err'; code: ChatErrorCode; nonce?: string; cooldownMs?: number; until?: number; ground?: string };
// Parsers IGNORE unknown keys and return objects built only from known fields (review #39): an old client keeps
// working when the Worker adds a field, and no unexpected key (tier, source, …) can ride through into state.
export function parseClientFrame(raw: string | ArrayBuffer): ClientFrame | null;
export function parseServerFrame(raw: string): ServerFrame | null;
export function encode(frame: ServerFrame | ClientFrame): string;
```

- [ ] **8.2 Implement.** `src/lib/community/protocol.ts` (types as above, then):

```ts
import type { ChatMessage, ChatMode, ChatModeReason, ChatErrorCode, HoldCode, ReportCategory } from './types';

export const MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 4096;
export const MAX_BODY_CHARS = 500;
export const MAX_RAW_BODY_CHARS = 2000;
export const REPORT_CATEGORIES = ['illegal', 'harassment', 'personal_data', 'spam', 'misinformation', 'other'] as const satisfies readonly ReportCategory[];
const MODES = ['open', 'slow', 'readonly', 'closed'] as const;
const REASONS = ['owner', 'global', 'daily_budget', 'auto_slow', 'filters_unavailable', 'attended_hours', 'unreviewed_report'] as const;
const HOLDS = ['slur', 'pii', 'link', 'duplicate', 'reports', 'grave_report', 'language'] as const;
const ERRORS = ['rate_limited', 'too_long', 'empty', 'banned', 'auth_required', 'account_too_new', 'readonly', 'closed', 'bad_frame', 'not_author', 'report_limited', 'duplicate', 'shouting', 'unknown', 'daily_budget', 'room_full', 'chat_disabled'] as const;
export const PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1;
export const CLOSE = { banned: 1008, closed: 4002, disabled: 4003, full: 4004, badFrames: 4008, revoked: 4009 } as const;
const NONCE_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,64}$/;
const LANG_RE = /^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$/;

type Obj = Record<string, unknown>;
const isObj = (v: unknown): v is Obj => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
/** Copy only the listed keys that are present. Unknown keys are dropped, never rejected (review #39). */
const pick = (o: Obj, keys: readonly string[]): Obj => Object.fromEntries(keys.filter((k) => k in o).map((k) => [k, o[k]]));
const isId = (v: unknown): v is number => Number.isInteger(v) && (v as number) > 0;
const isTs = (v: unknown): v is number => Number.isInteger(v) && (v as number) >= 0;
const oneOf = <T extends string>(v: unknown, list: readonly T[]): v is T => typeof v === 'string' && (list as readonly string[]).includes(v);
const optional = (o: Obj, k: string, ok: (v: unknown) => boolean) => !(k in o) || ok(o[k]);

function parseJson(raw: string): Obj | null {
  if (new TextEncoder().encode(raw).length > MAX_FRAME_BYTES) return null;
  try { const v: unknown = JSON.parse(raw); return isObj(v) ? v : null; } catch { return null; }
}

export function parseClientFrame(raw: string | ArrayBuffer): ClientFrame | null {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const o = parseJson(raw);
  if (!o) return null;
  switch (o.t) {
    case 'send':
      if (typeof o.body !== 'string' || o.body.length > MAX_RAW_BODY_CHARS) return null;
      if (typeof o.nonce !== 'string' || !NONCE_RE.test(o.nonce)) return null;
      if (!optional(o, 'lang', (v) => typeof v === 'string' && LANG_RE.test(v))) return null;
      return o.lang === undefined ? { t: 'send', body: o.body, nonce: o.nonce } : { t: 'send', body: o.body, nonce: o.nonce, lang: o.lang as string };
    case 'report':
      if (!isId(o.id) || !oneOf(o.category, REPORT_CATEGORIES)) return null;
      return { t: 'report', id: o.id, category: o.category };
    case 'delete':
      if (!isId(o.id)) return null;
      return { t: 'delete', id: o.id };
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

const MESSAGE_KEYS = ['id', 'ts', 'handle', 'body', 'lang', 'state', 'mine', 'holdCode', 'reason', 'moderator'] as const;
function pickMessage(v: unknown): ChatMessage | null {
  if (!isObj(v)) return null;
  const ok = isId(v.id) && isTs(v.ts) && typeof v.handle === 'string' && typeof v.body === 'string'
    && oneOf(v.state, ['visible', 'held', 'removed'] as const)
    && optional(v, 'lang', (x) => typeof x === 'string' && LANG_RE.test(x))
    && optional(v, 'mine', (x) => typeof x === 'boolean')
    && optional(v, 'moderator', (x) => typeof x === 'boolean')
    && optional(v, 'holdCode', (x) => oneOf(x, HOLDS))
    && optional(v, 'reason', (x) => typeof x === 'string');
  return ok ? (pick(v, MESSAGE_KEYS) as unknown as ChatMessage) : null;
}
const isSanction = (x: unknown) => isObj(x) && isTs(x.until) && typeof x.ground === 'string';

export function parseServerFrame(raw: string): ServerFrame | null {
  let o: Obj;
  try { const v: unknown = JSON.parse(raw); if (!isObj(v)) return null; o = v; } catch { return null; }
  const modeOk = () => oneOf(o.mode, MODES) && optional(o, 'reason', (x) => oneOf(x, REASONS)) && optional(o, 'until', isTs);
  const out = (keys: readonly string[]) => pick(o, keys) as unknown as ServerFrame;
  switch (o.t) {
    case 'hello': {
      if (!isTs(o.v) || !modeOk() || !Array.isArray(o.history)) return null;
      const history = o.history.map(pickMessage);
      if (history.some((m) => m === null)) return null;
      let me: Obj | undefined;
      if ('me' in o) {
        const m = o.me;
        if (!isObj(m) || typeof m.handle !== 'string' || typeof m.canWrite !== 'boolean' || !isTs(m.cooldownMs)
          || !optional(m, 'banned', isSanction)) return null;
        me = pick(m, ['handle', 'canWrite', 'cooldownMs']);
        if (m.banned) me.banned = pick(m.banned as Obj, ['until', 'ground']);
      }
      return { ...(out(['t', 'v', 'mode', 'reason', 'until']) as object), history, ...(me ? { me } : {}) } as ServerFrame;
    }
    case 'ack':
      return typeof o.nonce === 'string' && isId(o.id) && oneOf(o.state, ['visible', 'held'] as const)
        && optional(o, 'holdCode', (x) => oneOf(x, HOLDS)) ? out(['t', 'nonce', 'id', 'state', 'holdCode']) : null;
    case 'msg': {
      const m = pickMessage(o.m);
      return m ? { t: 'msg', m } : null;
    }
    case 'remove':
      return isId(o.id) && optional(o, 'reason', (x) => typeof x === 'string') ? out(['t', 'id', 'reason']) : null;
    case 'mode':
      return modeOk() ? out(['t', 'mode', 'reason', 'until']) : null;
    case 'err':
      return oneOf(o.code, ERRORS) && optional(o, 'nonce', (x) => typeof x === 'string') && optional(o, 'cooldownMs', isTs)
        && optional(o, 'until', isTs) && optional(o, 'ground', (x) => typeof x === 'string')
        ? out(['t', 'code', 'nonce', 'cooldownMs', 'until', 'ground']) : null;
    default:
      return null;          // an unknown frame TYPE is dropped by the client; unknown KEYS are not a reason to drop
  }
}

export const encode = (frame: ServerFrame | ClientFrame): string => JSON.stringify(frame);
```

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve, join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

const ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '../../..');
function walk(dir: string, out: string[] = []): string[] {
  if (!existsSync(dir)) return out;
  for (const e of readdirSync(dir)) {
    if (e === 'node_modules' || e === 'dist') continue;
    const p = join(dir, e);
    statSync(p).isDirectory() ? walk(p, out) : out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}
const code = (f: string) => /\.(ts|tsx|astro|mjs|js)$/.test(f);
const communityFiles = [...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'src/lib/community')), ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'src/components/islands/community'))]
  .filter((f) => !f.endsWith('.test.ts'));

const FORBIDDEN_MESSAGE_KEYS = ['tier', 'source', 'sourceTier', 'pole', 'contested', 'media', 'confidence', 'verified'];
const FORBIDDEN_STYLE = [/--tier-/, /--accent-red/, /--accent-amber/, /--accent-blue/, /#e74c3c/i, /#3498db/i, /#f39c12/i,
  /source-chip/, /freshness-indicator/, /SourceStatusChip/, /FreshnessBadge/, /JetBrains Mono/, /monospace/,
  /\.t[1-4]\b/, /class(Name)?=["'{][^"'}]*\bt[1-4]\b/];

describe('comments carry no tier (09-24 §3.2)', () => {
  it('ChatMessage declares none of the tier-like keys', () => {
    const src = readFileSync(resolve(ROOT, 'src/lib/community/types.ts'), 'utf8');
    const body = /export interface ChatMessage \{([\s\S]*?)\n\}/.exec(src)?.[1] ?? '';
    expect(body.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const k of FORBIDDEN_MESSAGE_KEYS) expect(body, `ChatMessage has ${k}`).not.toMatch(new RegExp(`\\b${k}\\??:`));
  });
  it('schemas.ts neither defines nor imports chat types', () => {
    const s = readFileSync(resolve(ROOT, 'src/lib/schemas.ts'), 'utf8');
    expect(s).not.toMatch(/community|ChatMessage/);
  });
});

describe('nothing that publishes data imports community code (09-24 §3.3)', () => {
  const guarded = [
    ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'scripts')), ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'mcp')), ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'video/src')),
    ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'src/pages/api')),
    ...walk(resolve(ROOT, 'src/pages')).filter((f) => /rss|feeds/.test(relative(ROOT, f))),
    resolve(ROOT, 'src/lib/schemas.ts'), resolve(ROOT, 'src/lib/data.ts'),
  ].filter(code);
  it('scans a non-trivial number of files', () => expect(guarded.length).toBeGreaterThan(50));
  for (const f of guarded) {
    it(`${relative(ROOT, f)} does not import community code`, () => {
      expect(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')).not.toMatch(/from ['"][^'"]*(lib\/community|islands\/community|worker\/chat)[^'"]*['"]/);
    });
  }
});

describe('no tracking in community code', () => {
  for (const f of communityFiles.filter(code)) {
    it(`${relative(ROOT, f)} has no analytics or browser storage`, () => {
      expect(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')).not.toMatch(/posthog|localStorage|sessionStorage|document\.cookie|navigator\.sendBeacon/);
    });
  }
});

describe('panel styling never borrows data styling (spec §5.2)', () => {
  for (const f of communityFiles.filter((x) => /\.(css|tsx)$/.test(x))) {
    it(`${relative(ROOT, f)} uses no tier colour, chip or mono font`, () => {
      const s = readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
      for (const re of FORBIDDEN_STYLE) expect(s, `${re} in ${f}`).not.toMatch(re);
    });
  }
});
```

**Files:** Create `worker/chat/normalize.ts`, `worker/chat/normalize.test.ts`, `worker/chat/filters.ts`, `worker/chat/filters.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// normalize.ts
export function normalize(raw: string): string;   // NFKC, strip controls/bidi/zero-width, ≤2 combining marks per base, collapse spaces, trim, ≤ MAX_BODY_CHARS code points
export function fold(text: string): string;       // lowercase, strip diacritics, leet map (0→o 1→i 3→e 4→a 5→s 7→t @→a $→s), non-letters → ' '
export function squash(text: string): string;     // fold() with all spaces removed ("b.a.d w o r d" → "badword")

// filters.ts
export interface TermList { version: number; terms: Record<'en' | 'es' | 'fr' | 'pt', string[]> }
export class FiltersUnavailable extends Error {}
export type Verdict =
  | { kind: 'accept' }
  | { kind: 'hold'; code: HoldCode }
  | { kind: 'reject'; code: 'empty' | 'duplicate' | 'shouting' };
export interface FilterInput {
  body: string;                       // already normalize()d
  ownRecent: string[];                // this uid's normalized bodies, last 10 min
  othersSameBody: number;             // distinct OTHER uids who sent this exact body in the last 5 min
  terms: TermList | null;
}
export function termsUsable(t: TermList | null): t is TermList;   // version ≥ 1 and ≥ 1 term overall
export function evaluate(input: FilterInput): Verdict;            // throws FiltersUnavailable when !termsUsable
export function containsPii(body: string): boolean;
export function containsLink(body: string): boolean;              // URLs, bare domains AND obfuscated forms (hxxp, t[.]me, "dot com")
export function mostlyOutsideSiteScripts(body: string): boolean;  // > 50 % of ≥ 10 letters are not Latin script (review #23)
export function luhn(digits: string): boolean;
export function bodyHash(body: string): string;                   // synchronous FNV-1a (52-bit) hex; a collision only means an extra body comparison
```

Two changes from the spec, both from the adversarial review: **every** message with a link (clickable or obfuscated) is held for review during the pilot (§4.1.6 held only accounts with < 3 accepted messages — three harmless lines in 24 s bypassed it; review #21); and a message whose letters are mostly outside the Latin script is held `language` (the rules restrict the room to the site's four languages; the term lists and the one moderator cover only those; review #23).
