- [ ] **10.2 Implement `normalize.ts`.**

```ts
import { MAX_BODY_CHARS } from './limits';

const INVISIBLE = /[\u0000-\u0008\u000B-\u001F\u007F-\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁠-⁩﻿]/g;
export function normalize(raw: string): string {
  let s = raw.normalize('NFKC').replace(INVISIBLE, '');
  s = s.replace(/(\P{M})(\p{M}{3,})/gu, (_m, base: string, marks: string) => base + [...marks].slice(0, 2).join(''));
  s = s.replace(/\s+/gu, ' ').trim();
  return [...s].slice(0, MAX_BODY_CHARS).join('');
}
const LEET: Record<string, string> = { '0': 'o', '1': 'i', '3': 'e', '4': 'a', '5': 's', '7': 't', '@': 'a', '$': 's' };
export function fold(text: string): string {
  return text.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/\p{M}/gu, '')
    .replace(/[013457@$]/g, (c) => LEET[c])
    .replace(/[^\p{L}]+/gu, ' ');
}
export const squash = (text: string): string => fold(text).replace(/ /g, '');
```

- [ ] **10.3 Implement `filters.ts`.**

```ts
import type { HoldCode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { fold, squash } from './normalize';

export class FiltersUnavailable extends Error {}
export function termsUsable(t: TermList | null): t is TermList {
  return !!t && Number.isInteger(t.version) && t.version >= 1
    && Object.values(t.terms ?? {}).reduce((n, list) => n + (Array.isArray(list) ? list.length : 0), 0) > 0;
}
export function luhn(digits: string): boolean {
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < digits.length; i++) {
    let d = Number(digits[digits.length - 1 - i]);
    if (i % 2 === 1) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }
    sum += d;
  }
  return digits.length >= 13 && sum % 10 === 0;
}
const EMAIL = /[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[a-z]{2,}/i;
const PHONE = /(?:\+?\d[\s().-]{0,2}){9,15}/;
const CARD = /\b(?:\d[ -]?){13,19}\b/g;
const IBAN = /\b[A-Z]{2}\d{2}(?:\s?[A-Z0-9]{4}){2,7}(?:\s?[A-Z0-9]{1,4})?\b/;
const COORDS = /-?\d{1,3}\.\d{4,}\s*,\s*-?\d{1,3}\.\d{4,}/;
const ADDRESS = [
  /\b\d{1,5}[a-z]?\s+(?:[\p{L}'-]+\s+){1,3}(?:street|st|avenue|ave|road|rd|lane|ln|boulevard|blvd|drive|dr)\b/iu,
  /\b(?:calle|avenida|av\.?|rua|travessa|paseo|carrera)\s+(?:[\p{L}.'-]+\s+){1,4}\d{1,5}\b/iu,
  /\b\d{1,5}(?:\s?(?:bis|ter))?,?\s+(?:rue|avenue|boulevard|bd|allée|chemin|place)\b/iu,
];
export function containsPii(body: string): boolean {
  if (EMAIL.test(body) || PHONE.test(body) || IBAN.test(body) || COORDS.test(body)) return true;
  for (const m of body.matchAll(CARD)) if (luhn(m[0].replace(/\D/g, ''))) return true;
  return ADDRESS.some((re) => re.test(body));
}
const TLD = '(?:com|org|net|io|ru|info|xyz|me|ly|gg|co|tk|to|su|cn|link|click|top)';
const LINK = [
  /\bhttps?:\/\//i, /\bhxxps?\b/i, /\bwww\./i, /\bt\.me\//i,
  new RegExp(`\\b[a-z0-9-]{2,}\\.${TLD}\\b`, 'i'),
  // obfuscated separators: t[.]me, site(.)com, example [dot] org, example dot com, example . com
  new RegExp(`\\b[a-z0-9-]+\\s*(?:\\[\\.\\]|\\(\\.\\)|\\{\\.\\}|\\[dot\\]|\\(dot\\)|\\s\\.\\s|\\s+dot\\s+)\\s*${TLD}\\b`, 'i'),
];
export const containsLink = (body: string): boolean => LINK.some((re) => re.test(body));
export function mostlyOutsideSiteScripts(body: string): boolean {
  const letters = body.match(/\p{L}/gu)?.length ?? 0;
  if (letters < 10) return false;
  const latin = body.match(/\p{Script=Latin}/gu)?.length ?? 0;
  return (letters - latin) / letters > 0.5;
}
export function bodyHash(body: string): string {
  let h = 0xcbf29ce484222325n;
  for (const b of new TextEncoder().encode(body)) h = ((h ^ BigInt(b)) * 0x100000001b3n) & 0xffffffffffffffffn;
  return (h >> 12n).toString(16).padStart(13, '0');
}

function hasSlur(body: string, t: TermList): boolean {
  const words = new Set(fold(body).split(' ').filter(Boolean));
  const flat = squash(body);
  for (const list of Object.values(t.terms)) {
    for (const raw of list) {
      const term = squash(raw);
      if (!term) continue;
      if (term.length >= 5 ? flat.includes(term) : words.has(term)) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}
function isShouting(body: string): boolean {
  if (/(.)\1{9,}/u.test(body)) return true;
  const letters = body.match(/\p{L}/gu) ?? [];
  const upper = letters.filter((c) => c !== c.toLowerCase()).length;
  return letters.length >= 20 && upper / letters.length > 0.7;
}
export function evaluate(i: FilterInput): Verdict {
  if (!termsUsable(i.terms)) throw new FiltersUnavailable('term list missing, empty or unversioned');
  if (i.body.length === 0) return { kind: 'reject', code: 'empty' };
  if (i.ownRecent.includes(i.body)) return { kind: 'reject', code: 'duplicate' };
  if (i.othersSameBody >= 2) return { kind: 'hold', code: 'duplicate' };
  if (hasSlur(i.body, i.terms)) return { kind: 'hold', code: 'slur' };
  if (containsPii(i.body)) return { kind: 'hold', code: 'pii' };
  if (containsLink(i.body)) return { kind: 'hold', code: 'link' };
  if (mostlyOutsideSiteScripts(i.body)) return { kind: 'hold', code: 'language' };
  if (isShouting(i.body)) return { kind: 'reject', code: 'shouting' };
  return { kind: 'accept' };
}
```

The ban check (spec §4.1.2) is done by the room before `evaluate` (Task 13), because it needs the room's `bans` table. Run `npm --prefix worker test -- chat/normalize chat/filters`. Expected: PASS. A failing corpus case is fixed in the pattern, never by deleting the case; if a pattern cannot satisfy both a positive and a negative case, stop and report the pair.

```ts
import type { ChatMode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { LIMITS } from './limits';

export const utcDay = (now: number) => new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
export const utcHour = (now: number) => new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 13);
export const msUntilUtcMidnight = (now: number) => 86_400_000 - (now % 86_400_000);
const msUntilNextHour = (now: number) => 3_600_000 - (now % 3_600_000);

export function checkIdentity(u: IdentityUsage, now: number, mode: ChatMode): RateDecision {
  if (mode === 'readonly' || mode === 'closed') return { ok: false, cooldownMs: 0 };
  if (u.dayCount >= LIMITS.perDay) return { ok: false, cooldownMs: msUntilUtcMidnight(now) };
  if (u.hourCount >= LIMITS.perHour) return { ok: false, cooldownMs: msUntilNextHour(now) };
  const gap = mode === 'slow' ? LIMITS.slowBurstMs : LIMITS.burstMs;
  const wait = u.lastSendAt + gap - now;
  return wait > 0 ? { ok: false, cooldownMs: wait } : { ok: true };
}
export const checkReport = (reportsThisHour: number) => reportsThisHour < LIMITS.reportsPerHour;
export function roomMinute(prev: { start: number; count: number }, now: number) {
  if (now - prev.start >= 60_000) return { start: now, count: 1, triggerSlow: false };
  const count = prev.count + 1;
  return { start: prev.start, count, triggerSlow: count === LIMITS.roomPerMinute };
}
export function roomBudget(msgs: number) {
  const ratio = msgs / LIMITS.roomMsgsPerDay;
  return { ratio, alert: ratio >= LIMITS.budgetAlertRatio, exhausted: ratio >= 1 };
}
```

(with the `IdentityUsage` / `RateDecision` types from the interface block). Run the test. Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **12.2 Implement.** `worker/chat/crypto.ts`:

```ts
const enc = new TextEncoder();
export function b64url(bytes: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): string {
  const u = bytes instanceof Uint8Array ? bytes : new Uint8Array(bytes);
  let s = '';
  for (const b of u) s += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(s).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
}
export function fromB64url(s: string): Uint8Array {
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(s)) throw new Error('bad base64url');
  const bin = atob(s.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/') + '='.repeat((4 - (s.length % 4)) % 4));
  return Uint8Array.from(bin, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
}
const key = (secret: string) => crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', enc.encode(secret), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign', 'verify']);
export async function sign(secret: string, data: string) { return b64url(await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', await key(secret), enc.encode(data))); }
export async function verifySig(secret: string, data: string, sig: string) {
  try { return await crypto.subtle.verify('HMAC', await key(secret), fromB64url(sig), enc.encode(data)); } catch { return false; }
}
export async function hmacHex(secret: string, data: string) {
  const mac = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', await key(secret), enc.encode(data)));
  return [...mac].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}
export function requireSecret(v: string | undefined, name: string): string {
  if (!v || v.length < 32) throw new Error(`${name} missing`);
  return v;
}
```

`worker/chat/session.ts`:

```ts
import { b64url, fromB64url, hmacHex, sign, verifySig } from './crypto';
import { RESERVED_HANDLES } from './limits';
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';

export const SESSION_COOKIE = '__Host-wb_chat';
export const SESSION_TTL_S = 2_592_000;
export async function signSession(p: SessionPayload, key: string) {
  const body = b64url(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(p)));
  return `${body}.${await sign(key, body)}`;
}
export async function verifySession(token: string, key: string, now: number): Promise<SessionPayload | null> {
  const parts = token.split('.');
  if (parts.length !== 2 || !(await verifySig(key, parts[0], parts[1]))) return null;
  try {
    const p = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(fromB64url(parts[0]))) as SessionPayload;
    if (typeof p.uid !== 'string' || typeof p.handle !== 'string' || !Number.isInteger(p.gen) || typeof p.exp !== 'number') return null;
    return p.exp * 1000 > now ? p : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}
export function readCookie(req: Request, name: string): string | null {
  for (const part of (req.headers.get('Cookie') ?? '').split(';')) {
    const i = part.indexOf('=');
    if (i > 0 && part.slice(0, i).trim() === name) return part.slice(i + 1).trim();
  }
  return null;
}
export const sessionCookie = (token: string) => `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}; Path=/; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=${SESSION_TTL_S}`;
export const clearSessionCookie = () => `${SESSION_COOKIE}=; Path=/; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=0`;
export const uidHmac = (banKey: string, uid: string) => hmacHex(banKey, uid);
export const isReservedHandle = (login: string) => (RESERVED_HANDLES as readonly string[]).includes(login.toLowerCase());
export function accountOldEnough(createdAtIso: string, now: number, minDays: number): boolean {
  const t = Date.parse(createdAtIso);
  return Number.isFinite(t) && now - t >= minDays * 86_400_000;
}
export function minAccountAgeDays(env: ChatEnv): number {
  const n = Number(env.CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS);
  if (env.CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS === undefined || !Number.isInteger(n) || n < 0) throw new Error('CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS not set (owner decision Q3)');
  return n;
}
```

### Task 13: `ChatRoom` — hello, send/ack/broadcast, idempotency, holds, modes, budget

No gate beyond Task 7. Spec §3.2, §3.4, §3.7, §4.1, §5.3. Branch `feat/chat-backend`. Still dark: production `CHAT_ENABLED` is unset and `CHAT_ROOMS = "_canary"`.

DO-to-DO calls use **JS RPC** on stubs (methods on the class), available at the pinned compatibility date. Step 13.3's first test run is also the check that RPC works under the pool; if it does not, stop and report — do not fall back to hand-rolled fetch routes without a plan update.

**Rule for `onSend` (review #6):** the DO input gate opens while the object awaits another object, so two frames could both pass the rate limit and the nonce check. `onSend` therefore contains **no `await` before its INSERT**: term list, bans and global mode are read from the room's own SQLite (D4, D12), and the only await (the automated decision record) comes after the write and the ack. A comment at the top of `onSend` states the rule; the race test in 13.2 enforces it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `worker/chat/router.ts` (identity headers), `worker/chat/room.ts`, `worker/chat/mod.ts` (terms/gen/global-mode/sync RPC)
- Create: `worker/test/helpers.ts`, `worker/test/room.test.ts`, `worker/test/room-cost.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// router.ts — identity is established ONLY here and passed to the room in headers the client cannot set
export const IDENTITY_HEADERS = ['X-Chat-Room', 'X-Chat-Uid', 'X-Chat-Handle', 'X-Chat-Uid-Hmac', 'X-Chat-Moderator', 'X-Chat-Banned'] as const;
// every incoming X-Chat-* header is deleted first; then, if the session cookie verifies AND its gen equals
// ChatMod.getUserState(uidHmac).gen, the router sets X-Chat-Uid/Handle/Uid-Hmac (X-Chat-Moderator: 1 when the
// numeric GitHub id is in CHAT_ADMIN_IDS; X-Chat-Banned: JSON {until, ground} when a ban is active, so the
// reader sees why and until when — review #20). Global mode 'closed' (ChatMod.getGlobalMode, cached 30 s per
// isolate) → refuseSocket([mode closed frame], 4002) before the room.

// mod.ts (RPC, this task)
getTerms(): TermList | null;                    setTerms(list: TermList['terms']): Promise<number>;   // returns new version
getUserState(uidHmac: string): { gen: number; ban?: Sanction };   bumpGen(uidHmac: string): Promise<number>;  // bump also revokes open sockets (Task 14)
getGlobalMode(): ModeState;
syncState(slug: string, bansVersion: number): { global: ModeState; bansVersion: number; bans?: Array<{ uidHmac: string; until: number; ground: string }>;
  terms: TermList | null };                     // registers the room (review #7); bans only when the version differs
checkKeyFingerprint(fp: string): boolean;       // first call stores it; later calls compare (review #34)

// room.ts (RPC, called by ChatMod / admin in Tasks 14-17)
applyBan(uidHmac: string, until: number, ground: string): void;
revoke(uidHmac: string): number;                // closes that user's sockets with 4009; returns how many
setMode(mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number): void;
setGlobal(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number): void;
termsChanged(list: TermList): void;             // stores the list locally; reopens a filters_unavailable room
moderate(msgId: number, action: 'publish' | 'remove', reason: string, preserve: boolean): { ok: boolean; facts?: string };
markReported(msgId: number, grave: boolean): { state: string | null; held: boolean };   // null state = no such message; grave → provisional hold (D13)
hideForReports(msgId: number): boolean;         // three trusted reporters (spec §4.3)
messageState(msgId: number): string | null;     // for /notice validation (review #15)
clearReported(msgId: number, preserve: boolean): { facts?: string };
eraseUid(uid: string): number;                  // returns the count re-read from SQLite
exportUid(uid: string): ChatMessage[];
stats(): RoomStats;                             // no full-table count: see 13.5
context(msgId: number, around: number): ChatMessage[];
ioTotals(): { read: number; written: number };  // sum of cursor.rowsRead / rowsWritten since construction (D11)
```

```ts
import { env, exports } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import type { ServerFrame } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';
import { signSession } from '../chat/session';
import type { ChatEnv } from '../chat/env';
import type { ChatRoom } from '../chat/room';
import type { ChatMod } from '../chat/mod';

export const E = env as unknown as ChatEnv & { CHAT_ROOM: DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>; CHAT_MOD: DurableObjectNamespace<ChatMod> };
export const TERMS = { en: ['badword'], es: ['palabrota'], fr: ['grosmot'], pt: ['palavrao'] };
export const mod = () => modStub(E);
export const room = (slug = 'test-room') => roomStub(E, slug);
/** Restore shared state after a test that changed it (Task 1.3 decides whether this is strictly needed; it is always safe). */
export async function resetChat() {
  await mod().setGlobalMode('open');
  resetGlobalCacheForTests();
}
export async function cookieFor(uid: string, handle: string, gen = 0) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  return `__Host-wb_chat=${await signSession({ uid, handle, iat: now, exp: now + 3600, gen }, E.CHAT_SESSION_KEY!)}`;
}
export async function waitFor<T>(fn: () => T | undefined, ms = 1500): Promise<T> {
  const end = Date.now() + ms;
  while (Date.now() < end) {
    const v = fn();
    if (v !== undefined) return v;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));
  }
  throw new Error('waitFor timed out');
}
export async function connect(slug = 'test-room', cookie?: string, extra: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const res = await (exports as { default: Fetcher }).default.fetch(`https://chat.watchboard.dev/room/${slug}`, {
    headers: { Upgrade: 'websocket', Origin: 'https://watchboard.dev', ...(cookie ? { Cookie: cookie } : {}), ...extra },
  });
  if (!res.webSocket) throw new Error(`no socket: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);
  const ws = res.webSocket;
  ws.accept();
  const frames: ServerFrame[] = [];
  let closeCode: number | undefined;
  ws.addEventListener('message', (e) => { frames.push(JSON.parse(e.data as string) as ServerFrame); });
  ws.addEventListener('close', (e) => { closeCode = e.code; });
  const of = <T extends ServerFrame['t']>(t: T) => frames.filter((f) => f.t === t) as Extract<ServerFrame, { t: T }>[];
  return {
    ws, frames, of,
    closed: () => closeCode,
    send: (o: unknown) => ws.send(typeof o === 'string' ? o : JSON.stringify(o)),
    next: <T extends ServerFrame['t']>(t: T, n = 1) => waitFor(() => (of(t).length >= n ? of(t)[n - 1] : undefined)),
  };
}
export const nonce = () => crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16);
```

```ts
    const c = await connect('test-room', undefined, { 'X-Chat-Uid': 'gh:999', 'X-Chat-Handle': 'gh/admin', 'X-Chat-Moderator': '1' });
    expect((await c.next('hello')).me).toBeUndefined();

    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:101', 'gh/alice'));
    const b = await connect();
    await a.next('hello'); await b.next('hello');
    const n = nonce();

    const ack = await a.next('ack');
    expect(ack).toMatchObject({ nonce: n, state: 'visible' });
    const msg = await b.next('msg');
    expect(msg.m).toMatchObject({ id: ack.id, handle: 'gh/alice', body: 'first real message', state: 'visible', lang: 'en' });
    expect(await rows('SELECT uid, state, body FROM messages WHERE id = ?', ack.id)).toEqual([{ uid: 'gh:101', state: 'visible', body: 'first real message' }]);
  });

  it('a retried nonce returns the same ack and writes one row', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:102', 'gh/bob'));
    await a.next('hello');
    const n = nonce();
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'once', nonce: n });
    const first = await a.next('ack');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'once', nonce: n });
    const second = await a.next('ack', 2);
    expect(second.id).toBe(first.id);
    expect(await rows('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE nonce = ?', n)).toEqual([{ c: 1 }]);
  });

  it('a held message is acked as held, reaches nobody else, and only its author sees it on reconnect', async () => {
    const cookie = await cookieFor('gh:103', 'gh/carol');
    const a = await connect('test-room', cookie);
    const b = await connect();
    await a.next('hello'); await b.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'write to me at carol@example.org', nonce: nonce() });
    expect(await a.next('ack')).toMatchObject({ state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii' });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    expect(b.of('msg')).toHaveLength(0);
    const a2 = await connect('test-room', cookie);
    expect((await a2.next('hello')).history.find((m) => m.state === 'held')).toMatchObject({ mine: true, holdCode: 'pii' });
    const anon = await connect();
    expect((await anon.next('hello')).history.some((m) => m.state === 'held')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rate limit holds across hibernation (state lives in SQLite + attachment)', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:104', 'gh/dave'));
    await a.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'one', nonce: nonce() });
    await a.next('ack');
    await evictDurableObject(room());
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'two', nonce: nonce() });
    const err = await a.next('err');
    expect(err.code).toBe('rate_limited');          // not auth_required: identity survived in the attachment
    expect(err.cooldownMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('oversized frame closes 1009; three malformed frames close 4008', async () => {
    const big = await connect(); await big.next('hello');
    big.send('x'.repeat(5000));
    expect(await waitFor(() => big.closed())).toBe(1009);
    const bad = await connect(); await bad.next('hello');
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) bad.send('not json');
    expect(await waitFor(() => bad.closed())).toBe(4008);
  });

  it('rate-limit rejections never close the socket and always carry the cooldown (review #38)', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:109', 'gh/ivy'));
    await a.next('hello');
    for (const b of ['r1', 'r2', 'r3', 'r4']) a.send({ t: 'send', body: `quick ${b}`, nonce: nonce() });
    await a.next('ack');
    const third = await a.next('err', 3);
    expect(third).toMatchObject({ code: 'rate_limited' });
    expect(third.cooldownMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    expect(a.closed()).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('two sends in the same tick: one ack, one rate_limited — the checks and the write are not split by an await (review #6)', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:110', 'gh/jon'));
    await a.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'race one', nonce: nonce() });
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'race two', nonce: nonce() });
    await a.next('ack');
    expect(await a.next('err')).toMatchObject({ code: 'rate_limited' });
    expect(await rows("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE uid = 'gh:110'")).toEqual([{ c: 1 }]);
    const n = nonce();
    const b = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:111', 'gh/kim'));
    await b.next('hello');
    b.send({ t: 'send', body: 'same nonce twice', nonce: n });
    b.send({ t: 'send', body: 'same nonce twice', nonce: n });
    const [x, y] = [await b.next('ack'), await b.next('ack', 2)];
    expect(y.id).toBe(x.id);
    expect(await rows('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE nonce = ?', n)).toEqual([{ c: 1 }]);
  });

  it('anonymous readers cannot fill the room for signed-in readers (review #4)', async () => {
    // vitest.config.ts sets CHAT_TEST_MAX_ANON = '3' so the cap is reachable in a test.
    const anons = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const c = await connect('_canary'); await c.next('hello'); anons.push(c); }
    const extra = await connect('_canary');
    expect((await extra.next('hello')).history).toBeDefined();          // the reader still gets the latest messages
    expect(await waitFor(() => extra.closed())).toBe(4004);
    const signed = await connect('_canary', await cookieFor('gh:112', 'gh/lee'));
    expect((await signed.next('hello')).me?.canWrite).toBe(true);
    expect(signed.closed()).toBeUndefined();
    for (const c of anons) c.ws.close();
  });

  it('a closed room serves no history and says so with 4002 (disables access, TCO art. 3)', async () => {
    const w = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:113', 'gh/max'));
    await w.next('hello');
    w.send({ t: 'send', body: 'visible before closing', nonce: nonce() });
    await w.next('ack');
    await room().setMode('closed', 'owner');
    const c = await connect();
    expect(await c.next('hello')).toMatchObject({ mode: 'closed', history: [] });
    expect(await waitFor(() => c.closed())).toBe(4002);
    await room().setMode('open', 'owner');
  });

  it('missing term list fails closed: room goes readonly and says why', async () => {
    await runInDurableObject(mod(), (_i, st) => { st.storage.sql.exec("DELETE FROM settings WHERE k = 'terms'"); });
    await runInDurableObject(room('_canary'), (_i, st) => { st.storage.sql.exec("DELETE FROM room WHERE k IN ('terms', 'sync')"); });
    const a = await connect('_canary', await cookieFor('gh:105', 'gh/erin'));
    await a.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'hello', nonce: nonce() });
    expect(await a.next('mode')).toMatchObject({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'filters_unavailable' });
    expect(await a.next('err')).toMatchObject({ code: 'readonly' });
  });

  it('author can delete; others get remove; a stranger cannot', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:106', 'gh/fay'));
    const s = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:107', 'gh/gus'));
    await a.next('hello'); await s.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'to delete', nonce: nonce() });
    const { id } = await a.next('ack');
    s.send({ t: 'delete', id });
    expect(await s.next('err')).toMatchObject({ code: 'not_author' });
    a.send({ t: 'delete', id });
    expect(await s.next('remove')).toMatchObject({ id });
    expect(await rows('SELECT state, body FROM messages WHERE id = ?', id)).toEqual([{ state: 'deleted', body: '' }]);
  });

  it('reaching the daily message budget turns the room readonly until midnight', async () => {
    const day = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    await evictDurableObject(room('t-cost'));             // drop the in-memory stats so the seeded row is read
    await runInDurableObject(room('t-cost'), (_i, st) => {
      st.storage.sql.exec("INSERT INTO room (k, v) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET v = excluded.v",
        `stats:${day}`, JSON.stringify({ day, msgs: 999, conns: 0, minuteStart: 0, minuteCount: 0, budgetAlerted: true, undelivered: 0, lastAlarmAt: 0 }));
    });
    const a = await connect('t-cost', await cookieFor('gh:108', 'gh/hal'));
    await a.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'the last one today', nonce: nonce() });
    await a.next('ack');
    const m = await a.next('mode');
    expect(m).toMatchObject({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget' });
    expect(m.until).toBe(Date.parse(day + 'T00:00:00Z') + 86_400_000);
    await room('t-cost').setMode('open', 'owner');
    await runInDurableObject(room('t-cost'), (_i, st) => { st.storage.sql.exec("DELETE FROM room WHERE k LIKE 'stats:%'"); });
    await evictDurableObject(room('t-cost'));
  });
});
```

`worker/test/room-cost.test.ts` — the row budget of spec §7.3 is checked against what SQLite actually bills (review #2, #3):

```ts
import { runInDurableObject } from 'cloudflare:test';
import { it, expect, beforeAll } from 'vitest';
import { connect, cookieFor, mod, room, nonce, TERMS } from './helpers';

const SLUG = 't-cost';
beforeAll(async () => {
  await mod().setTerms(TERMS);
  await runInDurableObject(room(SLUG), (_i, st) => {       // 10,000 old messages from 500 identities
    for (let i = 0; i < 10_000; i++)
      st.storage.sql.exec("INSERT INTO messages (ts, uid, handle, body, body_hash, state, nonce) VALUES (?, ?, 'gh/x', ?, ?, 'visible', ?)",
        Date.now() - 20 * 86_400_000 + i, `gh:${9000 + (i % 500)}`, `seed ${i}`, `h${i}`, `seed-${i}`);
  });
  const warm = await connect(SLUG); await warm.next('hello'); warm.ws.close();   // first connect registers + syncs
});
it('an anonymous connect writes nothing and reads about one page of history', async () => {
  const before = await room(SLUG).ioTotals();
  const c = await connect(SLUG); await c.next('hello');
  const after = await room(SLUG).ioTotals();
  expect(after.written - before.written).toBe(0);
  expect(after.read - before.read).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
  const c2 = await connect(SLUG); await c2.next('hello');
  expect((await room(SLUG).ioTotals()).read - after.read).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);   // warm room: history from memory
  c.ws.close(); c2.ws.close();
});
it('one accepted message costs at most 9 rows written and 150 rows read with 10,000 rows in the table', async () => {
  const a = await connect(SLUG, await cookieFor('gh:8001', 'gh/cost')); await a.next('hello');
  const before = await room(SLUG).ioTotals();
  a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'a normal comment about the news', nonce: nonce() });
  await a.next('ack');
  const after = await room(SLUG).ioTotals();
  console.log(`rows per send: written=${after.written - before.written} read=${after.read - before.read}`);
  expect(after.written - before.written).toBeLessThanOrEqual(9);
  expect(after.read - before.read).toBeLessThanOrEqual(150);
});
```

The two `console.log`-ed numbers are what ADR-0003 and spec §7.3 record (13.8). If a ceiling fails, fix the query or the index — never raise the ceiling without recomputing spec §7.3 in the same PR.

Run `npm --prefix worker test -- test/room`. Expected: FAIL (room answers 503 `room_not_ready`; `setTerms` not a function).

- [ ] **13.3 ChatMod RPC for terms, generation, global mode, registry and sync** (`worker/chat/mod.ts`; replaces the skeleton's `fetch`, which now answers 404 — ChatMod is reached only by RPC):

```ts
import { DurableObject } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import type { ChatMode, ChatModeReason } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import type { Sanction } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';
import { applySchema, MOD_SCHEMA } from './schema';
import { parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { roomStub } from './stubs';
import type { TermList } from './filters';

export interface ModeState { mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number }
export class ChatMod extends DurableObject<ChatEnv> {
  constructor(ctx: DurableObjectState, env: ChatEnv) { super(ctx, env); applySchema(ctx.storage.sql, MOD_SCHEMA); }
  private exec(q: string, ...b: unknown[]) { return this.ctx.storage.sql.exec(q, ...b).toArray(); }
  private get(k: string): string | null { const r = this.exec('SELECT v FROM settings WHERE k = ?', k)[0]; return r ? String(r.v) : null; }
  private put(k: string, v: string) { this.exec('INSERT INTO settings (k, v) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET v = excluded.v', k, v); }
  async fetch(): Promise<Response> { return new Response('not found', { status: 404 }); }

  /** Every room ever created plus the configured ones: erasure, bans and modes must reach rooms removed from CHAT_ROOMS (review #7). */
  protected rooms(): string[] {
    const reg = this.exec('SELECT slug FROM rooms').map((r) => String(r.slug));
    return [...new Set([...reg, ...parseRooms(this.env.CHAT_ROOMS)])];
  }
  protected room(slug: string) { return roomStub(this.env, slug); }
  /** Fan-out that never hides a failure: rooms that did not answer are counted and pick the state up on their next sync (D4). */
  protected async fanOut(fn: (slug: string) => Promise<unknown>): Promise<string[]> {
    const slugs = this.rooms();
    const res = await Promise.allSettled(slugs.map(fn));
    const failed = slugs.filter((_s, i) => res[i].status === 'rejected');
    if (failed.length) this.put(`fanout_failed:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`, String(Number(this.get(`fanout_failed:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`) ?? 0) + failed.length));
    return failed;
  }

  getTerms(): TermList | null { const raw = this.get('terms'); return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as TermList) : null; }
  async setTerms(terms: TermList['terms']): Promise<number> {
    const list: TermList = { version: (this.getTerms()?.version ?? 0) + 1, terms };
    this.put('terms', JSON.stringify(list));
    await this.fanOut((s) => this.room(s).termsChanged(list));
    return list.version;
  }
  getUserState(uidHmac: string): { gen: number; ban?: Sanction } {
    const u = this.exec('SELECT gen FROM users WHERE uid_hmac = ?', uidHmac)[0];
    const b = this.exec('SELECT until, ground FROM bans WHERE uid_hmac = ? AND until > ?', uidHmac, Date.now())[0];
    return { gen: u ? Number(u.gen) : 0, ...(b ? { ban: { until: Number(b.until), ground: String(b.ground) } } : {}) };
  }
  /** Bumping the generation also closes that user's open sockets everywhere (review #41). */
  async bumpGen(uidHmac: string): Promise<number> {
    this.exec('INSERT INTO users (uid_hmac, gen) VALUES (?, 1) ON CONFLICT(uid_hmac) DO UPDATE SET gen = gen + 1', uidHmac);
    await this.fanOut((s) => this.room(s).revoke(uidHmac));
    return this.getUserState(uidHmac).gen;
  }
  getGlobalMode(): ModeState {
    const raw = this.get('global_mode');
    if (!raw) return { mode: 'open' };
    const g = JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState;
    return g.until && g.until <= Date.now() ? { mode: 'open' } : g;
  }
  syncState(slug: string, bansVersion: number) {
    this.exec('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO rooms (slug, first_seen) VALUES (?, ?)', slug, Date.now());
    const version = Number(this.get('bans_version') ?? 0);
    const bans = version === bansVersion ? undefined : this.exec('SELECT uid_hmac, until, ground FROM bans WHERE until > ?', Date.now())
      .map((r) => ({ uidHmac: String(r.uid_hmac), until: Number(r.until), ground: String(r.ground) }));
    return { global: this.getGlobalMode(), bansVersion: version, ...(bans ? { bans } : {}), terms: this.getTerms() };
  }
  /** CHAT_BAN_KEY must never rotate: every ban, reporter record and session generation is keyed by it (review #34). */
  checkKeyFingerprint(fp: string): boolean {
    const stored = this.get('ban_key_fp');
    if (stored === null) { this.put('ban_key_fp', fp); return true; }
    return stored === fp;
  }
}
```

`setGlobalMode` and `ban` (Task 14) bump `bans_version`/store the mode **before** fanning out, so a room that misses the fan-out converges on its next sync.

and add to `router.ts` (`modStub` is imported from `./stubs`, never redefined here):

```ts
let globalCache: { at: number; value: ModeState } | null = null;
async function cachedGlobalMode(env: ChatEnv) {
  if (globalCache && Date.now() - globalCache.at < 30_000) return globalCache.value;
  const value = await modStub(env).getGlobalMode();
  globalCache = { at: Date.now(), value };
  return value;
}
let keyChecked = false;
export async function identityFromCookie(request: Request, env: ChatEnv) {
  const token = readCookie(request, SESSION_COOKIE);
  if (!token) return null;
  const s = await verifySession(token, requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY'), Date.now());
  if (!s) return null;
  const banKey = requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY');
  if (!keyChecked) {   // a rotated CHAT_BAN_KEY would silently lift every ban: fail closed instead (review #34)
    if (!(await modStub(env).checkKeyFingerprint(await uidHmac(banKey, 'fingerprint')))) {
      console.error('CHAT_BAN_KEY differs from the stored fingerprint: every identity is refused (reads continue, writes stop)');
      return null;     // ChatMod.health() reports keyOk:false, which fails the canary (Task 19)
    }
    keyChecked = true;
  }
  const hmac = await uidHmac(banKey, s.uid);
  const st = await modStub(env).getUserState(hmac);
  if (st.gen !== s.gen) return null;          // revoked by "sign out", erasure or a session revocation
  const ghId = s.uid.startsWith('gh:') ? s.uid.slice(3) : '';
  const admins = (env.CHAT_ADMIN_IDS ?? '').split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return { uid: s.uid, handle: s.handle, uidHmac: hmac, moderator: ghId !== '' && admins.includes(ghId), ...(st.ban ? { ban: st.ban } : {}) };
}
export function resetGlobalCacheForTests() { globalCache = null; keyChecked = false; }
```

The fake-env tests of Task 7 now need a `CHAT_MOD` whose stub has `getGlobalMode: async () => ({ mode: 'open' })`, `getUserState: async () => ({ gen: 0 })` and `checkKeyFingerprint: async () => true`; update `fakeEnv` accordingly, and add tests: global mode `closed` → status 101, one `{ t: 'mode', mode: 'closed', reason: 'global' }` frame and close `4002`, without `CHAT_ROOM.idFromName`; `checkKeyFingerprint` answering `false` → a valid cookie yields no identity (`identityFromCookie` returns `null`). Call `resetGlobalCacheForTests()` in a `beforeEach`.

- [ ] **13.5 Room — connection, state and hello** (`worker/chat/room.ts`, first half):

```ts
import { DurableObject } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { encode, parseClientFrame, CLOSE, MAX_FRAME_BYTES, PROTOCOL_VERSION, type ClientFrame, type Sanction, type ServerFrame } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';
import type { ChatMessage, ChatMode, ChatModeReason, HoldCode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { applySchema, ROOM_SCHEMA } from './schema';
import { bodyHash, evaluate, FiltersUnavailable, type TermList } from './filters';
import { normalize } from './normalize';
import { checkIdentity, checkReport, msUntilUtcMidnight, roomBudget, roomMinute, utcDay, utcHour, type IdentityUsage } from './ratelimit';
import { HISTORY_LIMIT, LIMITS } from './limits';
import { modStub } from './stubs';
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';

interface Conn { uid?: string; handle?: string; uidHmac?: string; moderator: boolean; ban?: Sanction; badFrames: number }
interface ModeState { mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number }
export interface RoomStats { day: string; msgs: number; conns: number; minuteStart: number; minuteCount: number;
  budgetAlerted: boolean; undelivered: number; lastAlarmAt: number }
const RANK: Record<ChatMode, number> = { open: 0, slow: 1, readonly: 2, closed: 3 };
const freshStats = (day: string): RoomStats =>
  ({ day, msgs: 0, conns: 0, minuteStart: 0, minuteCount: 0, budgetAlerted: false, undelivered: 0, lastAlarmAt: 0 });

export class ChatRoom extends DurableObject<ChatEnv> {
  private io = { read: 0, written: 0 };                       // billed rows since construction (D11)
  private st: RoomStats | null = null;                        // today's counters; persisted in room 'stats:<day>'
  private termsMem: TermList | null = null;                   // parsed copy of room 'terms'
  private lastSync = 0;
  /** Last 100 visible messages while the object is awake: anonymous connects then read 0 rows, so a connection
   *  flood cannot burn the account's 5M rows-read/day (spec §7.3). */
  private visibleCache: ChatMessage[] | null = null;
  constructor(ctx: DurableObjectState, env: ChatEnv) { super(ctx, env); applySchema(ctx.storage.sql, ROOM_SCHEMA); }

  /** Every statement goes through here so rowsRead/rowsWritten are measured, not guessed. */
  private sql(q: string, ...b: unknown[]) {
    if (/^\s*(INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE)\b[\s\S]*\bmessages\b/i.test(q)) this.visibleCache = null;   // any write to messages invalidates
    const cur = this.ctx.storage.sql.exec(q, ...b);
    const rows = cur.toArray();
    this.io.read += cur.rowsRead; this.io.written += cur.rowsWritten;
    return rows;
  }
  ioTotals() { return { ...this.io }; }
  private kv(k: string): string | null { const r = this.sql('SELECT v FROM room WHERE k = ?', k)[0]; return r ? String(r.v) : null; }
  private setKv(k: string, v: string) { this.sql('INSERT INTO room (k, v) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET v = excluded.v', k, v); }
  private today(now = Date.now()): RoomStats {
    const day = utcDay(now);
    if (this.st?.day !== day) { const raw = this.kv(`stats:${day}`); this.st = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as RoomStats) : freshStats(day); }
    return this.st;
  }
  private saveStats() { if (this.st) this.setKv(`stats:${this.st.day}`, JSON.stringify(this.st)); }   // one row, at most once per send
  private terms(): TermList | null {
    if (!this.termsMem) { const raw = this.kv('terms'); this.termsMem = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as TermList) : null; }
    return this.termsMem;
  }
  private usage(uid: string, now: number): IdentityUsage {
    const r = this.sql('SELECT * FROM usage WHERE uid = ?', uid)[0];
    return { lastSendAt: r ? Number(r.last) : 0, hourCount: r && r.hour === utcHour(now) ? Number(r.hour_n) : 0,
      dayCount: r && r.day === utcDay(now) ? Number(r.day_n) : 0 };
  }
  private recordSend(uid: string, now: number, u: IdentityUsage) {   // one WITHOUT ROWID row per identity
    this.sql(`INSERT INTO usage (uid, last, hour, hour_n, day, day_n) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(uid) DO UPDATE SET last = excluded.last, hour = excluded.hour, hour_n = excluded.hour_n, day = excluded.day, day_n = excluded.day_n`,
      uid, now, utcHour(now), u.hourCount + 1, utcDay(now), u.dayCount + 1);
  }
  private mod() { return modStub(this.env); }
  /** Pull global mode, bans and terms from ChatMod (D4). Never called from onSend. A failed pull stops writes. */
  private async sync(now: number, force = false) {
    if (!force && now - this.lastSync < LIMITS.syncEveryMs) return;
    try {
      const bansVersion = Number(this.kv('bans_version') ?? -1);
      const s = await this.mod().syncState(this.kv('slug') ?? '', bansVersion);
      this.ctx.storage.transactionSync(() => {
        if (s.bans) {
          this.sql('DELETE FROM bans');
          for (const b of s.bans) this.sql('INSERT INTO bans (uid_hmac, until, ground) VALUES (?, ?, ?)', b.uidHmac, b.until, b.ground);
          this.setKv('bans_version', String(s.bansVersion));
        }
        if (s.terms && s.terms.version !== this.terms()?.version) { this.setKv('terms', JSON.stringify(s.terms)); this.termsMem = s.terms; }
        if (this.kv('sync_failed')) this.sql("DELETE FROM room WHERE k = 'sync_failed'");
        if (this.kv('global') !== JSON.stringify(s.global)) this.changeMode(s.global, 'global');   // a missed fan-out converges here
      });
      this.lastSync = now;
    } catch {
      if (!this.kv('sync_failed')) this.setKv('sync_failed', String(now));
    }
  }

  /** Most restrictive of room mode and global mode; expired modes read as open. */
  private mode(now = Date.now()): ModeState {
    const read = (k: string): ModeState => {
      const raw = this.kv(k);
      if (!raw) return { mode: 'open' };
      const m = JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState;
      return m.until && m.until <= now ? { mode: 'open' } : m;
    };
    const room = read('mode');
    // A room that could not reach ChatMod cannot know the global mode or the bans: it refuses writes (D4, review #33).
    const global: ModeState = this.kv('sync_failed') ? { mode: 'readonly' } : read('global');
    return RANK[global.mode] > RANK[room.mode] ? { ...global, reason: 'global' } : room;
  }
  private broadcast(frame: ServerFrame, except?: WebSocket) {
    const s = encode(frame);
    for (const ws of this.ctx.getWebSockets()) if (ws !== except) { try { ws.send(s); } catch { /* socket gone */ } }
  }
  private changeMode(m: ModeState, key: 'mode' | 'global' = 'mode') {
    this.setKv(key, JSON.stringify(m));
    const eff = this.mode();
    this.broadcast({ t: 'mode', mode: eff.mode, ...(eff.reason ? { reason: eff.reason } : {}), ...(eff.until ? { until: eff.until } : {}) });
  }
  private toMessage(r: Record<string, unknown>, viewerUid?: string): ChatMessage {
    const m: ChatMessage = { id: Number(r.id), ts: Number(r.ts), handle: String(r.handle), body: String(r.body), state: r.state as ChatMessage['state'] };
    if (r.lang) m.lang = String(r.lang);
    if (r.hold_code) m.holdCode = r.hold_code as HoldCode;
    if (r.reason) m.reason = String(r.reason);
    if (viewerUid && r.uid === viewerUid) m.mine = true;
    if (Number(r.moderator) === 1) m.moderator = true;   // rendered as the text "Watchboard (moderation)", never a colour
    return m;
  }
  /** Two indexed reads instead of one OR query (review #2): the last 100 visible by rowid, plus the viewer's own held/removed. */
  private history(viewerUid?: string): ChatMessage[] {
    if (!this.visibleCache)
      this.visibleCache = this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE state = 'visible' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", HISTORY_LIMIT)
        .reverse().map((r) => this.toMessage(r));
    if (!viewerUid) return this.visibleCache;
    // Signed-in viewers: the cached page re-marked as "mine" where it is, plus their own held/removed rows (indexed by uid).
    const mineIds = new Set(this.sql('SELECT id FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND ts > ?', viewerUid, Date.now() - 30 * 86_400_000).map((r) => Number(r.id)));
    const own = this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND ts > ? AND state IN ('held','removed') ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT 20",
      viewerUid, Date.now() - 30 * 86_400_000).map((r) => this.toMessage(r, viewerUid));
    return [...this.visibleCache.map((m) => (mineIds.has(m.id) ? { ...m, mine: true } : m)), ...own].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
  }
  private anonCap() { return Number(this.env.CHAT_TEST_MAX_ANON ?? LIMITS.maxAnonSockets); }   // test-only override
  /** A socket that exists only to explain a refusal: not hibernatable, closed right after the frames (review #36). */
  private explain(frames: ServerFrame[], code: number, reason: string): Response {
    const pair = new WebSocketPair();
    pair[1].accept();
    for (const f of frames) pair[1].send(encode(f));
    pair[1].close(code, reason);
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: pair[0] });
  }

  async fetch(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    if (request.headers.get('Upgrade')?.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') return new Response('upgrade required', { status: 426 });
    const h = request.headers;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!this.kv('slug')) this.setKv('slug', h.get('X-Chat-Room') ?? '');
    await this.sync(now);              // registers the room; pulls global mode, bans and terms (D4, D12). Not on the send path.
    const conn: Conn = {
      uid: h.get('X-Chat-Uid') ?? undefined, handle: h.get('X-Chat-Handle') ?? undefined,
      uidHmac: h.get('X-Chat-Uid-Hmac') ?? undefined, moderator: h.get('X-Chat-Moderator') === '1', badFrames: 0,
      ...(h.get('X-Chat-Banned') ? { ban: JSON.parse(h.get('X-Chat-Banned')!) as Sanction } : {}),
    };
    const anon = !conn.uid;
    const m = this.mode(now);
    const cooldownMs = conn.uid ? (() => { const r = checkIdentity(this.usage(conn.uid!, now), now, m.mode); return r.ok ? 0 : r.cooldownMs; })() : 0;
    const hello: ServerFrame = {
      t: 'hello', v: PROTOCOL_VERSION, mode: m.mode, history: m.mode === 'closed' ? [] : this.history(conn.uid),
      ...(m.reason ? { reason: m.reason } : {}), ...(m.until ? { until: m.until } : {}),
      ...(conn.uid && conn.handle ? { me: { handle: conn.handle, canWrite: RANK[m.mode] < 2 && !conn.ban, cooldownMs,
        ...(conn.ban ? { banned: conn.ban } : {}) } } : {}),
    };
    // Closed rooms serve no content at all (TCO art. 3: disabling access counts). Full rooms still show the latest messages.
    if (m.mode === 'closed') return this.explain([hello], CLOSE.closed, 'closed');
    const all = this.ctx.getWebSockets().length;
    if (all >= LIMITS.maxSockets || (anon && this.ctx.getWebSockets('anon').length >= this.anonCap()))
      return this.explain([hello, { t: 'err', code: 'room_full' }], CLOSE.full, 'room full');
    const pair = new WebSocketPair();
    const [client, server] = [pair[0], pair[1]];
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server, [anon ? 'anon' : 'auth']);
    server.serializeAttachment(conn);
    server.send(encode(hello));
    this.today(now).conns += 1;        // memory only: flushed with the next send or alarm, so an anonymous connect writes 0 rows (D10)
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }
```

(the class stays open; 13.6 continues inside it). Add `CHAT_TEST_MAX_ANON?: string` to `ChatEnv` and `CHAT_TEST_MAX_ANON: '3'` to the `miniflare.bindings` of `worker/vitest.config.ts`; production never sets it. The first `hello` of a never-synced room is sent after `sync()`, so a new room already has the term list and the global mode before its first reader arrives.

- [ ] **13.6 Room — frames and RPC** (`worker/chat/room.ts`, second half, inside the class):

```ts
  async webSocketMessage(ws: WebSocket, raw: string | ArrayBuffer) {
    const size = typeof raw === 'string' ? new TextEncoder().encode(raw).length : raw.byteLength;
    if (size > MAX_FRAME_BYTES) { ws.close(1009, 'frame too large'); return; }
    const conn = ws.deserializeAttachment() as Conn;
    const frame = parseClientFrame(raw);
    if (!frame) return this.badFrame(ws, conn);
    if (frame.t === 'send') return this.onSend(ws, conn, frame);
    if (frame.t === 'delete') return this.onDelete(ws, conn, frame.id);
    return this.onReport(ws, conn, frame.id, frame.category);           // implemented in Task 14.4
  }
  async webSocketClose(ws: WebSocket, code: number) { try { ws.close(code === 1005 ? 1000 : code, 'bye'); } catch { /* already closed */ } }

  /** A refusal the reader can act on (cooldown, readonly, banned…): sent, never counted, never closes (review #38). */
  private reject(ws: WebSocket, _conn: Conn, err: Extract<ServerFrame, { t: 'err' }>) { ws.send(encode(err)); }
  /** Only malformed frames count toward closing, with 4008 — not 1008, which means "banned" to the client. */
  private badFrame(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn) {
    conn.badFrames += 1;
    ws.serializeAttachment(conn);
    ws.send(encode({ t: 'err', code: 'bad_frame' }));
    if (conn.badFrames >= LIMITS.maxConsecutiveBadFrames) ws.close(CLOSE.badFrames, 'too many malformed frames');
  }
  private accepted(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn) { if (conn.badFrames) { conn.badFrames = 0; ws.serializeAttachment(conn); } }
  private alert(code: AlertCode, extra: Omit<AlertContext, 'room'> = {}) {
    const room = this.kv('slug') ?? 'unknown';
    this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, code, { room, ...extra }).then((ok) => { if (!ok) { this.today().undelivered += 1; this.saveStats(); } }));
  }

  // RULE (review #6): no `await` above the INSERT. Everything this handler checks lives in this object's SQLite.
  private async onSend(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, f: Extract<ClientFrame, { t: 'send' }>) {
    if (!conn.uid || !conn.handle || !conn.uidHmac) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'auth_required', nonce: f.nonce });
    const prior = this.sql('SELECT id, uid, state, hold_code FROM messages WHERE nonce = ?', f.nonce)[0];
    if (prior) {
      if (prior.uid !== conn.uid) return this.badFrame(ws, conn);
      const st = prior.state === 'held' ? 'held' : 'visible';
      ws.send(encode({ t: 'ack', nonce: f.nonce, id: Number(prior.id), state: st, ...(prior.hold_code ? { holdCode: prior.hold_code as HoldCode } : {}) }));
      return;
    }
    const now = Date.now();
    const m = this.mode(now);
    if (m.mode === 'closed' || m.mode === 'readonly')
      return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: m.reason === 'daily_budget' ? 'daily_budget' : m.mode, nonce: f.nonce, ...(m.until ? { until: m.until } : {}) });
    const ban = this.sql('SELECT until, ground FROM bans WHERE uid_hmac = ? AND until > ?', conn.uidHmac, now)[0];
    if (ban) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'banned', nonce: f.nonce, until: Number(ban.until), ground: String(ban.ground) });
    const usage = this.usage(conn.uid, now);
    const rate = checkIdentity(usage, now, m.mode);
    if (!rate.ok) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'rate_limited', nonce: f.nonce, cooldownMs: rate.cooldownMs });

    const body = normalize(f.body);
    const hash = bodyHash(body);
    let verdict;
    try {
      verdict = evaluate({
        body, terms: this.terms(),
        ownRecent: this.sql('SELECT body FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND ts > ?', conn.uid, now - 600_000).map((r) => String(r.body)),
        othersSameBody: new Set(this.sql('SELECT uid, body FROM messages WHERE body_hash = ? AND ts > ?', hash, now - 300_000)
          .filter((r) => r.body === body && r.uid !== conn.uid).map((r) => String(r.uid))).size,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof FiltersUnavailable)) throw e;
      this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'filters_unavailable' });
      this.alert('filters_unavailable');
      return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'readonly', nonce: f.nonce });
    }
    if (verdict.kind === 'reject') return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: verdict.code, nonce: f.nonce });

    const state = verdict.kind === 'hold' ? 'held' : 'visible';
    const holdCode = verdict.kind === 'hold' ? verdict.code : null;
    this.sql('INSERT INTO messages (ts, uid, handle, body, body_hash, lang, moderator, state, hold_code, nonce) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      now, conn.uid, conn.handle, body, hash, f.lang ?? null, conn.moderator ? 1 : 0, state, holdCode, f.nonce);
    const id = Number(this.sql('SELECT last_insert_rowid() AS id')[0].id);
    if (state === 'held') this.sql('INSERT INTO holds (msg_id, ts) VALUES (?, ?)', id, now);
    this.recordSend(conn.uid, now, usage);
    const st = this.today(now);
    st.msgs += 1;
    const minute = roomMinute({ start: st.minuteStart, count: st.minuteCount }, now);
    st.minuteStart = minute.start; st.minuteCount = minute.count;
    const budget = roomBudget(st.msgs);
    const alertBudget = budget.alert && !budget.exhausted && !st.budgetAlerted;
    if (alertBudget) st.budgetAlerted = true;
    this.saveStats();
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    // The writes above are synchronous; the DO output gate holds this send until they are durable (spec §3.4).
    ws.send(encode({ t: 'ack', nonce: f.nonce, id, state, ...(holdCode ? { holdCode } : {}) }));
    if (state === 'visible') this.broadcast({ t: 'msg', m: this.toMessage(this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?', id)[0]) }, ws);

    if (minute.triggerSlow && this.mode(now).mode === 'open') {
      this.changeMode({ mode: 'slow', reason: 'auto_slow', until: now + LIMITS.autoSlowMs });
      this.alert('auto_slow');
    }
    if (budget.exhausted) this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget', until: now + msUntilUtcMidnight(now) });
    if (alertBudget) this.alert('budget_70');
    // The only await, after the write and the ack: the automated statement of reasons for a hold (DSA art. 17).
    if (state === 'held') await this.mod().recordDecision({ room: this.kv('slug') ?? '', msgId: id, action: 'hold', ground: `filter:${holdCode}`, automated: true });
  }

  /** Text under an open report is kept, hidden, until the report is decided (spec §6.2; GDPR art. 17(3)(b)/(e); review #16). */
  private preserveIfReported(id: number) {
    this.sql(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO evidence (msg_id, ts, uid, handle, body, saved_at)
      SELECT id, ts, uid, handle, body, ? FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND reported = 1 AND body != ''`, Date.now(), id);
  }
  private onDelete(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, id: number) {
    const row = this.sql("SELECT uid FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state IN ('visible','held')", id)[0];
    if (!row || !conn.uid || row.uid !== conn.uid) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'not_author' });
    this.ctx.storage.transactionSync(() => {
      this.preserveIfReported(id);
      this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'deleted' WHERE id = ?", id);
      this.sql('DELETE FROM holds WHERE msg_id = ?', id);
    });
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id });
  }

  // Until Task 14.4: reports are refused visibly, never swallowed.
  private onReport(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, _id: number, _category: string) {
    return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'unknown' });
  }

  // ── RPC used by ChatMod and /admin (Tasks 14-17) ──
  private closeWhere(uidHmac: string, frame: ServerFrame | null, code: number, reason: string): number {
    let n = 0;
    for (const ws of this.ctx.getWebSockets('auth')) {
      if ((ws.deserializeAttachment() as Conn).uidHmac !== uidHmac) continue;
      if (frame) ws.send(encode(frame));
      ws.close(code, reason); n += 1;
    }
    return n;
  }
  applyBan(uidHmac: string, until: number, ground: string) {
    this.sql('INSERT INTO bans (uid_hmac, until, ground) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(uid_hmac) DO UPDATE SET until = excluded.until, ground = excluded.ground', uidHmac, until, ground);
    this.closeWhere(uidHmac, { t: 'err', code: 'banned', until, ground }, CLOSE.banned, 'banned');   // statement of reasons with the ban (review #20)
  }
  revoke(uidHmac: string): number { return this.closeWhere(uidHmac, null, CLOSE.revoked, 'signed out'); }   // review #41
  setMode(mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number) { this.changeMode({ mode, reason, ...(until ? { until } : {}) }); }
  setGlobal(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number) { this.changeMode({ mode, ...(reason ? { reason } : {}), ...(until ? { until } : {}) }, 'global'); }
  termsChanged(list: TermList) {
    this.setKv('terms', JSON.stringify(list)); this.termsMem = list;
    const raw = this.kv('mode');
    if (raw && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).reason === 'filters_unavailable') this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' });
  }
  /** `preserve` (owner's choice in /admin, Q6) returns the text so ChatMod can keep it in decisions.facts; otherwise evidence is dropped. */
  moderate(msgId: number, action: 'publish' | 'remove', reason: string, preserve: boolean): { ok: boolean; facts?: string } {
    const row = this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state IN ('visible','held')", msgId)[0];
    if (!row) return { ok: false };
    let facts: string | undefined;
    this.ctx.storage.transactionSync(() => {
      this.sql('DELETE FROM holds WHERE msg_id = ?', msgId);
      if (action === 'publish') {
        this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'visible', hold_code = NULL, reported = 0 WHERE id = ?", msgId);
      } else {
        if (preserve) facts = JSON.stringify({ ts: row.ts, uid: row.uid, handle: row.handle, body: row.body });
        this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'removed', reason = ?, reported = 0 WHERE id = ?", reason, msgId);
      }
      this.sql('DELETE FROM evidence WHERE msg_id = ?', msgId);
    });
    if (action === 'publish') this.broadcast({ t: 'msg', m: this.toMessage(this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]) });
    else this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: msgId, reason });
    this.reopenIfGraveCleared();
    return { ok: true, ...(facts ? { facts } : {}) };
  }
  /** Report bookkeeping. A grave report (signed-in, or csam/terrorism/threat notice) holds the message at once, not the room (D13). */
  markReported(msgId: number, grave: boolean): { state: string | null; held: boolean } {
    const row = this.sql('SELECT state FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0];
    if (!row) return { state: null, held: false };
    this.sql('UPDATE messages SET reported = 1 WHERE id = ? AND reported = 0', msgId);
    if (!grave || row.state !== 'visible') return { state: String(row.state), held: false };
    const now = Date.now();
    this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'held', hold_code = 'grave_report' WHERE id = ?", msgId);
    this.sql('INSERT INTO holds (msg_id, ts, grave) VALUES (?, ?, 1) ON CONFLICT(msg_id) DO UPDATE SET grave = 1', msgId, now);
    this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: msgId });
    this.ensureAlarm(now + LIMITS.graveUnreviewedMs + 5_000);   // the volume check runs on time, not at the next hourly tick (review #8)
    return { state: 'held', held: true };
  }
  /** Called by ChatMod when every report on the message is closed without a remove (dismiss, moot). */
  clearReported(msgId: number, preserve: boolean): { facts?: string } {
    const ev = this.sql('SELECT * FROM evidence WHERE msg_id = ?', msgId)[0];
    // A dismissed report restores a message that reports (not filters) had hidden.
    const restored = this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'visible', hold_code = NULL WHERE id = ? AND state = 'held' AND hold_code IN ('grave_report','reports') RETURNING id", msgId).length > 0;
    this.sql('DELETE FROM evidence WHERE msg_id = ?', msgId);
    this.sql('UPDATE messages SET reported = 0 WHERE id = ?', msgId);
    if (restored) {
      this.sql('DELETE FROM holds WHERE msg_id = ?', msgId);
      this.broadcast({ t: 'msg', m: this.toMessage(this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]) });
      this.reopenIfGraveCleared();
    }
    return preserve && ev ? { facts: JSON.stringify({ ts: ev.ts, uid: ev.uid, handle: ev.handle, body: ev.body }) } : {};
  }
  hideForReports(msgId: number): boolean {
    const changed = this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'held', hold_code = 'reports' WHERE id = ? AND state = 'visible' RETURNING id", msgId).length;
    if (changed) { this.sql('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO holds (msg_id, ts) VALUES (?, ?)', msgId, Date.now()); this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: msgId }); }
    return changed > 0;
  }
  messageState(msgId: number): string | null { const r = this.sql('SELECT state FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]; return r ? String(r.state) : null; }
  eraseUid(uid: string): number {
    const ids = this.sql("SELECT id, state FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND (body != '' OR state IN ('visible','held'))", uid);
    this.ctx.storage.transactionSync(() => {
      for (const r of ids) this.preserveIfReported(Number(r.id));
      for (const r of ids) this.sql('DELETE FROM holds WHERE msg_id = ?', Number(r.id));
      // uid is blanked too: 'gh:<id>' maps to a public GitHub profile and decisions reference room + msg_id only (review #25).
      this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'deleted', handle = '', uid = '' WHERE uid = ?", uid);
      this.sql('DELETE FROM usage WHERE uid = ?', uid);
    });
    const left = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE uid = ?', uid)[0].c);
    if (left !== 0) throw new Error(`erasure left ${left} rows`);   // never report a count we did not achieve
    for (const r of ids) if (r.state === 'visible') this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: Number(r.id) });
    return ids.length;
  }
  exportUid(uid: string): ChatMessage[] {
    return this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND body != '' ORDER BY id", uid).map((r) => this.toMessage(r, uid));
  }
  /** Cheap by construction: no count(*) over messages (review #2). `held` counts the small holds table. */
  stats() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const t = this.today(now);
    const oldest = this.sql('SELECT ts FROM messages WHERE reported = 0 ORDER BY id LIMIT 1')[0];   // retention's own measure (review #40)
    return { day: t.day, msgs: t.msgs, conns: t.conns, sockets: this.ctx.getWebSockets().length,
      anonSockets: this.ctx.getWebSockets('anon').length,
      held: Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM holds')[0].c),
      graveHeld: Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM holds WHERE grave = 1')[0].c),
      undeliveredAlerts: t.undelivered, lastAlarmAt: t.lastAlarmAt,
      oldestMessageAgeMs: oldest ? now - Number(oldest.ts) : null,
      syncFailed: this.kv('sync_failed') !== null, mode: this.mode(now) };
  }
  context(msgId: number, around = 5): ChatMessage[] {
    return this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY id', msgId - around, msgId + around).map((r) => this.toMessage(r));
  }
  /** Schedule the alarm no later than `at` (default: in one hour). Task 15 adds alarm(); this lives here because markReported needs it. */
  protected ensureAlarm(at?: number) {
    const want = Math.max(Date.now() + 1_000, at ?? Date.now() + 3_600_000);
    this.ctx.waitUntil(this.ctx.storage.getAlarm().then((cur) => {
      if (cur === null || want < cur) return this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(want);
    }));
  }
  /** Room-level readonly is for volume only (D13): reopen when fewer than graveHeldForReadonly grave holds remain. */
  protected reopenIfGraveCleared() {
    const raw = this.kv('mode');
    if (!raw || (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).reason !== 'unreviewed_report') return;
    const n = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM holds WHERE grave = 1')[0].c);
    if (n < LIMITS.graveHeldForReadonly) this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' });
  }
}
```

(`eraseUid` blanks `handle` and `uid`, because both are personal data; the row stays only as an id for decisions that reference it. Text under an open report survives in `evidence` until the report is decided — the documented exception of ADR-0003 §11.)

Also add to 13.2's `room.test.ts`:

```ts
  it('author delete during an open report keeps the text as evidence, hidden (review #16)', async () => {
    const a = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:114', 'gh/ned'));
    await a.next('hello');
    a.send({ t: 'send', body: 'text someone reported', nonce: nonce() });
    const { id } = await a.next('ack');
    await room().markReported(id, false);
    a.send({ t: 'delete', id });
    await waitFor(() => (a.of('remove').length ? true : undefined));
    expect(await rows('SELECT state, body FROM messages WHERE id = ?', id)).toEqual([{ state: 'deleted', body: '' }]);
    expect(await rows('SELECT body FROM evidence WHERE msg_id = ?', id)).toEqual([{ body: 'text someone reported' }]);
  });
  it('erasure leaves no row carrying the uid (review #25)', async () => {
    await runInDurableObject(room(), (_i, st) => {
      st.storage.sql.exec("INSERT INTO messages (ts, uid, handle, body, body_hash, state, nonce) VALUES (?, 'gh:115', 'gh/oz', 'x', 'h', 'visible', ?)", Date.now(), nonce());
    });
    expect(await room().eraseUid('gh:115')).toBe(1);
    expect(await rows("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE uid = 'gh:115' OR handle = 'gh/oz'")).toEqual([{ c: 0 }]);
```

- [ ] **13.7 Alerts module** (needed by the room; Task 15 adds the triggers that live outside the room). Create `worker/chat/alerts.ts` and `worker/chat/alerts.test.ts`:

```ts
// alerts.ts — content-free by construction: the context type has no field that could carry message text.
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';
export type AlertCode = 'report_grave' | 'queue_stale' | 'auto_slow' | 'budget_70' | 'filters_unavailable' | 'global_mode' | 'unreviewed_readonly';
export interface AlertContext { room?: string; category?: 'illegal' | 'personal_data' | 'csam' | 'terrorism' | 'threat'; count?: number; mode?: string }
const TEXT: Record<AlertCode, (c: AlertContext) => string> = {
  // Batched by ChatMod: at most one per room per LIMITS.graveAlertEveryMs, with the count since the last one (review #18).
  // Terrorism is flagged URGENT: a TCO removal order must be executed within one hour (spec §6.2 bis).
  report_grave: (c) => `Chat: ${c.category === 'terrorism' ? 'URGENT ' : ''}${c.count ?? 1} grave report(s) (${c.category}) in ${c.room}`,
  queue_stale: (c) => `Chat: ${c.count ?? 1} item(s) waiting > 2 h`,
  auto_slow: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} switched to slow mode automatically (40 msg/min)`,
  budget_70: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} passed 70 % of today's budget`,
  filters_unavailable: (c) => `Chat: filters unavailable, ${c.room} is read-only`,
  global_mode: (c) => `Chat: global mode is now ${c.mode}`,
  unreviewed_readonly: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} went read-only — ${c.count ?? 3} grave-held messages waited > 60 min`,
};
/** Liveness of the Worker's OWN copy of the bot token and chat id (review #35): getMe + getChat, no message sent. */
export async function probeTelegram(env: ChatEnv): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || !env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID) return false;
  try {
    const base = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}`;
    const me = await fetch(`${base}/getMe`);
    const chat = await fetch(`${base}/getChat?chat_id=${encodeURIComponent(env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID)}`);
    return me.ok && chat.ok;
  } catch { return false; }
}
export const ADMIN_URL = 'https://chat.watchboard.dev/admin';
export function alertText(code: AlertCode, c: AlertContext): string { return `${TEXT[code](c)} — ${ADMIN_URL}`; }
export async function sendAlert(env: ChatEnv, code: AlertCode, c: AlertContext): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || !env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID) { console.error(`chat alert undelivered (no ops chat configured): ${code}`); return false; }
  const body = new URLSearchParams({ chat_id: env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID, text: alertText(code, c) });
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}/sendMessage`, { method: 'POST', body });
    return res.ok;
  } catch { return false; }
}
```

```ts
import { alertText, sendAlert } from './alerts';

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());
describe('alerts', () => {
  it('every alert links to /admin', () => expect(alertText('auto_slow', { room: 'cdmx' })).toContain('https://chat.watchboard.dev/admin'));
  it('posts to the ops chat id, never another', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(new Response('{"ok":true}'));
    expect(await sendAlert({ TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: 't', TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID: '-100ops' } as never, 'budget_70', { room: 'cdmx' })).toBe(true);
    const sent = spy.mock.calls[0][1]?.body as URLSearchParams;
    expect(sent.get('chat_id')).toBe('-100ops');
  });
  it('reports undelivered instead of pretending', async () => {
    expect(await sendAlert({} as never, 'budget_70', { room: 'cdmx' })).toBe(false);
  });
  it('the probe fails when Telegram rejects the token or the chat id', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockImplementation(async (u) => new Response('{}', { status: String(u).includes('getChat') ? 400 : 200 }));
    expect(await probeTelegram({ TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: 't', TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID: '-100ops' } as never)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
  /** facts (preserved illegal content, spec §6.2) carry their own expiry: facts_until = now + RETENTION.modRecordsMs. */
  recordDecision(d: { room: string; msgId: number | null; action: string; ground: string; automated: boolean; facts?: string }): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.ctx.storage.sql.exec('INSERT INTO decisions (ts, room, msg_id, action, ground, automated, facts, facts_until) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      now, d.room, d.msgId, d.action, d.ground, d.automated ? 1 : 0, d.facts ?? null, d.facts ? now + RETENTION.modRecordsMs : null);

  }
```

```ts
// 'moot' = "content no longer available" (deleted by its author, erased, expired): closes the reports WITHOUT counting a dismissal (review #16)

setRoomMode(slug: string, mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number): Promise<void>;
```

Reports and notices (D13, review #15/#18): a report from a signed-in reader of category `illegal` or `personal_data`, and a `/notice` of kind `csam`, `terrorism` or `threat`, are **grave**: the message is held at once (`markReported(msgId, true)`), subject to limits — a signed-in reporter's grave reports count against `reportsPerHour`, and anonymous notices may auto-hold at most `LIMITS.anonGraveHoldsPerRoomHour` messages per room per hour (beyond that they are queued without hiding and the alert says so). Every other report or notice is only queued (`markReported(msgId, false)`). A report on a message that does not exist is refused and stores nothing. Grave alerts are batched per room (`graveAlertEveryMs`), and repeated reports on the same message never re-alert.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';

import { resetGlobalCacheForTests } from '../chat/router';

  const c = await connect(slug, await cookieFor(uid, handle));
  await c.next('hello');
  c.send({ t: 'send', body, nonce: nonce() });
  return { c, id: (await c.next('ack')).id };
}

    await runInDurableObject(mod(), (_i, s) => { s.storage.sql.exec('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO users (uid_hmac, gen, dismissed_reports) VALUES (?, 0, 3)', noisy); });
    expect(await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', source: { kind: 'reader', reporterUidHmac: noisy } })).toMatchObject({ hide: false });
    expect(await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', source: await reader('gh:221') })).toMatchObject({ hide: false });
    expect(await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', source: await reader('gh:222') })).toMatchObject({ hide: false });

      .toEqual({ error: 'no_such_message' });

    expect(d).toEqual({ action: 'remove', ground: 'rules §3 personal data of third parties', automated: 0 });

    const c = await connect(); await c.next('hello');

    expect(await c.next('mode')).toMatchObject({ mode: 'closed', reason: 'global' });
    resetGlobalCacheForTests();
    const again = await connect();
    expect(await again.next('mode')).toMatchObject({ mode: 'closed' });

    await evictDurableObject(room('t-mod'));

  });

  it('erasure and bans reach a room that is no longer in CHAT_ROOMS (review #7)', async () => {
    await runInDurableObject(room('gone'), (_i, s) => {
      s.storage.sql.exec("INSERT INTO messages (ts, uid, handle, body, body_hash, state, nonce) VALUES (?, 'gh:239', 'gh/old', 'old text', 'h', 'visible', ?)", Date.now(), nonce());
    });
    await mod().syncState('gone', -1);                  // how a room registers itself
    const res = await mod().erase('gh:239', await h('gh:239'));
    expect(res.perRoom.gone).toBe(1);
```
