- [ ] **14.3 Implement ChatMod** (add to the class from 13.3, which already has `rooms()`, `room()`, `exec()`, `fanOut()`; import `LIMITS`, `RETENTION`, `sendAlert`, `ReportCategory`, `ChatMessage`, `NoticeJson`):

```ts
  private day() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  /** ChatMod's alerts are counted when undelivered, like the rooms' (review #35). */
  private alert(code: AlertCode, c: AlertContext) {
    this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, code, c).then((ok) => {
      if (!ok) this.put(`alert_undelivered:${this.day()}`, String(Number(this.get(`alert_undelivered:${this.day()}`) ?? 0) + 1));
    }));
  }
  /** One grave alert per room per window with a count; a second report on the same message never re-alerts (review #18). */
  private queueGraveAlert(room: string, category: AlertContext['category']) {
    const k = `gravealert:${room}`;
    const s = JSON.parse(this.get(k) ?? '{"last":0,"pending":0}') as { last: number; pending: number; category?: string };
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - s.last >= LIMITS.graveAlertEveryMs) {
      this.put(k, JSON.stringify({ last: now, pending: 0 }));
      this.alert('report_grave', { room, category, count: s.pending + 1 });
    } else {
      this.put(k, JSON.stringify({ ...s, pending: s.pending + 1, category }));
      this.ensureAlarm(s.last + LIMITS.graveAlertEveryMs);   // the mod alarm flushes pending counts (Task 15.3)
    }
  }

  async report(r: { room: string; msgId: number; category: ReportCategory; source: ReportSource }) {
    const now = Date.now();
    const notice = r.source.kind === 'notice' ? r.source.notice : null;
    const reporter = r.source.kind === 'reader' ? r.source.reporterUidHmac : null;
    const grave = notice ? ['csam', 'terrorism', 'threat'].includes(notice.kind) : r.category === 'illegal' || r.category === 'personal_data';
    let hold = grave;
    if (grave && notice) {                     // anonymous holds are capped so notices cannot blank a room (D13)
      const k = `anonhold:${r.room}:${new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 13)}`;
      const n = Number(this.get(k) ?? 0);
      if (n >= LIMITS.anonGraveHoldsPerRoomHour) hold = false; else this.put(k, String(n + 1));
    }
    const marked = await this.room(r.room).markReported(r.msgId, hold);
    if (marked.state === null) return { error: 'no_such_message' as const };      // nothing stored, nothing alerted
    const already = Number(this.exec('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ?', r.room, r.msgId)[0].c) > 0;
    this.exec('INSERT INTO reports (room, msg_id, ts, category, reporter_uid_hmac, notice_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      r.room, r.msgId, now, r.category, reporter, notice ? JSON.stringify(notice) : null);
    const id = Number(this.exec('SELECT last_insert_rowid() AS id')[0].id);
    if ((grave || r.category === 'illegal') && !already)
      this.queueGraveAlert(r.room, (notice && grave ? notice.kind : r.category) as AlertContext['category']);
    // Only identified reporters with a clean record count toward the 3-reporter provisional hide (spec §4.3).
    const trusted = Number(this.exec(
      `SELECT count(DISTINCT r.reporter_uid_hmac) AS c FROM reports r LEFT JOIN users u ON u.uid_hmac = r.reporter_uid_hmac
       WHERE r.room = ? AND r.msg_id = ? AND r.status = 'open' AND r.reporter_uid_hmac IS NOT NULL
         AND COALESCE(u.dismissed_reports, 0) < 3`, r.room, r.msgId)[0].c);
    this.ensureAlarm();
    return { id, hide: trusted >= 3, held: marked.held };
  }

  async decide(d: { room: string; msgId: number; action: 'publish' | 'remove' | 'dismiss' | 'moot'; ground: string; preserve?: boolean;
    ban?: { uidHmac: string; days: 1 | 7 | 365 } }) {
    let facts: string | undefined;
    if (d.action === 'publish' || d.action === 'remove') {
      const res = await this.room(d.room).moderate(d.msgId, d.action, d.ground, !!d.preserve);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`message ${d.room}#${d.msgId} is no longer available: decide "moot" instead`);
      facts = res.facts;
    } else {
      if (d.action === 'dismiss') {            // only a real dismissal counts against reporters; "moot" never does
        this.exec(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (uid_hmac) SELECT DISTINCT reporter_uid_hmac FROM reports
          WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open' AND reporter_uid_hmac IS NOT NULL`, d.room, d.msgId);
        this.exec(`UPDATE users SET dismissed_reports = dismissed_reports + 1 WHERE uid_hmac IN
          (SELECT reporter_uid_hmac FROM reports WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open')`, d.room, d.msgId);
      }
      facts = (await this.room(d.room).clearReported(d.msgId, !!d.preserve)).facts;
    }
    const decisionId = this.recordDecision({ room: d.room, msgId: d.msgId, action: d.action, ground: d.ground, automated: false, ...(facts ? { facts } : {}) });
    const status = d.action === 'dismiss' ? 'dismissed' : d.action === 'moot' ? 'moot' : 'resolved';
    this.exec("UPDATE reports SET status = ? WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open'", status, d.room, d.msgId);
    if (d.ban) await this.ban(d.ban.uidHmac, d.ban.days, d.ground, d.room, d.msgId, decisionId);
    return { decisionId };
  }

  /** A ban does not revoke the session: the banned reader keeps reading and sees the ground and the end date (review #20). */
  async ban(uidHmac: string, days: number, ground: string, room: string, msgId: number | null, decisionId?: number) {
    const until = Date.now() + Math.min(days * 86_400_000, RETENTION.maxBanMs);
    this.exec('INSERT INTO bans (uid_hmac, until, ground, decision_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(uid_hmac) DO UPDATE SET until = excluded.until, ground = excluded.ground, decision_id = excluded.decision_id',
      uidHmac, until, ground, decisionId ?? this.recordDecision({ room, msgId, action: 'ban', ground, automated: false }));
    this.put('bans_version', String(Number(this.get('bans_version') ?? 0) + 1));   // before the fan-out: missed rooms converge on sync
    await this.fanOut((s) => this.room(s).applyBan(uidHmac, until, ground));
    this.ensureAlarm();
    return until;
  }

  async setGlobalMode(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number) {
    this.put('global_mode', JSON.stringify({ mode, ...(reason ? { reason } : {}), ...(until ? { until } : {}) }));
    await this.fanOut((s) => this.room(s).setGlobal(mode, reason, until));
    this.alert('global_mode', { mode });
    this.ensureAlarm();
  }
  async setRoomMode(slug: string, mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number) {
    if (!this.rooms().includes(slug)) throw new Error(`room ${slug} not in CHAT_ROOMS`);
    await this.room(slug).setMode(mode, reason, until);
  }

  /** Every registry room, including rooms removed from CHAT_ROOMS (review #7). A room that does not answer is named, never skipped silently. */
  async erase(uid: string, uidHmac: string) {
    const perRoom: Record<string, number> = {};
    const unreachable: string[] = [];
    for (const s of this.rooms()) {
      try { perRoom[s] = await this.room(s).eraseUid(uid); } catch { unreachable.push(s); }
    }
    await this.bumpGen(uidHmac);               // also closes that user's open sockets
    return { erased: Object.values(perRoom).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), perRoom, unreachable };
  }
  async exportFor(uid: string) {
    const out: Record<string, ChatMessage[]> = {};
    for (const s of this.rooms()) out[s] = await this.room(s).exportUid(uid);
    return out;
  }
  openReports() {
    return this.exec(`SELECT min(id) AS id, room, msg_id AS msgId, min(ts) AS ts, group_concat(DISTINCT category) AS category,
      count(*) AS reporters FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' GROUP BY room, msg_id ORDER BY ts`) as unknown as
      Array<{ id: number; room: string; msgId: number; ts: number; category: string; reporters: number }>;
  }
  /** DSA art. 16(5): the notifier can look up the decision by reference, without an account (review #19). No content is returned. */
  noticeStatus(ref: number) {
    const r = this.exec('SELECT room, msg_id, ts, status FROM reports WHERE id = ? AND notice_json IS NOT NULL', ref)[0];
    if (!r) return null;
    const d = this.exec('SELECT ground, ts FROM decisions WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND automated = 0 AND ts >= ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1',
      r.room, r.msg_id, r.ts)[0];
    return { status: String(r.status) as 'open' | 'resolved' | 'dismissed' | 'moot', ...(d ? { ground: String(d.ground), decidedAt: Number(d.ts) } : {}) };
  }
  roomStats(slug: string, stats: unknown) {
    this.publicCache = null;
    this.exec('INSERT INTO room_stats (slug, at, json) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(slug) DO UPDATE SET at = excluded.at, json = excluded.json',
      slug, Date.now(), JSON.stringify(stats));
  }
  private undeliveredToday(): number { return Number(this.get(`alert_undelivered:${this.day()}`) ?? 0); }
  /** Live and expensive (fans out to every room): /admin/health only. */
  async health() {
    const t = this.getTerms();
    const oldest = this.exec("SELECT min(ts) AS ts FROM reports WHERE status = 'open'")[0].ts as number | null;
    const rooms: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const s of this.rooms()) { try { rooms[s] = await this.room(s).stats(); } catch { rooms[s] = { unreachable: true }; } }
    return { globalMode: this.getGlobalMode(), termsVersion: t?.version ?? 0,
      termsCount: t ? Object.values(t.terms).reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0) : 0,
      oldestOpenReportMs: oldest === null ? null : Date.now() - oldest, rooms, modUndeliveredAlerts: this.undeliveredToday(),
      fanoutFailedToday: Number(this.get(`fanout_failed:${this.day()}`) ?? 0) };
  }
  private publicCache: { at: number; value: ReturnType<ChatMod['computePublic']> } | null = null;
  /** Public /health: from the room_stats rows each room pushes hourly — no fan-out, cached 60 s (review #9, #30). */
  publicHealth() {
    if (!this.publicCache || Date.now() - this.publicCache.at > 60_000) this.publicCache = { at: Date.now(), value: this.computePublic() };
    return this.publicCache.value;
  }
  private computePublic() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const t = this.getTerms();
    const rows = this.exec('SELECT slug, at, json FROM room_stats').map((r) => ({ slug: String(r.slug), at: Number(r.at),
      s: JSON.parse(String(r.json)) as { day: string; msgs: number; undeliveredAlerts: number; lastAlarmAt: number; oldestMessageAgeMs: number | null; syncFailed: boolean } }));
    const today = rows.filter((r) => r.s.day === this.day());
    return {
      globalMode: this.getGlobalMode().mode, termsVersion: t?.version ?? 0,
      termsCount: t ? Object.values(t.terms).reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0) : 0,
      maxBudgetRatio: Math.max(0, ...today.map((r) => r.s.msgs / LIMITS.roomMsgsPerDay)),
      undeliveredAlerts: this.undeliveredToday() + today.reduce((n, r) => n + r.s.undeliveredAlerts, 0),
      alertsOk: this.get('alerts_ok') === '1',                       // Worker-side Telegram probe, hourly (Task 15.3)
      keyOk: this.get('key_mismatch') === null,
      rooms: Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.slug, { statsAgeMs: now - r.at, lastAlarmAgeMs: r.s.lastAlarmAt ? now - r.s.lastAlarmAt : null,
        oldestMessageAgeMs: r.s.oldestMessageAgeMs, syncFailed: r.s.syncFailed }])),
    };
  }
```

In `checkKeyFingerprint` (13.3), on a mismatch also `this.put('key_mismatch', String(Date.now()))` before returning `false`, so `/health` reports `keyOk: false`. `setTerms` already fans out through `fanOut` (13.3). Add ChatMod's `protected ensureAlarm(at?: number)` now, with the same body as the room's (13.6: schedule no later than `at`, default one hour); Task 15.3 adds `alarm()`. In `worker/test/helpers.ts`, `resetChat()` now gains its first line `await mod().setGlobalMode('open');` (13.1).

```ts
  private async onReport(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, id: number, category: ReportCategory) {
    if (!conn.uid || !conn.uidHmac) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'auth_required' });
    const now = Date.now();
    // The per-identity report limit is checked and written before the await (same rule as onSend).
    const u = this.sql('SELECT rep_hour, rep_n FROM usage WHERE uid = ?', conn.uid)[0];
    const n = u && u.rep_hour === utcHour(now) ? Number(u.rep_n) : 0;
    if (!checkReport(n)) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'report_limited' });
    if (!this.sql("SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state = 'visible'", id).length) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'unknown' });
    this.sql(`INSERT INTO usage (uid, rep_hour, rep_n) VALUES (?, ?, 1) ON CONFLICT(uid) DO UPDATE SET rep_hour = excluded.rep_hour,
      rep_n = CASE WHEN usage.rep_hour = excluded.rep_hour THEN usage.rep_n + 1 ELSE 1 END`, conn.uid, utcHour(now));
    const res = await this.mod().report({ room: this.kv('slug') ?? '', msgId: id, category, source: { kind: 'reader', reporterUidHmac: conn.uidHmac } });
    if ('error' in res) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'unknown' });
    if (res.hide) this.hideForReports(id);
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    ws.send(encode({ t: 'reported', id, ref: res.id }));
  }
  heldList() { return this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE state = 'held' ORDER BY id").map((r) => ({ ...this.toMessage(r), uid: String(r.uid) })); }
  authorOf(msgId: number): string | null { const r = this.sql('SELECT uid FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]; return r ? String(r.uid) : null; }
```

### Task 15: Alarms — retention, mode expiry, stale queue, unattended degradation

Gate: none except step 15.5 (`BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q5`). Spec §3.2 (single alarm), §3.8, §4.4-4.5. Branch `feat/chat-backend`.

**Files:** Modify `worker/chat/room.ts`, `worker/chat/mod.ts`; create `worker/test/alarm.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// room.ts
async alarm(): Promise<void>;   // 1) retention (no RPC before it), 2) mode expiry, 3) volume degradation, 4) sync + stats push + decisions
                                // for expired holds (each RPC in try/catch), 5) ALWAYS reschedules in `finally` (review #40)
// ensureAlarm(at?) already exists (13.6); it is called after every insert, every mode change with `until`, and by markReported
// mod.ts
async alarm(): Promise<void>;   // retention of reports/decisions/facts/bans/day keys, stale-queue alert, pending grave-alert flush,
                                // Telegram probe → settings 'alerts_ok'; runs EVERY hour, even idle (review #35)
```

The room no longer asks ChatMod for "the oldest open grave report" (the old `graveOpenSince`): after D13 the degradation input is the room's own `holds` table (grave holds older than 60 min), so the alarm's first steps never depend on another object.

- [ ] **15.2 Room alarm.** In `room.ts` add, and call `this.ensureAlarm()` in `onSend` right after the INSERT (before the final await), and `this.ensureAlarm(m.until)` inside `changeMode` when the new mode has `until`:

```ts
  async alarm() {
    const now = Date.now();
    const expired: Array<{ id: number; code: string }> = [];
    try {
      // 1. Retention FIRST and without any RPC: a ChatMod outage must never stop deletion (review #40).
      this.ctx.storage.transactionSync(() => {
        // ids grow with ts, so "everything below the first recent id" is the expired range; reads ≈ rows deleted (review #2).
        const first = this.sql('SELECT id FROM messages WHERE ts >= ? ORDER BY id LIMIT 1', now - RETENTION.visibleMs)[0];
        this.sql('DELETE FROM messages WHERE id < ? AND reported = 0', first ? Number(first.id) : Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
        for (const h of this.sql('SELECT h.msg_id AS id, m.hold_code AS code, m.reported AS rep FROM holds h JOIN messages m ON m.id = h.msg_id WHERE h.ts < ? AND h.grave = 0', now - RETENTION.heldMs)) {
          if (Number(h.rep) === 1) continue;     // never expire a message under an open report (review #16)
          // A stub with its automated statement of reasons stays for the rest of the 30 days, visible to its author (review #20).
          this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'removed', reason = ? WHERE id = ? AND state = 'held'",
            `Not published: automated filter "${String(h.code)}" (automated decision)`, Number(h.id));
          this.sql('DELETE FROM holds WHERE msg_id = ?', Number(h.id));
          expired.push({ id: Number(h.id), code: String(h.code) });
        }
        this.sql("DELETE FROM room WHERE k LIKE 'stats:%' AND k < ?", `stats:${utcDay(now - 2 * 86_400_000)}`);
        this.sql('DELETE FROM usage WHERE day < ? AND rep_hour < ?', utcDay(now - 86_400_000), utcHour(now - 86_400_000));
        this.sql('DELETE FROM bans WHERE until < ?', now);
      });
    } catch (e) { console.error('room alarm: retention failed', e); }
    try {
      // 2. Mode expiry. 3. Room-level readonly only on volume (D13).
      for (const key of ['mode', 'global'] as const) {
        const raw = this.kv(key);
        if (raw && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).until && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).until! <= now) this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' }, key);
      }
      const stale = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM holds WHERE grave = 1 AND ts < ?', now - LIMITS.graveUnreviewedMs)[0].c);
      const cur = this.mode(now);
      if (stale >= LIMITS.graveHeldForReadonly && cur.mode !== 'readonly' && cur.mode !== 'closed') {
        this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'unreviewed_report' });
        this.alert('unreviewed_readonly', { count: stale });
      }
      const t = this.today(now); t.lastAlarmAt = now; this.saveStats();
    } catch (e) { console.error('room alarm: modes failed', e); }
    try {
      // 4. Everything that needs ChatMod, each failure isolated.
      await this.sync(now, true);
      await this.mod().roomStats(this.kv('slug') ?? '', this.stats());
      for (const x of expired) await this.mod().recordDecision({ room: this.kv('slug') ?? '', msgId: x.id, action: 'hold_expired', ground: `filter:${x.code}`, automated: true });
    } catch (e) { console.error('room alarm: ChatMod unreachable', e); }
    finally {
      // 5. Always reschedule while anything could need it; an idle, empty room sleeps for free.
      const anything = this.sql('SELECT 1 FROM messages LIMIT 1').length > 0 || this.sql('SELECT 1 FROM holds LIMIT 1').length > 0;
      const until = this.mode(now).until;
      if (anything || until || this.ctx.getWebSockets().length > 0)
        await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Math.min(now + 3_600_000, until ?? Infinity));
    }
  }
```

(import `RETENTION` from `./limits`). The old `clearUnreviewed()` is replaced by `reopenIfGraveCleared()` (13.6), which `moderate` and `clearReported` already call.

- [ ] **15.3 Mod alarm.** In `mod.ts` add (`ensureAlarm` exists since 14.3; `setTerms` also calls it):

```ts
  async alarm() {
    const now = Date.now();
    try {
      const hour = (t: number) => new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 13);
      this.exec('DELETE FROM reports WHERE ts < ?', now - RETENTION.modRecordsMs);
      this.exec('DELETE FROM decisions WHERE ts < ?', now - RETENTION.modRecordsMs);
      this.exec('UPDATE decisions SET facts = NULL WHERE facts_until < ?', now);          // preserved content has its own expiry
      this.exec('DELETE FROM bans WHERE until < ?', now);
      for (const p of ['auth:', 'alert_undelivered:', 'fanout_failed:'])
        this.exec('DELETE FROM settings WHERE k LIKE ? AND k < ?', `${p}%`, `${p}${utcDay(now - 7 * 86_400_000)}`);
      this.exec("DELETE FROM settings WHERE (k LIKE 'notice:%' OR k LIKE 'noticemsg:%' OR k LIKE 'anonhold:%') AND substr(k, -13) < ?", hour(now - 86_400_000));
      // Stale queue: at most one alert per newly stale report.
      const cutoff = now - 2 * 3_600_000;
      const lastCutoff = Number(this.get('stale_alerted_upto') ?? 0);
      const count = (q: string, ...b: unknown[]) => Number(this.exec(q, ...b)[0].c);
      if (count("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' AND ts < ? AND ts >= ?", cutoff, lastCutoff) > 0) {
        this.put('stale_alerted_upto', String(cutoff));
        this.alert('queue_stale', { count: count("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' AND ts < ?", cutoff) });
      }
      // Flush batched grave alerts whose window has passed (14.3 queueGraveAlert).
      for (const r of this.exec("SELECT k, v FROM settings WHERE k LIKE 'gravealert:%'")) {
        const s = JSON.parse(String(r.v)) as { last: number; pending: number; category?: AlertContext['category'] };
        if (s.pending > 0 && now - s.last >= LIMITS.graveAlertEveryMs) {
          this.put(String(r.k), JSON.stringify({ last: now, pending: 0 }));
          this.alert('report_grave', { room: String(r.k).slice('gravealert:'.length), category: s.category, count: s.pending });
        }
      }
      // The Worker's own Telegram credentials, checked without sending a message (review #35).
      this.put('alerts_ok', (await probeTelegram(this.env)) ? '1' : '0');
      this.publicCache = null;
    } catch (e) { console.error('mod alarm failed', e); }
    finally {
      await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Math.min(now + 3_600_000, this.nextGraveFlush() ?? Infinity));   // always: the probe must keep running
    }
  }
  private nextGraveFlush(): number | null {
    const due = this.exec("SELECT v FROM settings WHERE k LIKE 'gravealert:%'").map((r) => JSON.parse(String(r.v)) as { last: number; pending: number })
      .filter((s) => s.pending > 0).map((s) => s.last + LIMITS.graveAlertEveryMs);
    return due.length ? Math.max(Date.now() + 1_000, Math.min(...due)) : null;
  }
```

(import `probeTelegram`, `AlertContext`, `utcDay` and `LIMITS`.) The stale alert repeats at most once per new stale report. ChatMod's alarm runs every hour even with nothing open — 24 DO requests a day — because the Telegram probe is the only check of the Worker's copy of the token. Room reopening after a decision is done by the room itself (`reopenIfGraveCleared`, 13.6).

```ts
// oauth.ts
export interface OAuthDeps { fetch: typeof fetch; now: () => number; random: (n: number) => Uint8Array }
export const defaultDeps: OAuthDeps;
export async function authStart(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps?: OAuthDeps): Promise<Response>;     // 302 to github.com + __Host-wb_oauth cookie
export async function authCallback(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps?: OAuthDeps): Promise<Response>;  // 302 back to watchboard.dev with __Host-wb_chat, or an HTML refusal page
export function safeReturnPath(raw: string | null): string;   // '/' unless it starts with a single '/' and matches /^\/[a-z0-9\/_-]*$/i
// me.ts (CORS: Allow-Origin = request Origin if allowed, Allow-Credentials: true)
export async function logout(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response>;   // always revokes every token + open socket of the user (D14)
export async function eraseMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response>;   // POST, Origin required; { erased, perRoom }
export async function exportMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response>;  // GET; application/json attachment
// pages.ts
export function page(title: string, bodyHtml: string, status?: number): Response;  // worker-served HTML, CSP default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'
// mod.ts
countAuth(kind: 'done' | 'refused_age' | 'refused_reserved' | 'failed'): void;   // per UTC day in settings; read by health().
// No 'start' count: /auth/github/start is unauthenticated, and a storage write per hit would let anyone spend the
// account's rows-written quota (review #9). Starts are inferred as done + refused + failed; abandoned sign-ins are
// not counted, and the pilot log says so.
```

```ts
export const esc = (s: string) =>
  s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]!);
const CSP = "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'";
/** Worker-served HTML. `bodyHtml` must already be escaped; callers build it with esc(). */
export function page(title: string, bodyHtml: string, status = 200): Response {
  const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>${esc(title)}</title><style>body{font:16px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:40rem;margin:2rem auto;padding:0 16px}
button,input,select,textarea{font:inherit;min-height:44px}</style></head><body>${bodyHtml}</body></html>`;
  return new Response(html, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'Content-Security-Policy': CSP, 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff', 'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer' } });
}
```

- [ ] **16.4 OAuth implementation.** `worker/chat/oauth.ts` (below the facts comment from 16.1):

```ts
import { b64url, fromB64url, requireSecret, sign, verifySig } from './crypto';
import { accountOldEnough, isReservedHandle, minAccountAgeDays, readCookie, sessionCookie, signSession, SESSION_TTL_S, uidHmac } from './session';
import { parseOrigins, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { modStub } from './stubs';

export interface OAuthDeps { fetch: typeof fetch; now: () => number; random: (n: number) => Uint8Array }
export const defaultDeps: OAuthDeps = { fetch: (i, init) => fetch(i, init), now: () => Date.now(), random: (n) => crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(n)) };
const OAUTH_COOKIE = '__Host-wb_oauth';
const clearOauth = `${OAUTH_COOKIE}=; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=0`;
const siteOrigin = (env: ChatEnv) => [...parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS)][0] ?? 'https://watchboard.dev';

export function safeReturnPath(raw: string | null): string {
  return raw && raw.startsWith('/') && !raw.startsWith('//') && /^\/[a-z0-9\/_-]*$/i.test(raw) ? raw : '/';
}
async function challenge(verifier: string) {
  return b64url(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier)));
}

export async function authStart(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps: OAuthDeps = defaultDeps): Promise<Response> {
  const key = requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY');
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const state = b64url(deps.random(16));
  const verifier = b64url(deps.random(32));
  const data = b64url(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ state, verifier, ret: safeReturnPath(url.searchParams.get('return')), exp: deps.now() + 600_000 })));
  const gh = new URL('https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize');
  gh.search = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID ?? '', redirect_uri: `https://${url.host}/auth/github/callback`,
    scope: 'read:user', state, code_challenge: await challenge(verifier), code_challenge_method: 'S256', allow_signup: 'false' }).toString();
  return new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { Location: gh.toString(), 'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'Set-Cookie': `${OAUTH_COOKIE}=${data}.${await sign(key, data)}; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=600` } });
}

function refuse(status: number, title: string, text: string, env: ChatEnv): Response {
  const res = page(title, `<h1>${esc(title)}</h1><p>${esc(text)}</p><p><a href="${esc(siteOrigin(env))}/">Back to Watchboard</a></p>`, status);
  res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', clearOauth);
  return res;
}

export async function authCallback(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps: OAuthDeps = defaultDeps): Promise<Response> {
  const key = requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY');
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const raw = readCookie(req, OAUTH_COOKIE);
  const [data, sig] = raw?.split('.') ?? [];
  if (!data || !sig || !(await verifySig(key, data, sig))) return refuse(400, 'Sign-in expired', 'Please start again from the chat panel.', env);
  const st = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(fromB64url(data))) as { state: string; verifier: string; ret: string; exp: number };
  if (st.exp < deps.now() || url.searchParams.get('state') !== st.state || !url.searchParams.get('code'))
    return refuse(400, 'Sign-in expired', 'Please start again from the chat panel.', env);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  try {
    const tok = await deps.fetch('https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token', { method: 'POST',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: new URLSearchParams({ client_id: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID ?? '', client_secret: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET ?? '',
        code: url.searchParams.get('code')!, redirect_uri: `https://${url.host}/auth/github/callback`, code_verifier: st.verifier }).toString() });
    const { access_token } = (await tok.json()) as { access_token?: string };
    if (!access_token) throw new Error('no access_token');
    const u = await deps.fetch('https://api.github.com/user', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${access_token}`,
      Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json', 'User-Agent': 'watchboard-chat' } });
    if (!u.ok) throw new Error(`github user ${u.status}`);
    // The GitHub token is dropped here: only id, login and created_at are kept (spec §3.6).
    const { id, login, created_at } = (await u.json()) as { id: number; login: string; created_at: string };
    const minDays = minAccountAgeDays(env);
    if (isReservedHandle(login)) { await mod.countAuth('refused_reserved'); return refuse(403, 'Handle not available', 'This GitHub login is reserved in the Watchboard chat. You can still read.', env); }
    if (!accountOldEnough(created_at, deps.now(), minDays)) {
      await mod.countAuth('refused_age');
      return refuse(403, 'Account too new', `To write in the chat your GitHub account must be at least ${minDays} days old. You can still read.`, env);
    }
    const uid = `gh:${id}`;
    const { gen } = await mod.getUserState(await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid));
    const iat = Math.floor(deps.now() / 1000);
    const token = await signSession({ uid, handle: `gh/${login}`, iat, exp: iat + SESSION_TTL_S, gen }, key);
    await mod.countAuth('done');
    const res = new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { Location: `${siteOrigin(env)}${st.ret}#community`, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
    res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', sessionCookie(token));
    res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', clearOauth);
    return res;
  } catch {
    await mod.countAuth('failed');
    return refuse(502, 'GitHub sign-in failed', 'GitHub did not answer as expected. Please try again later.', env);
  }
}
```

In `mod.ts` add (and put `auth: this.authCounts()` in the object `health()` returns):

```ts
  countAuth(kind: 'done' | 'refused_age' | 'refused_reserved' | 'failed') {
    const k = `auth:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}:${kind}`;
    this.exec("INSERT INTO settings (k, v) VALUES (?, '1') ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET v = CAST(v AS INTEGER) + 1", k);
  }
  authCounts(): Record<string, number> {
    const p = `auth:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}:`;
    return Object.fromEntries(this.exec('SELECT k, v FROM settings WHERE k LIKE ?', `${p}%`).map((r) => [String(r.k).slice(p.length), Number(r.v)]));
  }
```

```ts
import { parseOrigins, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { chatJson } from './http';
import { clearSessionCookie } from './session';
import { identityFromCookie } from './router';
import { modStub } from './stubs';

function cors(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Record<string, string> {
  const o = req.headers.get('Origin');
  return o && parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS).has(o)
    ? { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': o, 'Access-Control-Allow-Credentials': 'true', Vary: 'Origin' } : {};
}
const originOk = (req: Request, env: ChatEnv) => parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS).has(req.headers.get('Origin') ?? '');
export function preflight(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Response {
  return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: { ...cors(req, env), 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type', 'Access-Control-Max-Age': '600' } });
}
/** Every sign-out revokes every token of the user and closes their sockets (D14): a copied cookie must not outlive it. */
export async function logout(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  if (!originOk(req, env)) return chatJson({ error: 'forbidden_origin' }, 403);
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (id) await modStub(env).bumpGen(id.uidHmac);
  return chatJson({ ok: true, revoked: !!id }, 200, { ...cors(req, env), 'Set-Cookie': clearSessionCookie() });
}
export async function eraseMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  if (!originOk(req, env)) return chatJson({ error: 'forbidden_origin' }, 403);
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id) return chatJson({ error: 'auth_required' }, 401, cors(req, env));
  const res = await modStub(env).erase(id.uid, id.uidHmac);   // counts are re-read inside each room (Task 13.6)
  // A room that did not answer means the erasure is incomplete: say so (502) instead of reporting success (review #7).
  return chatJson(res, res.unreachable.length ? 502 : 200, { ...cors(req, env), 'Set-Cookie': clearSessionCookie() });
}
export async function exportMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id) return chatJson({ error: 'auth_required' }, 401, cors(req, env));
  const rooms = await modStub(env).exportFor(id.uid);
  return chatJson({ exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), handle: id.handle, rooms }, 200,
    { ...cors(req, env), 'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename="watchboard-chat-export.json"' });
}
```

```ts
  if (path === '/me/export' && request.method === 'GET') return exportMe(request, env);
```

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// limits.ts — closed list; the statement of reasons (DSA art. 17) is always one of these
export const GROUNDS = ['illegal content', 'rules §1 harassment or hate', 'rules §2 spam or flooding',
  'rules §3 personal data of others', 'rules §4 dangerous misinformation', 'rules §5 impersonation',
  'rules §6 not in English, Spanish, French or Portuguese', 'content no longer available'] as const;   // last one: action "moot"
// admin.ts
export async function handleAdmin(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, path: string): Promise<Response>;
// GET  /admin              queue: open reports + held messages of every room, oldest first, 5 messages of context each
// GET  /admin/health       JSON of ChatMod.health()
// POST /admin/decide       room, msgId, action=publish|remove|dismiss|moot, ground ∈ GROUNDS, ban=0|1|7|365, preserve=on (Q6: keep the text for authorities, 183 days)
// POST /admin/mode         scope=global|<slug>, mode, minutes (optional; 0 = no expiry)
// GET|POST /admin/terms    one textarea per locale, one term per line; the terms are never logged or alerted
// POST /admin/erase        login → GitHub numeric id (public API) → ChatMod.erase; shows the re-read count
// POST /admin/purge        room, confirm (must equal the slug) → ChatRoom.purge()
// room.ts
purge(): number;   // deletes every message row; returns the count re-read as deleted; broadcasts a 'mode' frame so open panels reload
```

Account age is **not** shown in the queue (spec §4.4 asks for it): it is checked once at login and never stored, which is the smaller data footprint. Recorded as D7 in ADR-0003.

```ts
const ADMIN_ORIGIN = { Origin: 'https://chat.watchboard.dev' };

  (exports as { default: Fetcher }).default.fetch(`https://chat.watchboard.dev${path}`, { redirect: 'manual', ...init });

    expect(row).toEqual({ state: 'removed', body: '', reason: 'rules §3 personal data of others' });

    expect(bans).toBe(1);

    expect(decisions).toBe(1);
  });

    const id = await seed('x');

    expect(await ok.text()).toMatch(/Deleted \d+ messages/);
    expect(await runInDurableObject(room(), (_i, s) => s.storage.sql.exec('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages').one().c)).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **17.2 Implement.** `worker/chat/admin.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatMode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { requireSecret } from './crypto';
import { parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { chatJson } from './http';
import { GROUNDS, roomName } from './limits';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { identityFromCookie } from './router';
import { modStub } from './stubs';
import { uidHmac } from './session';
import type { ChatRoom } from './room';

const MODES: readonly ChatMode[] = ['open', 'slow', 'readonly', 'closed'];
const LOCALES = ['en', 'es', 'fr', 'pt'] as const;
const roomStub = (env: ChatEnv, slug: string) =>
  (env.CHAT_ROOM as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>).get(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName(roomName(slug)));
const back = () => new Response(null, { status: 303, headers: { Location: '/admin', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
const bad = (msg: string, status = 400) => page('Refused', `<p>${esc(msg)}</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`, status);
const opts = (list: readonly (string | number)[]) => list.map((v) => `<option>${esc(String(v))}</option>`).join('');

async function queuePage(env: ChatEnv, rooms: string[]): Promise<Response> {
  const mod = modStub(env);
  const reports = await mod.openReports();
  const parts: string[] = [];
  const item = async (slug: string, msgId: number, label: string) => {
    const ctx = await roomStub(env, slug).context(msgId, 5);
    const lines = ctx.map((m) => `<li${m.id === msgId ? ' style="font-weight:bold"' : ''}>${esc(m.handle)}: ${esc(m.body || `[${m.state}]`)}</li>`).join('');
    parts.push(`<section style="border:1px solid #888;padding:8px;margin:12px 0"><p>${esc(slug)} #${msgId} · ${esc(label)}</p><ol>${lines}</ol>
<form method="post" action="/admin/decide"><input type="hidden" name="room" value="${esc(slug)}"><input type="hidden" name="msgId" value="${msgId}">
<select name="action">${opts(['remove', 'publish', 'dismiss', 'moot'])}</select> <label><input type="checkbox" name="preserve"> preserve</label> <select name="ground">${opts(GROUNDS)}</select>
<select name="ban">${opts([0, 1, 7, 365])}</select> <button>Apply</button></form></section>`);
  };
  for (const r of reports) await item(r.room, r.msgId, `report: ${r.category} · ${r.reporters} reporter(s) · ${Math.round((Date.now() - r.ts) / 60_000)} min`);
  for (const slug of rooms) for (const m of await roomStub(env, slug).heldList()) {
    if (!reports.some((r) => r.room === slug && r.msgId === m.id)) await item(slug, m.id, `held: ${m.holdCode ?? ''}`);
  }
  const modeForm = `<form method="post" action="/admin/mode"><select name="scope">${opts(['global', ...rooms])}</select>
<select name="mode">${opts(MODES)}</select> minutes <input name="minutes" value="0" inputmode="numeric" size="4"> <button>Set mode</button></form>`;
  return page('Chat admin', `<h1>Queue (${parts.length})</h1>${parts.join('') || '<p>Nothing waiting.</p>'}<h2>Modes</h2>${modeForm}
<p><a href="/admin/terms">Term list</a> · <a href="/admin/health">Health JSON</a></p>
<h2>Erase a user</h2><form method="post" action="/admin/erase">GitHub login <input name="login"> <button>Erase</button></form>
<h2>Purge a room's history</h2><form method="post" action="/admin/purge"><select name="room">${opts(rooms)}</select> type the slug to confirm <input name="confirm"> <button>Purge</button></form>`);
}

export async function handleAdmin(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, path: string): Promise<Response> {
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id?.moderator) return page('Moderators only', '<p>Moderators only.</p><p><a href="/auth/github/start?return=/__admin">Sign in with GitHub</a></p>', 403);
  // Same-origin check on every action (spec §6.4); SameSite=Lax alone would still allow top-level POSTs from elsewhere.
  if (req.method === 'POST' && req.headers.get('Origin') !== new URL(req.url).origin) return bad('Cross-origin action refused.', 403);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  const rooms = [...parseRooms(env.CHAT_ROOMS)];
  const f = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.formData() : null;
  const s = (k: string) => String(f?.get(k) ?? '');
  switch (`${req.method} ${path}`) {
    case 'GET /admin': return queuePage(env, rooms);
    case 'GET /admin/health': return chatJson(await mod.health());
    case 'POST /admin/decide': {
      const slug = s('room'), msgId = Number(s('msgId')), action = s('action'), ground = s('ground'), days = Number(s('ban'));
      if (!rooms.includes(slug) || !Number.isInteger(msgId) || !['publish', 'remove', 'dismiss', 'moot'].includes(action)
        || !(GROUNDS as readonly string[]).includes(ground) || ![0, 1, 7, 365].includes(days)) return bad('Invalid decision.');
      let ban: { uidHmac: string; days: 1 | 7 | 365 } | undefined;
      if (days > 0) {
        const uid = await roomStub(env, slug).authorOf(msgId);
        if (!uid) return bad('Author not found.');
        ban = { uidHmac: await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid), days: days as 1 | 7 | 365 };
      }
      await mod.decide({ room: slug, msgId, action: action as 'publish' | 'remove' | 'dismiss' | 'moot', ground, preserve: s('preserve') === 'on', ...(ban ? { ban } : {}) });
      return back();
    }
    case 'POST /admin/mode': {
      const scope = s('scope'), mode = s('mode') as ChatMode, minutes = Number(s('minutes') || '0');
      if (!MODES.includes(mode) || !Number.isFinite(minutes) || minutes < 0) return bad('Invalid mode.');
      const until = minutes > 0 ? Date.now() + minutes * 60_000 : undefined;
      if (scope === 'global') await mod.setGlobalMode(mode, 'owner', until);
      else if (rooms.includes(scope)) await mod.setRoomMode(scope, mode, 'owner', until);
      else return bad('Unknown room.');
      return back();
    }
    case 'GET /admin/terms': {
      const t = await mod.getTerms();
      const areas = LOCALES.map((l) => `<p>${l}<br><textarea name="${l}" rows="8" cols="40">${esc((t?.terms[l] ?? []).join('\n'))}</textarea></p>`).join('');
      return page('Term list', `<h1>Term list v${t?.version ?? 0}</h1><form method="post" action="/admin/terms">${areas}<button>Save new version</button></form>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/terms': {
      const terms = Object.fromEntries(LOCALES.map((l) => [l, s(l).split('\n').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)])) as Record<(typeof LOCALES)[number], string[]>;
      const total = Object.values(terms).reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0);
      if (total === 0) return bad('An empty list would make every room read-only (filters fail closed). Nothing saved.');
      const version = await mod.setTerms(terms);
      return page('Saved', `<p>Term list v${version} saved: ${total} terms.</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/erase': {
      const login = s('login').trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,39}$/.test(login)) return bad('Not a GitHub login.');
      const gh = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${login}`, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'watchboard-chat', Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json' } });
      if (!gh.ok) return bad(`GitHub answered ${gh.status}; nothing erased.`, 502);
      const uid = `gh:${((await gh.json()) as { id: number }).id}`;
      const r = await mod.erase(uid, await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid));
      return page('Erased', `<p>Erased ${r.erased} messages of ${esc(login)} (re-read per room: ${esc(JSON.stringify(r.perRoom))}).</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/purge': {
      const slug = s('room');
      if (!rooms.includes(slug) || s('confirm') !== slug) return bad('Type the room slug exactly to confirm.');
      const n = await roomStub(env, slug).purge();
      return page('Purged', `<p>Deleted ${n} messages from ${esc(slug)}.</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    default: return bad('Not found.', 404);
  }
}
```

In `room.ts` add:

```ts
  purge(): number {
    const before = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages')[0].c);
    this.sql('DELETE FROM messages');
    const after = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages')[0].c);
    if (after !== 0) throw new Error(`purge left ${after} rows`);
    const m = this.mode();
    this.broadcast({ t: 'mode', mode: m.mode, ...(m.reason ? { reason: m.reason } : {}), ...(m.until ? { until: m.until } : {}) });
    return before;
  }
```

```ts
// notice.ts
export type NoticeKind = 'illegal' | 'csam' | 'terrorism' | 'threat';
export interface NoticeJson { kind: NoticeKind; explanation: string; name?: string; email?: string; goodFaith: true; submittedAt: number }
export async function handleNotice(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response>;   // GET /notice (form, ?room=&id=&lang=), POST /notice, GET /notice/status?ref=&lang=
export function parseNotice(form: FormData, rooms: Set<string>, lang: NoticeLang): { room: string; msgId: number; notice: Omit<NoticeJson, 'submittedAt'> } | { error: string };
export function noticeLang(req: Request): NoticeLang;   // ?lang= first, then Accept-Language, default 'en'
// notice-strings.ts — the form, its errors, the receipt and the status page in en/es/fr/pt (spec §5.4, review #19)
export type NoticeLang = 'en' | 'es' | 'fr' | 'pt';
export const NOTICE_TEXT: Record<NoticeLang, Record<NoticeKey, string>>;
// mod.ts
noticeAllowed(kind: NoticeKind, room: string, msgId: number): boolean;
// csam / terrorism / threat: ALWAYS allowed — the channel for the gravest content can never be locked by a flood (review #18).
// illegal (other): at most NOTICES_PER_MESSAGE_HOUR per message per hour and NOTICES_PER_HOUR in total per hour.
// The flood control for all of /notice is the Cloudflare rate-limiting rule of Task 26.6 (per IP, path-based), not a global counter.
```

A notice about a message that does not exist, or no longer exists, in that room is refused with the localised `noSuchMessage` error and stores nothing (`ChatMod.report` returns `no_such_message`, 14.3). The receipt page shows the reference and a link to `/notice/status?ref=<the reference>`, which shows the status (`open`, `resolved`, `dismissed`, `moot`) and the decision's ground and date — never the content — as DSA art. 16(5) requires (review #19).

- [ ] **18.2 Implement.** `worker/chat/notice.ts`:

```ts
import { parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { modStub } from './stubs';
import { NOTICE_TEXT, type NoticeKey, type NoticeLang } from './notice-strings';

export type NoticeKind = 'illegal' | 'csam' | 'terrorism' | 'threat';
export interface NoticeJson { kind: NoticeKind; explanation: string; name?: string; email?: string; goodFaith: true; submittedAt: number }
const KINDS: readonly NoticeKind[] = ['illegal', 'csam', 'terrorism', 'threat'];
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@<>]{1,64}@[^\s@<>]{1,190}\.[a-z]{2,}$/i;
const LANGS: readonly NoticeLang[] = ['en', 'es', 'fr', 'pt'];

export function noticeLang(req: Request): NoticeLang {
  const q = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get('lang');
  if (q && (LANGS as readonly string[]).includes(q)) return q as NoticeLang;
  for (const part of (req.headers.get('Accept-Language') ?? '').split(',')) {
    const l = part.trim().slice(0, 2).toLowerCase();
    if ((LANGS as readonly string[]).includes(l)) return l as NoticeLang;
  }
  return 'en';
}
export function parseNotice(f: FormData, rooms: Set<string>, lang: NoticeLang) {
  const T = NOTICE_TEXT[lang];
  const s = (k: string) => String(f.get(k) ?? '').trim();
  const room = s('room'), msgId = Number(s('msgId')), kind = s('kind') as NoticeKind, explanation = s('explanation');
  const name = s('name'), email = s('email');
  if (!rooms.has(room) || !Number.isInteger(msgId) || msgId <= 0) return { error: T.errRoom };
  if (!KINDS.includes(kind)) return { error: T.errKind };
  if (explanation.length < 20 || explanation.length > 5000) return { error: T.errWhy };
  if (email && !EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { error: T.errEmail };
  if (name.length > 200) return { error: T.errName };
  if (s('goodFaith') !== 'on') return { error: T.errGoodFaith };
  return { room, msgId, notice: { kind, explanation, goodFaith: true as const, ...(name ? { name } : {}), ...(email ? { email } : {}) } };
}

function form(env: ChatEnv, lang: NoticeLang, room: string, id: string, error?: string, status = 400): Response {
  const T = NOTICE_TEXT[lang];
  const contact = env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL
    ? `${esc(T.contact)} <a href="mailto:${esc(env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL)}">${esc(env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL)}</a>` : esc(T.contactNone);
  const kinds = KINDS.map((k) => `<label><input type="radio" name="kind" value="${k}" required> ${esc(T[`kind_${k}` as NoticeKey])}</label><br>`).join('');
  return page(T.title, `<h1>${esc(T.title)}</h1>${error ? `<p role="alert"><strong>${esc(error)}</strong></p>` : ''}
<p>${esc(T.intro)}</p>
<form method="post" action="/notice?lang=${lang}">
<p>${esc(T.room)} <input name="room" value="${esc(room)}" required> ${esc(T.msg)} <input name="msgId" value="${esc(id)}" inputmode="numeric" required></p>
<fieldset><legend>${esc(T.type)}</legend>${kinds}</fieldset>
<p><label>${esc(T.why)}<br><textarea name="explanation" rows="6" cols="40" minlength="20" maxlength="5000" required></textarea></label></p>
<p><label>${esc(T.name)}<br><input name="name" maxlength="200"></label></p>
<p><label>${esc(T.email)}<br><input name="email" type="email"></label></p>
<p><label><input type="checkbox" name="goodFaith"> ${esc(T.goodFaith)}</label></p>
<p><button>${esc(T.send)}</button></p></form><p>${contact}</p>`, error ? status : 200, lang);
}

async function status(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, lang: NoticeLang): Promise<Response> {
  const T = NOTICE_TEXT[lang];
  const ref = Number(new URL(req.url).searchParams.get('ref'));
  const st = Number.isInteger(ref) && ref > 0 ? await modStub(env).noticeStatus(ref) : null;
  if (!st) return page(T.statusTitle, `<h1>${esc(T.statusTitle)}</h1><p>${esc(T.statusUnknown)}</p>`, 404, lang);
  const decision = st.ground ? `<p>${esc(T.decision)} ${esc(st.ground)} (${esc(new Date(st.decidedAt!).toISOString().slice(0, 10))})</p>` : '';
  return page(T.statusTitle, `<h1>${esc(T.statusTitle)} ${ref}</h1><p>${esc(T[`status_${st.status}` as NoticeKey])}</p>${decision}<p>${esc(T.redress)}</p>`, 200, lang);
}

export async function handleNotice(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const lang = noticeLang(req);
  const T = NOTICE_TEXT[lang];
  if (url.pathname === '/notice/status' && req.method === 'GET') return status(req, env, lang);
  if (req.method === 'GET') return form(env, lang, url.searchParams.get('room') ?? '', url.searchParams.get('id') ?? '');
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return page('405', '<p>Method not allowed.</p>', 405, lang);
  if (req.headers.get('Origin') !== url.origin) return page('403', '<p>Cross-origin submission refused.</p>', 403, lang);
  const f = await req.formData();
  const again = (e: string, code = 400) => form(env, lang, String(f.get('room') ?? ''), String(f.get('msgId') ?? ''), e, code);
  const parsed = parseNotice(f, parseRooms(env.CHAT_ROOMS), lang);
  if ('error' in parsed) return again(parsed.error);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  if (!(await mod.noticeAllowed(parsed.notice.kind, parsed.room, parsed.msgId))) return again(T.tooMany, 429);
  const notice: NoticeJson = { ...parsed.notice, submittedAt: Date.now() };
  const res = await mod.report({ room: parsed.room, msgId: parsed.msgId, category: 'illegal', source: { kind: 'notice', notice } });
  if ('error' in res) return again(T.noSuchMessage);
  // No email provider exists (spec §1): the acknowledgement of receipt is this page, with a reference and a status link.
  return page(T.receivedTitle, `<h1>${esc(T.receivedTitle)}</h1><p>Reference: ${res.id}</p><p>${esc(T.received)}</p>
<p><a href="/notice/status?ref=${res.id}&amp;lang=${lang}">${esc(T.statusLink)}</a></p>`, 200, lang);
}
```

`pages.ts` `page()` gains an optional fourth parameter `lang = 'en'` used for `<html lang>`. In `mod.ts` add:

```ts
  noticeAllowed(kind: NoticeKind, room: string, msgId: number): boolean {
    if (kind === 'csam' || kind === 'terrorism' || kind === 'threat') return true;     // never capped (review #18)
    const hour = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 13);
    const total = `notice:${hour}`, perMsg = `noticemsg:${room}:${msgId}:${hour}`;
    if (Number(this.get(total) ?? 0) >= NOTICES_PER_HOUR || Number(this.get(perMsg) ?? 0) >= NOTICES_PER_MESSAGE_HOUR) return false;
    this.put(total, String(Number(this.get(total) ?? 0) + 1));
    this.put(perMsg, String(Number(this.get(perMsg) ?? 0) + 1));
    return true;
  }
```

(import `NOTICES_PER_HOUR`, `NOTICES_PER_MESSAGE_HOUR` from `./limits`; the mod alarm's hourly-key cleanup of 15.3 already matches `noticemsg:%`.) In `router.ts`, after the admin route and above the `/room/` match: `if (path === '/notice' || path === '/notice/status') return handleNotice(request, env);`, and add the `/notice` line to the CHAT_ENABLED test of `me.test.ts` (16.5).

| key | en | es | fr | pt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| title | Report illegal content | Denunciar contenido ilegal | Signaler un contenu illicite | Denunciar conteúdo ilegal |
| intro | Use this form to notify Watchboard of chat content you consider illegal (EU Digital Services Act, art. 16). No account is needed. For rule breaches that are not illegal, use "Report" in the chat. | Usa este formulario para avisar a Watchboard de contenido del chat que consideres ilegal (Ley de Servicios Digitales de la UE, art. 16). No necesitas cuenta. Para faltas a las normas que no son ilegales, usa "Reportar" en el chat. | Utilisez ce formulaire pour signaler à Watchboard un contenu du chat que vous estimez illicite (règlement européen sur les services numériques, art. 16). Aucun compte n’est nécessaire. Pour une infraction aux règles qui n’est pas illicite, utilisez « Signaler » dans le chat. | Use este formulário para avisar o Watchboard de conteúdo do chat que você considera ilegal (Lei de Serviços Digitais da UE, art. 16). Não é preciso conta. Para infrações às regras que não são ilegais, use "Denunciar" no chat. |
| room | Room | Sala | Salon | Sala |
| msg | Message number | Número de mensaje | Numéro du message | Número da mensagem |
| type | Type | Tipo | Type | Tipo |
| kind_illegal | Illegal content (other) | Contenido ilegal (otro) | Contenu illicite (autre) | Conteúdo ilegal (outro) |
| kind_csam | Child sexual abuse material | Material de abuso sexual infantil | Contenu pédopornographique | Material de abuso sexual infantil |
| kind_terrorism | Terrorist content | Contenido terrorista | Contenu à caractère terroriste | Conteúdo terrorista |
| kind_threat | Threat to life or safety | Amenaza a la vida o la seguridad | Menace pour la vie ou la sécurité | Ameaça à vida ou à segurança |
| why | Why is it illegal? (required) | ¿Por qué es ilegal? (obligatorio) | Pourquoi est-ce illicite ? (obligatoire) | Por que é ilegal? (obrigatório) |
| name | Your name (optional; not needed for child sexual abuse material) | Tu nombre (opcional; no hace falta para material de abuso sexual infantil) | Votre nom (facultatif ; inutile pour un contenu pédopornographique) | Seu nome (opcional; não é necessário para material de abuso sexual infantil) |
| email | Your email (optional; used only to follow up on this notice) | Tu correo (opcional; solo para dar seguimiento a este aviso) | Votre e-mail (facultatif ; utilisé uniquement pour le suivi de ce signalement) | Seu e-mail (opcional; usado só para acompanhar esta denúncia) |
| goodFaith | I confirm in good faith that the information and allegations in this notice are accurate and complete. | Confirmo de buena fe que la información y las alegaciones de este aviso son exactas y completas. | Je confirme de bonne foi que les informations et allégations de ce signalement sont exactes et complètes. | Confirmo de boa-fé que as informações e alegações desta denúncia são exatas e completas. |
| send | Send notice | Enviar aviso | Envoyer le signalement | Enviar denúncia |
| contact | Contact point (DSA arts. 11-12): | Punto de contacto (arts. 11-12 DSA): | Point de contact (art. 11-12 DSA) : | Ponto de contato (arts. 11-12 DSA): |
| contactNone | Contact point: not yet published. | Punto de contacto: aún no publicado. | Point de contact : pas encore publié. | Ponto de contato: ainda não publicado. |
| errRoom | The room or message number is not valid. | La sala o el número de mensaje no son válidos. | Le salon ou le numéro de message n’est pas valide. | A sala ou o número da mensagem não é válido. |
| errKind | Choose a type of notice. | Elige un tipo de aviso. | Choisissez un type de signalement. | Escolha um tipo de denúncia. |
| errWhy | Explain in 20 to 5000 characters why the content is illegal. | Explica en 20 a 5000 caracteres por qué el contenido es ilegal. | Expliquez en 20 à 5000 caractères pourquoi le contenu est illicite. | Explique em 20 a 5000 caracteres por que o conteúdo é ilegal. |
| errEmail | The email address is not valid. | La dirección de correo no es válida. | L’adresse e-mail n’est pas valide. | O endereço de e-mail não é válido. |
| errName | The name is too long. | El nombre es demasiado largo. | Le nom est trop long. | O nome é longo demais. |
| errGoodFaith | The statement of good faith is required (DSA art. 16(2)(d)). | La declaración de buena fe es obligatoria (art. 16(2)(d) DSA). | La déclaration de bonne foi est obligatoire (art. 16(2)(d) DSA). | A declaração de boa-fé é obrigatória (art. 16(2)(d) DSA). |
| noSuchMessage | That message does not exist in that room (it may have been removed already). | Ese mensaje no existe en esa sala (puede que ya se haya retirado). | Ce message n’existe pas dans ce salon (il a peut-être déjà été retiré). | Essa mensagem não existe nessa sala (talvez já tenha sido removida). |
| tooMany | Too many notices about this content right now; please try again in an hour or write to the contact point. | Demasiados avisos sobre este contenido ahora mismo; inténtalo en una hora o escribe al punto de contacto. | Trop de signalements sur ce contenu pour l’instant ; réessayez dans une heure ou écrivez au point de contact. | Denúncias demais sobre este conteúdo agora; tente em uma hora ou escreva ao ponto de contato. |
| receivedTitle | Notice received | Aviso recibido | Signalement reçu | Denúncia recebida |
| received | It is in the moderation queue. Keep the reference number to follow it. | Está en la cola de moderación. Guarda el número de referencia para seguirlo. | Il est dans la file de modération. Conservez le numéro de référence pour le suivre. | Está na fila de moderação. Guarde o número de referência para acompanhá-la. |
| statusLink | Follow this notice | Seguir este aviso | Suivre ce signalement | Acompanhar esta denúncia |
| statusTitle | Notice | Aviso | Signalement | Denúncia |
| statusUnknown | No notice with that reference. | No hay ningún aviso con esa referencia. | Aucun signalement avec cette référence. | Nenhuma denúncia com essa referência. |
| status_open | Open: not yet decided. | Abierto: aún sin decidir. | Ouvert : pas encore décidé. | Aberta: ainda sem decisão. |
| status_resolved | Decided. | Decidido. | Décidé. | Decidida. |
| status_dismissed | Decided: no action, the content was not found illegal or contrary to the rules. | Decidido: sin acción, el contenido no se consideró ilegal ni contrario a las normas. | Décidé : aucune mesure, le contenu n’a pas été jugé illicite ni contraire aux règles. | Decidida: sem ação, o conteúdo não foi considerado ilegal nem contrário às regras. |
| status_moot | Closed: the content was no longer available. | Cerrado: el contenido ya no estaba disponible. | Clos : le contenu n’était plus disponible. | Encerrada: o conteúdo já não estava disponível. |
| decision | Decision and ground: | Decisión y fundamento: | Décision et motif : | Decisão e fundamento: |
| redress | If you disagree, write to the contact point with this reference to ask for a review; you can also go to the courts. | Si no estás de acuerdo, escribe al punto de contacto con esta referencia para pedir una revisión; también puedes acudir a los tribunales. | Si vous n’êtes pas d’accord, écrivez au point de contact avec cette référence pour demander un réexamen ; vous pouvez aussi saisir les tribunaux. | Se discordar, escreva ao ponto de contato com esta referência para pedir revisão; você também pode recorrer aos tribunais. |

### Task 19: Public `/health`, canary room, live test, credential canary

No gate for the code. Step 19.5 (turning `CHAT_ENABLED` on in production, with only `_canary` in `CHAT_ROOMS`) needs the owner's go-ahead in the conversation, and Tasks 16.7, 17.5 done (terms can only be seeded by an admin). Spec §11.4, §12 step 2. Branch `feat/chat-backend`.

**Files:** Create `tests/live/chat.live.test.ts`, `tests/ci/chat-canary-wired.test.ts`. Modify `worker/chat/router.ts`, `worker/test/router.test.ts`, `.github/workflows/credential-canary.yml`.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// router.ts — public, no personal data, no message text; the detailed, live view stays at /admin/health
export interface PublicHealth {
  enabled: true; version: string; globalMode: ChatMode; termsVersion: number; termsCount: number;
  maxBudgetRatio: number; undeliveredAlerts: number; alertsOk: boolean; keyOk: boolean;
  rooms: Record<string, { statsAgeMs: number; lastAlarmAgeMs: number | null; oldestMessageAgeMs: number | null; syncFailed: boolean }>;
}
```

`/health` is unauthenticated and sends `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`, so it must cost at most one DO request per minute per isolate and never fan out (review #9, #30): the router keeps a module-level 60-second cache, the response carries `Cache-Control: public, max-age=60`, and ChatMod answers from the `room_stats` rows the rooms push hourly (14.3 `publicHealth`), with its own 60-second cache.

- [ ] **19.3 Live test.** `tests/live/chat.live.test.ts` (Node's global `WebSocket` honours a `headers` option — checked on Node 26.7 against a local upgrade server that logged `origin= https://watchboard.dev`; if a runtime ignores it, the upgrade gets 403 and the test fails visibly):

```ts
import { request } from 'node:https';
import { expect } from 'vitest';
import { liveDescribe, liveIt, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../helpers/live';

const HOST = 'chat.watchboard.dev';
liveDescribe('chat.watchboard.dev (canary room)', () => {
  liveIt('health reports a deployed version and a loaded term list', async () => {
    const h = await (await fetch(`https://${HOST}/health`)).json() as { enabled: boolean; version: string; termsCount: number };
    expect(h.enabled).toBe(true);
    expect(h.version).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/);
    expect(h.termsCount).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
  liveIt('the canary room answers hello with a mode', async () => {
    const ws = new WebSocket(`wss://${HOST}/room/_canary`, { headers: { Origin: 'https://watchboard.dev' } } as never);
    const hello = await new Promise<{ t: string; mode: string }>((resolve, reject) => {
      ws.onmessage = (e) => { resolve(JSON.parse(String(e.data))); };
      ws.onerror = () => { reject(new Error('socket error')); };
      setTimeout(() => { reject(new Error('no hello in 15 s')); }, 15_000);
    });
    ws.close();
    expect(hello.t).toBe('hello');
    expect(['open', 'slow', 'readonly', 'closed']).toContain(hello.mode);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
  liveIt('a foreign Origin is refused with 403 before the room', async () => {
    const status = await new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
      const req = request({ host: HOST, path: '/room/_canary', headers: { Connection: 'Upgrade', Upgrade: 'websocket', 'Sec-WebSocket-Version': '13',
        'Sec-WebSocket-Key': Buffer.from('0123456789abcdef').toString('base64'), Origin: 'https://evil.example' } });
      req.on('response', (res) => { resolve(res.statusCode ?? 0); res.resume(); });
      req.on('upgrade', () => { reject(new Error('upgraded with a foreign Origin')); });
      req.on('error', reject);
      req.end();
    });
    expect(status).toBe(403);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
});
```

```yaml
      # Chat (spec 2026-09-26 §11.4). The intended state is a repo variable the runbook sets with every
      # lever; "disabled" is a failure unless the owner meant it, and a lit panel over a dark Worker is too.
      - name: Check chat
        id: chat
        continue-on-error: true
        env:
          CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED: ${{ vars.CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED }}
          PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED: ${{ vars.PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED }}
        run: |
          H=$(curl -s --max-time 20 https://chat.watchboard.dev/health) || { echo "::error::chat /health unreachable"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H"
          GOT=$(echo "$H" | jq -r '.enabled')
          WANT=$([ "$CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED" = "true" ] && echo true || echo false)
          [ "$GOT" = "$WANT" ] || { echo "::error::chat enabled=$GOT but CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED=$WANT"; exit 1; }
          [ "$PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED" = "true" ] && [ "$GOT" != "true" ] && { echo "::error::the site mounts the panel but the Worker chat is off"; exit 1; }
          [ "$GOT" = "false" ] && { echo "chat off, as intended"; exit 0; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.version | test("^[0-9a-f]{40}$")' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::no deployed version"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.termsCount > 0' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::term list empty: rooms are read-only"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.maxBudgetRatio < 0.7' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::a room passed 70% of its daily budget"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.undeliveredAlerts == 0' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::chat alerts are not reaching Telegram"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.alertsOk == true' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::the Worker's own Telegram token or chat id is rejected"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '.keyOk == true' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::CHAT_BAN_KEY changed: every identity is refused"; exit 1; }
          # Retention runs in each room's alarm: a room with messages must have run it within 2 h, and nothing may be
          # older than 30 days + 2 h (oldestMessageAgeMs ignores messages under an open report, which stay until decided).
          echo "$H" | jq -e '[.rooms[] | select(.oldestMessageAgeMs != null) | .lastAlarmAgeMs != null and .lastAlarmAgeMs < 7200000] | all' >/dev/null \
            || { echo "::error::a room's retention alarm has not run for 2 h"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '[.rooms[] | .syncFailed | not] | all' >/dev/null || { echo "::error::a room cannot reach ChatMod (writes refused)"; exit 1; }
          echo "$H" | jq -e '[.rooms[] | (.oldestMessageAgeMs // 0) < (30*86400000 + 7200000)] | all' >/dev/null \
            || { echo "::error::a room keeps messages past the 30-day retention"; exit 1; }
```

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';

import { CHAT_MAX_ROOMS } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';

});
```

```ts
import type { TrackerConfig } from '../tracker-config';

/** Build-time only. The flag is `import.meta.env.PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED`; only the exact string "true" turns it on (spec §8 lever 4). */
export function chatEnabledFor(config: Pick<TrackerConfig, 'status' | 'community'>, flag: string | undefined): boolean {
  return flag === 'true' && config.status === 'active' && config.community?.chat === true;
}
```

```bash
# check_absent PATH FORBIDDEN_SUBSTRING LABEL — the page answers 200 and does NOT contain the substring.
check_absent() {
  local path="$1" forbid="$2" label="$3" code
  code=$(curl -sS -o /tmp/smoke-body -w '%{http_code}' "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}${path}") || code=000
  if [ "$code" = "200" ] && ! grep -q -- "$forbid" /tmp/smoke-body; then
    echo "| $label ($path) | ✅ 200, no \`$forbid\` |" >> smoke-summary.md
  else
    echo "| $label ($path) | ❌ $code or contains \`$forbid\` |" >> smoke-summary.md; fail=1
  fi
}
```

### Task 23: Client state machine (reconnect, ack timeout, visible states) and the `chat.*` strings

No gate. Spec §3.4, §5.3, §5.4. Branch `feat/chat-panel`. Pure TypeScript, unit-tested in node; no React yet.

**Files:** Create `src/lib/community/chat-client.ts`, `src/lib/community/chat-client.test.ts`. Modify `src/lib/community/protocol.ts` (`ACK_TIMEOUT_MS`), `src/i18n/translations.ts`.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// protocol.ts
export const ACK_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
// chat-client.ts
export type ConnState = 'idle' | 'connecting' | 'open' | 'retrying' | 'failed';
export interface PendingSend { nonce: string; body: string; lang?: string; status: 'sending' | 'failed'; error?: ChatErrorCode }
export interface ChatState {
  conn: ConnState; loadedOnce: boolean; attempts: number;
  mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number;
  messages: ChatMessage[]; pending: PendingSend[];
  me?: { handle: string; canWrite: boolean; cooldownMs: number; banned?: Sanction };
  lastError?: ChatErrorCode; lastReported?: { id: number; ref: number };
  closeCode?: number;          // the application close code that ended the last socket (4002 closed, 4003 disabled, 4004 full, 4008 reload, 1008 banned)
  cooldownUntil?: number;      // epoch ms before which the send button stays disabled (review #38)
}
export interface SocketLike {
  send(data: string): void; close(code?: number): void;
  onopen: (() => void) | null; onmessage: ((e: { data: unknown }) => void) | null;
  onclose: ((e: { code: number }) => void) | null; onerror: (() => void) | null;
}
export interface ClientDeps {
  connect: (url: string) => SocketLike; now: () => number; random: () => number;
  setTimer: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown; clearTimer: (id: unknown) => void; nonce: () => string;
}
export const BACKOFF_MS: readonly number[];          // [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000, 30_000]; after the last one → 'failed'
export const NO_RETRY_CODES: readonly number[];      // [1008, 4002, 4003, 4004, 4008] — the server said why; retrying would only repeat it
export const HELLO_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;              // a socket that never says hello is a failed attempt (review #39)
// protocol.ts gains BURST_MS = 8_000 and SLOW_BURST_MS = 60_000 (worker/chat/limits.ts re-exports them into LIMITS)
export const initialState: ChatState;
export function backoffMs(attempt: number, random: number): number;     // BACKOFF_MS[attempt] ± 20 %
export function reduce(s: ChatState, f: ServerFrame): ChatState;        // pure
export class ChatClient {
  constructor(url: string, deps: ClientDeps, onChange: (s: ChatState) => void);
  readonly state: ChatState;
  open(): void; close(): void;                        // close() cancels timers; state keeps the messages
  send(body: string, lang?: string): string;          // returns the nonce
  retry(nonce: string): void;                         // re-sends with the SAME nonce (server is idempotent)
  report(id: number, category: ReportCategory): void; remove(id: number): void;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { ChatClient, reduce, initialState, backoffMs, BACKOFF_MS, HELLO_TIMEOUT_MS, type SocketLike, type ChatState } from './chat-client';
import { ACK_TIMEOUT_MS, BURST_MS } from './protocol';

class FakeSocket implements SocketLike {
  sent: string[] = []; closed?: number;
  onopen: SocketLike['onopen'] = null; onmessage: SocketLike['onmessage'] = null;
  onclose: SocketLike['onclose'] = null; onerror: SocketLike['onerror'] = null;
  send(d: string) { this.sent.push(d); }
  close(code = 1000) { this.closed = code; }
  frame(o: unknown) { this.onmessage?.({ data: JSON.stringify(o) }); }
}
function harness() {
  let t = 0; const timers: Array<{ at: number; fn: () => void; id: number }> = []; let n = 0;
  const sockets: FakeSocket[] = []; let state: ChatState = initialState;
  const client = new ChatClient('wss://chat.test/room/x', {
    connect: () => { const s = new FakeSocket(); sockets.push(s); return s; },
    now: () => t, random: () => 0.5, nonce: () => `nonce${String(++n).padStart(4, '0')}`,
    setTimer: (fn, ms) => { const id = ++n; timers.push({ at: t + ms, fn, id }); return id; },
    clearTimer: (id) => { const i = timers.findIndex((x) => x.id === id); if (i >= 0) timers.splice(i, 1); },
  }, (s) => { state = s; });
  const advance = (ms: number) => { t += ms; for (const x of timers.filter((y) => y.at <= t)) { timers.splice(timers.indexOf(x), 1); x.fn(); } };
  return { client, sockets, advance, get state() { return state; }, get timers() { return timers; } };
}
const hello = { t: 'hello', v: 1, mode: 'open', history: [{ id: 1, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/a', body: 'old', state: 'visible' }], me: { handle: 'gh/me', canWrite: true, cooldownMs: 0 } };

describe('chat client', () => {
  let h: ReturnType<typeof harness>;
  beforeEach(() => { h = harness(); });
  it('opens nothing until open() is called', () => { expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(0); expect(h.state.conn).toBe('idle'); });
  it('hello loads history and marks the room open', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].onopen?.(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, messages: [{ id: 1 }], me: { handle: 'gh/me' } });
  });
  it('a send without ack within ACK_TIMEOUT_MS becomes "failed", and retry re-uses the nonce', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('hola', 'es');
    expect(h.state.pending).toEqual([{ nonce, body: 'hola', lang: 'es', status: 'sending' }]);
    h.advance(ACK_TIMEOUT_MS + 1);
    expect(h.state.pending[0].status).toBe('failed');
    h.client.retry(nonce);
    expect(JSON.parse(h.sockets[0].sent.at(-1)!)).toEqual({ t: 'send', body: 'hola', nonce, lang: 'es' });
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'ack', nonce, id: 2, state: 'visible' });
    expect(h.state.pending).toEqual([]);
    expect(h.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 2, body: 'hola', mine: true, handle: 'gh/me' });
  });
  it('a held ack shows the message as held and mine', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('mail me at x@y.org');
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'ack', nonce, id: 3, state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii' });
    expect(h.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 3, state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii', mine: true });
  });
  it('reconnects with backoff, keeps what was loaded, and gives up visibly after the last step', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1006 });
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'retrying', messages: [{ id: 1 }] });
    for (let i = 0; i < BACKOFF_MS.length; i++) { h.advance(60_000); h.sockets.at(-1)!.onclose?.({ code: 1006 }); }
    expect(h.state.conn).toBe('failed');
    expect(h.state.messages).toHaveLength(1);        // "showing the last loaded", never "0 messages"
    expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(BACKOFF_MS.length + 1);
  });
  it('pending sends are re-sent with the same nonce after a reconnect', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('queued');
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1006 });
    h.advance(60_000); h.sockets[1].frame(hello);
    expect(h.sockets[1].sent.map((s) => JSON.parse(s).nonce)).toContain(nonce);
  });
  it('close 1008 (banned) does not reconnect', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello); h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'err', code: 'banned' });
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1008 }); h.advance(120_000);
    expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'failed', lastError: 'banned', closeCode: 1008 });
  });
  it.each([4002, 4003, 4004, 4008])('close %i is an explained refusal: no retry, code kept for the view (review #36)', (code) => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code }); h.advance(120_000);
    expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'failed', closeCode: code });
  });
  it('close 4009 (signed out elsewhere) reconnects at once, anonymously', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);

  });
  it('tracks the cooldown the server announces and the burst after an ack (review #38)', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const n = h.client.send('one');
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'ack', nonce: n, id: 9, state: 'visible' });
    expect(h.state.cooldownUntil).toBe(BURST_MS);            // clock starts at 0 in the harness
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'err', code: 'rate_limited', cooldownMs: 30_000 });
    expect(h.state.cooldownUntil).toBe(30_000);
  });
  it('close() cancels every timer', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello); h.client.send('x'); h.client.close();
    expect(h.timers).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

  it('remove blanks the body and keeps the statement of reasons', () => {

});
```
