- [ ] **23.2 Implement.** Add `export const ACK_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;`, `export const BURST_MS = 8_000;` and `export const SLOW_BURST_MS = 60_000;` to `protocol.ts`, and in `worker/chat/limits.ts` import the last two and use them for `LIMITS.burstMs`/`LIMITS.slowBurstMs` (one source for server and client). `src/lib/community/chat-client.ts` (types from the interface block above, then):

```ts
import { ACK_TIMEOUT_MS, BURST_MS, CLOSE, SLOW_BURST_MS, encode, parseServerFrame, type Sanction, type ServerFrame } from './protocol';
import type { ChatErrorCode, ChatMessage, ChatMode, ChatModeReason, ReportCategory } from './types';

export const BACKOFF_MS = [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000, 30_000] as const;
export const NO_RETRY_CODES = [CLOSE.banned, CLOSE.closed, CLOSE.disabled, CLOSE.full, CLOSE.badFrames] as const;
export const HELLO_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
export const initialState: ChatState = { conn: 'idle', loadedOnce: false, attempts: 0, mode: 'open', messages: [], pending: [] };
export const backoffMs = (attempt: number, random: number) => Math.round(BACKOFF_MS[attempt] * (0.8 + 0.4 * random));

const upsert = (list: ChatMessage[], m: ChatMessage) =>
  list.some((x) => x.id === m.id) ? list.map((x) => (x.id === m.id ? { ...x, ...m } : x)) : [...list, m].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);

export function reduce(s: ChatState, f: ServerFrame): ChatState {
  const modeOf = (x: { mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number }) => ({ mode: x.mode, reason: x.reason, until: x.until });
  switch (f.t) {
    case 'hello': return { ...s, ...modeOf(f), conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, attempts: 0, messages: f.history, me: f.me, lastError: undefined };
    case 'ack': {
      const p = s.pending.find((x) => x.nonce === f.nonce);
      if (!p) return s;
      const m: ChatMessage = { id: f.id, ts: Date.now(), handle: s.me?.handle ?? '', body: p.body, state: f.state, mine: true,
        ...(p.lang ? { lang: p.lang } : {}), ...(f.holdCode ? { holdCode: f.holdCode } : {}) };
      return { ...s, pending: s.pending.filter((x) => x !== p), messages: upsert(s.messages, m) };
    }
    case 'msg': return { ...s, messages: upsert(s.messages, f.m) };
    case 'remove': return { ...s, messages: s.messages.map((m) => (m.id === f.id ? { ...m, body: '', state: 'removed', ...(f.reason ? { reason: f.reason } : {}) } : m)) };
    case 'mode': return { ...s, ...modeOf(f), me: s.me ? { ...s.me, canWrite: f.mode === 'open' || f.mode === 'slow' } : s.me };
    case 'err': return {
      ...s, lastError: f.code,
      pending: f.nonce ? s.pending.map((p) => (p.nonce === f.nonce ? { ...p, status: 'failed', error: f.code } : p)) : s.pending,
    };
    case 'reported': return { ...s, lastReported: { id: f.id, ref: f.ref } };
    default: return s;
  }
}

export class ChatClient {
  private s: ChatState = initialState;
  private ws: SocketLike | null = null;
  private retryTimer: unknown = null;
  private ackTimers = new Map<string, unknown>();
  private closedByUser = false;
  constructor(private url: string, private deps: ClientDeps, private onChange: (s: ChatState) => void) {}
  get state() { return this.s; }
  private set(next: ChatState) { if (next !== this.s) { this.s = next; this.onChange(next); } }
  private clearAcks() { for (const id of this.ackTimers.values()) this.deps.clearTimer(id); this.ackTimers.clear(); }
  private armAck(nonce: string) {
    this.ackTimers.set(nonce, this.deps.setTimer(() => {
      this.ackTimers.delete(nonce);
      this.set({ ...this.s, pending: this.s.pending.map((p) => (p.nonce === nonce && p.status === 'sending' ? { ...p, status: 'failed' } : p)) });
    }, ACK_TIMEOUT_MS));
  }
  private transmit(p: PendingSend) {
    this.ws?.send(encode({ t: 'send', body: p.body, nonce: p.nonce, ...(p.lang ? { lang: p.lang } : {}) }));
    this.armAck(p.nonce);
  }
  private helloTimer: unknown = null;
  private clearHello() { if (this.helloTimer !== null) { this.deps.clearTimer(this.helloTimer); this.helloTimer = null; } }
  open() {
    this.closedByUser = false;
    this.set({ ...this.s, conn: this.s.attempts ? 'retrying' : 'connecting', closeCode: undefined });
    const ws = this.deps.connect(this.url);
    this.ws = ws;
    // An old client meeting a newer server, or a proxy that swallows frames, must not sit on "Connecting…" forever.
    this.helloTimer = this.deps.setTimer(() => {
      this.helloTimer = null;
      ws.onclose = null; ws.close(4000);
      this.handleClose(1006);
    }, HELLO_TIMEOUT_MS);
    ws.onmessage = (e) => {
      const f = typeof e.data === 'string' ? parseServerFrame(e.data) : null;
      if (!f) return;                                   // an unknown frame type is dropped; unknown keys were already ignored
      if (f.t === 'hello') this.clearHello();
      if (f.t === 'ack') { const id = this.ackTimers.get(f.nonce); if (id !== undefined) { this.deps.clearTimer(id); this.ackTimers.delete(f.nonce); } }
      let next = reduce(this.s, f);
      const now = this.deps.now();
      if (f.t === 'ack') next = { ...next, cooldownUntil: now + (next.mode === 'slow' ? SLOW_BURST_MS : BURST_MS) };
      if (f.t === 'err' && f.cooldownMs) next = { ...next, cooldownUntil: now + f.cooldownMs };
      if (f.t === 'hello' && f.me?.cooldownMs) next = { ...next, cooldownUntil: now + f.me.cooldownMs };
      this.set(next);
      if (f.t === 'hello') for (const p of this.s.pending) if (p.status === 'sending') this.transmit(p);
    };
    ws.onclose = (e) => this.handleClose(e.code);
    ws.onerror = () => { /* onclose follows and decides */ };
  }
  private handleClose(code: number) {
    this.ws = null; this.clearAcks(); this.clearHello();
    if (this.closedByUser) return;
    if (code === CLOSE.revoked) {                       // signed out on another device: reconnect now, as a reader
      this.set({ ...this.s, me: undefined, attempts: 0 });
      this.retryTimer = this.deps.setTimer(() => { this.retryTimer = null; this.open(); }, 0);
      return;
    }
    const give = (NO_RETRY_CODES as readonly number[]).includes(code) || this.s.attempts >= BACKOFF_MS.length;
    if (give) { this.set({ ...this.s, conn: 'failed', closeCode: code, pending: this.s.pending.map((p) => ({ ...p, status: 'failed' })) }); return; }
    const wait = backoffMs(this.s.attempts, this.deps.random());
    this.set({ ...this.s, conn: 'retrying', attempts: this.s.attempts + 1 });
    this.retryTimer = this.deps.setTimer(() => { this.retryTimer = null; this.open(); }, wait);
  }
  close() {
    this.closedByUser = true;
    this.clearHello();
    if (this.retryTimer !== null) { this.deps.clearTimer(this.retryTimer); this.retryTimer = null; }
    this.clearAcks();
    this.ws?.close(1000); this.ws = null;
    this.set({ ...this.s, conn: 'idle', attempts: 0 });
  }
  send(body: string, lang?: string): string {
    const p: PendingSend = { nonce: this.deps.nonce(), body, status: 'sending', ...(lang ? { lang } : {}) };
    this.set({ ...this.s, pending: [...this.s.pending, p] });
    if (this.s.conn === 'open') this.transmit(p);
    return p.nonce;
  }
  retry(nonce: string) {
    const p = this.s.pending.find((x) => x.nonce === nonce);
    if (!p) return;
    const again: PendingSend = { nonce: p.nonce, body: p.body, status: 'sending', ...(p.lang ? { lang: p.lang } : {}) };
    this.set({ ...this.s, pending: this.s.pending.map((x) => (x === p ? again : x)) });
    if (this.s.conn === 'open') this.transmit(again);
  }
  report(id: number, category: ReportCategory) { this.ws?.send(encode({ t: 'report', id, category })); }
  remove(id: number) { this.ws?.send(encode({ t: 'delete', id })); }
}
```

The test's `hello` frames reach `conn: 'open'` without `onopen` because the server sends `hello` first; `onopen` is not needed. In the browser, the panel passes `connect: (u) => new WebSocket(u) as unknown as SocketLike` (the browser sends the `Origin` itself; the session cookie rides on the same-site handshake, spec §3.6), `setTimer: (f, ms) => window.setTimeout(f, ms)`, `nonce: () => crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16)`.

Run `npx vitest run src/lib/community/chat-client.test.ts src/lib/community/separation.test.ts src/lib/community/protocol.test.ts`. Expected: all PASS.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// chat-view.ts — pure, node-tested; the panel only renders what these return
export const CHAT_HOST_URL = 'https://chat.watchboard.dev';
export const roomUrl = (slug: string) => `wss://chat.watchboard.dev/room/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`;
export interface StatusLine { key: TranslationKey; reasonKey?: TranslationKey; until?: number; retry: boolean }
export function statusLine(s: ChatState): StatusLine | null;                 // spec §5.3, one state → one line, never "0 messages"
export function messageNote(m: ChatMessage): { key: TranslationKey; params?: Record<string, string> } | null;  // removed / held-mine
export function fill(text: string, params: Record<string, string | number>): string;   // '{n} new' → '5 new'
export function announcement(arrivals: number[], now: number): { grouped: false } | { grouped: true; n: number };  // > 3 in 10 s → grouped
export const IDLE_CLOSE_MS = 120_000;                                         // tab hidden / chat tab left > 2 min → socket closed (spec §5.1, §5.6)
// ChatPanel.tsx
export default function ChatPanel(p: { slug: string; locale: Locale; active: boolean; onClose?: () => void; returnPath: string }): JSX.Element;
// ChatButton.tsx (client:idle) — renders the button only; ChatPanel is a lazy chunk loaded on first open
export default function ChatButton(p: { slug: string; locale: Locale; returnPath: string }): JSX.Element;
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { statusLine, messageNote, fill, announcement } from './chat-view';
import { initialState, type ChatState } from './chat-client';

const s = (o: Partial<ChatState>): ChatState => ({ ...initialState, ...o });
const msg = { id: 1, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/a', body: 'x', state: 'visible' as const };
describe('statusLine (spec §5.3)', () => {
  it('connecting before anything loaded', () => expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'connecting' }))?.key).toBe('chat.connecting'));
  it('failed keeps showing what was loaded and offers retry — never an empty room', () => {
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'failed', loadedOnce: true, messages: [msg] }))).toEqual({ key: 'chat.failed', retry: true });
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'failed', loadedOnce: false }))).toEqual({ key: 'chat.failed', retry: true });
  });
  it('empty only when open, loaded and really empty', () => {
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true }))?.key).toBe('chat.empty');
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, messages: [msg] }))).toBeNull();
  });
  it('read-only carries its concrete reason and reopening time', () => {
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget', until: 99 })))
      .toEqual({ key: 'chat.readonly', reasonKey: 'chat.reason.daily_budget', until: 99, retry: false });
  });
  it('closed says closed', () => expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, mode: 'closed', reason: 'owner' }))?.key).toBe('chat.closed'));
  it('slow mode shows its reason', () => expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, mode: 'slow', reason: 'auto_slow', messages: [msg] }))?.key).toBe('chat.reason.auto_slow'));
  it.each([
    [4002, 'chat.closed'], [4003, 'chat.err.chat_disabled'], [4004, 'chat.err.room_full'], [4008, 'chat.reloadNeeded'], [1008, 'chat.err.banned'],
  ] as const)('an explained refusal (close %i) shows its own line with no retry (review #36)', (code, key) => {
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'failed', closeCode: code, loadedOnce: true, messages: [msg] }))).toMatchObject({ key, retry: false });
  });
  it('the daily budget reads as a pause until 00:00 UTC, never as a quiet room (spec §7.4)', () => {
    expect(statusLine(s({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget', until: 5 }))).toMatchObject({ reasonKey: 'chat.reason.daily_budget', until: 5 });
  });
});
describe('messageNote', () => {
  it('removed with and without a statement of reasons', () => {
    expect(messageNote({ ...msg, state: 'removed', body: '', reason: 'rules §2 spam or flooding' })).toEqual({ key: 'chat.removed', params: { reason: 'rules §2 spam or flooding' } });
    expect(messageNote({ ...msg, state: 'removed', body: '' })).toEqual({ key: 'chat.removedNoReason' });
  });
  it('held is labelled only for its author', () => {
    expect(messageNote({ ...msg, state: 'held', mine: true })).toEqual({ key: 'chat.heldMine' });
    expect(messageNote({ ...msg, state: 'visible' })).toBeNull();
  });
});
describe('helpers', () => {
  it('fill replaces every placeholder', () => expect(fill('{n} of {n} · {ref}', { n: 2, ref: 'x' })).toBe('2 of 2 · x'));
  it('announcements group above 3 messages in 10 s', () => {
    expect(announcement([1_000, 2_000, 3_000], 5_000)).toEqual({ grouped: false });
    expect(announcement([1_000, 2_000, 3_000, 4_000, 9_000], 10_000)).toEqual({ grouped: true, n: 5 });
    expect(announcement([1_000, 2_000, 3_000, 4_000], 30_000)).toEqual({ grouped: false });
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { TranslationKey } from '../../i18n/translations';
import type { ChatState } from './chat-client';
import { CLOSE } from './protocol';
import type { ChatMessage } from './types';

export const CHAT_HOST_URL = 'https://chat.watchboard.dev';
export const roomUrl = (slug: string) => `wss://chat.watchboard.dev/room/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`;
export const IDLE_CLOSE_MS = 120_000;
export interface StatusLine { key: TranslationKey; reasonKey?: TranslationKey; until?: number; retry: boolean }

const REFUSALS: Record<number, TranslationKey> = {
  [CLOSE.closed]: 'chat.closed', [CLOSE.disabled]: 'chat.err.chat_disabled', [CLOSE.full]: 'chat.err.room_full',
  [CLOSE.badFrames]: 'chat.reloadNeeded', [CLOSE.banned]: 'chat.err.banned',
};
export function statusLine(s: ChatState): StatusLine | null {
  if (s.conn === 'failed' && s.closeCode !== undefined && REFUSALS[s.closeCode]) return { key: REFUSALS[s.closeCode], retry: false };
  if (s.conn === 'failed') return { key: 'chat.failed', retry: true };
  if (s.conn === 'connecting' || s.conn === 'retrying' || (s.conn === 'idle' && !s.loadedOnce)) return { key: 'chat.connecting', retry: false };
  const reasonKey = s.reason ? (`chat.reason.${s.reason}` as TranslationKey) : undefined;
  if (s.mode === 'closed') return { key: 'chat.closed', ...(reasonKey ? { reasonKey } : {}), ...(s.until ? { until: s.until } : {}), retry: false };
  if (s.mode === 'readonly') return { key: 'chat.readonly', ...(reasonKey ? { reasonKey } : {}), ...(s.until ? { until: s.until } : {}), retry: false };
  if (s.mode === 'slow') return { key: reasonKey ?? 'chat.reason.owner', ...(s.until ? { until: s.until } : {}), retry: false };
  if (s.loadedOnce && s.messages.length === 0) return { key: 'chat.empty', retry: false };
  return null;
}
export function messageNote(m: ChatMessage): { key: TranslationKey; params?: Record<string, string> } | null {
  if (m.state === 'removed') return m.reason ? { key: 'chat.removed', params: { reason: m.reason } } : { key: 'chat.removedNoReason' };
  if (m.state === 'held' && m.mine) return { key: 'chat.heldMine' };
  return null;
}
export const fill = (text: string, params: Record<string, string | number>) =>
  text.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (all, k: string) => (k in params ? String(params[k]) : all));
export function announcement(arrivals: number[], now: number) {
  const recent = arrivals.filter((t) => now - t <= 10_000).length;
  return recent > 3 ? { grouped: true as const, n: recent } : { grouped: false as const };
}
```

Run the test → PASS. `npx vitest run src/lib/community/separation.test.ts` → PASS.

In the panel (24.3): the send button is also disabled while `Date.now() < state.cooldownUntil` (re-rendered by a one-shot `setTimeout` to that instant, cleared on unmount), so a reader in slow mode is never surprised by a refusal; when `state.me.banned` is set, the composer is replaced by `L('chat.bannedUntil', { date, ground })` followed by `L('chat.redress')`; every `messageNote` of a removed message of mine is followed by `L('chat.redress')` (DSA art. 17(3)(f), review #20); `chat.redress` is also appended to the legal `<details>`.

- [ ] **24.3 The panel.** `src/components/islands/community/ChatPanel.tsx` (plain text only — React text nodes, never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, spec §6.4):

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { t, type Locale } from '../../../i18n/translations';
import { ChatClient, initialState, type ChatState, type SocketLike } from '../../../lib/community/chat-client';
import { announcement, CHAT_HOST_URL, fill, IDLE_CLOSE_MS, messageNote, roomUrl, statusLine } from '../../../lib/community/chat-view';
import { MAX_BODY_CHARS, REPORT_CATEGORIES } from '../../../lib/community/protocol';
import './chat.css';

interface Props { slug: string; locale: Locale; active: boolean; onClose?: () => void; returnPath: string }

export default function ChatPanel({ slug, locale, active, onClose, returnPath }: Props) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ChatState>(initialState);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const [autoScroll, setAutoScroll] = useState(true);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [live, setLive] = useState('');
  const arrivals = useRef<number[]>([]);
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLOListElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  const L = (k: Parameters<typeof t>[0], p?: Record<string, string | number>) => (p ? fill(t(k, locale), p) : t(k, locale));

  const client = useMemo(() => new ChatClient(roomUrl(slug), {
    connect: (u) => new WebSocket(u) as unknown as SocketLike, now: () => Date.now(), random: Math.random,
    setTimer: (f, ms) => window.setTimeout(f, ms), clearTimer: (id) => window.clearTimeout(id as number),
    nonce: () => crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16),
  }, setState), [slug]);

  // Socket only while the panel is shown; closed after IDLE_CLOSE_MS hidden (tab in background or chat tab left).
  useEffect(() => {
    let idle: number | undefined;
    const visible = () => active && document.visibilityState === 'visible';
    const sync = () => {
      window.clearTimeout(idle);
      if (visible()) { if (client.state.conn === 'idle') client.open(); }
      else idle = window.setTimeout(() => client.close(), IDLE_CLOSE_MS);
    };
    sync();
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', sync);
    return () => { document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', sync); window.clearTimeout(idle); };
  }, [active, client]);
  useEffect(() => () => client.close(), [client]);

  // Focus: the input when signed in, else the sign-in link (spec §5.5).
  useEffect(() => { if (active) (inputRef.current ?? document.getElementById(`chat-signin-${slug}`))?.focus(); }, [active, state.me, slug]);

  const count = state.messages.length;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!count) return;
    arrivals.current = [...arrivals.current, Date.now()].slice(-20);
    const a = announcement(arrivals.current, Date.now());
    setLive(a.grouped ? L('chat.newMessages', { n: a.n }) : '');
    if (autoScroll) listRef.current?.lastElementChild?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' });
  }, [count]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const status = statusLine(state);
  const canWrite = !!state.me?.canWrite;
  const left = MAX_BODY_CHARS - [...draft].length;
  const submit = () => { const b = draft.trim(); if (b && left >= 0) { client.send(b, locale); setDraft(''); } };
  const account = async (path: string, method: 'GET' | 'POST') => {
    const res = await fetch(`${CHAT_HOST_URL}${path}`, { method, credentials: 'include' });
    if (!res.ok) { setNotice(L(`chat.err.${res.status === 401 ? 'auth_required' : 'unknown'}` as never)); return null; }
    return res;
  };

  return (
    <section className="cc-chat" data-pagefind-ignore aria-labelledby={`chat-h-${slug}`} onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose?.(); }}>
      <header className="cc-chat__head">
        <h2 id={`chat-h-${slug}`}>{L('chat.header')}</h2>
        <details><summary>{L('chat.rulesLink')}</summary><p>{L('chat.rules')}</p></details>
        <details><summary>{L('chat.legalLink')}</summary><p>{L('chat.legal')}</p></details>
        {onClose && <button type="button" className="cc-chat__close" onClick={onClose}>{L('chat.close')}</button>}
      </header>
      {status && (
        <p className="cc-chat__status" role="status">
          {L(status.key)}{status.reasonKey ? ` ${L(status.reasonKey)}` : ''}
          {status.until ? ` ${L('chat.reopensAt', { time: new Date(status.until).toLocaleTimeString(locale, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) })}` : ''}
          {status.retry && <button type="button" onClick={() => client.open()}>{L('chat.retryConnect')}</button>}
        </p>
      )}
      <ol className="cc-chat__log" role="log" aria-live="polite" ref={listRef}>
        {state.messages.map((m) => {
          const note = messageNote(m);
          return (
            <li key={m.id} className={`cc-chat__msg cc-chat__msg--${m.state}`} lang={m.lang}>
              <span className="cc-chat__who">{m.moderator ? L('chat.moderator') : m.handle}</span>{' '}
              {m.body && <span className="cc-chat__body">{m.body}</span>}
              {note && <em className="cc-chat__note">{L(note.key, note.params)}</em>}
              {m.state === 'visible' && (m.mine
                ? <button type="button" onClick={() => client.remove(m.id)}>{L('chat.delete')}</button>
                : state.me
                  ? <select aria-label={L('chat.report')} value="" onChange={(e) => client.report(m.id, e.target.value as never)}>
                      <option value="">{L('chat.report')}</option>
                      {REPORT_CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{L(`chat.cat.${c}` as never)}</option>)}
                    </select>
                  : <a href={`${CHAT_HOST_URL}/notice?room=${encodeURIComponent(slug)}&id=${m.id}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener">{L('chat.report')}</a>)}
            </li>
          );
        })}
        {state.pending.map((p) => (
          <li key={p.nonce} className="cc-chat__msg cc-chat__msg--pending">
            <span className="cc-chat__body">{p.body}</span>
            {p.status === 'failed' && <><em className="cc-chat__note">{L('chat.notSent')}{p.error ? ` · ${L(`chat.err.${p.error}` as never)}` : ''}</em>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => client.retry(p.nonce)}>{L('chat.retry')}</button></>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      <p className="cc-chat__sr" aria-live="polite">{live}</p>
      {state.lastReported && <p role="status">{L('chat.reportSent', { ref: state.lastReported.ref })}</p>}
      {state.lastError && !state.pending.some((p) => p.error) && <p role="alert">{L(`chat.err.${state.lastError}` as never)}</p>}
      {notice && <p role="alert">{notice}</p>}
      <button type="button" className="cc-chat__scroll" onClick={() => setAutoScroll(!autoScroll)}>{L(autoScroll ? 'chat.pauseScroll' : 'chat.resumeScroll')}</button>
      {state.me ? (
        <form className="cc-chat__compose" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); submit(); }}>
          <textarea ref={inputRef} value={draft} disabled={!canWrite} rows={2} placeholder={L('chat.placeholder')} aria-label={L('chat.placeholder')}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); submit(); } }} />
          {left <= 50 && <span aria-live="polite">{L('chat.charsLeft', { n: left })}</span>}
          <button type="submit" disabled={!canWrite || left < 0 || !draft.trim()}>{L('chat.send')}</button>
        </form>
      ) : (
        <a id={`chat-signin-${slug}`} className="cc-chat__signin" href={`${CHAT_HOST_URL}/auth/github/start?return=${encodeURIComponent(returnPath)}`}>{L('chat.signIn')}</a>
      )}
      {state.me && (
        <details className="cc-chat__account"><summary>{state.me.handle}</summary>
          <button type="button" onClick={async () => { if (await account('/auth/logout', 'POST')) client.close(); location.reload(); }}>{L('chat.signOut')}</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={async () => {
            const res = await account('/me/export', 'GET');
            if (!res) return;
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(await res.blob());
            const a = Object.assign(document.createElement('a'), { href: url, download: 'watchboard-chat-export.json' });
            a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
          }}>{L('chat.download')}</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={async () => {
            if (!window.confirm(L('chat.deleteConfirm'))) return;
            const res = await account('/me/erase', 'POST');
            if (!res) return;
            const { erased } = (await res.json()) as { erased: number };
            client.close();
            setNotice(L('chat.deletedCount', { n: erased }));   // the count the Worker re-read, never assumed
          }}>{L('chat.deleteAccount')}</button>
        </details>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **24.4 Desktop button + drawer, and the styles.** `src/components/islands/community/ChatButton.tsx`:

```tsx
import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { t, type Locale } from '../../../i18n/translations';

const ChatPanel = lazy(() => import('./ChatPanel'));   // panel + socket code load on first open only (spec §5.1)

export default function ChatButton({ slug, locale, returnPath }: { slug: string; locale: Locale; returnPath: string }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const btn = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => { if (location.hash === '#community') { setOpen(true); setLoaded(true); } }, []);   // back from GitHub sign-in
  const close = () => { setOpen(false); btn.current?.focus(); };
  return (
    <div className="cc-chat-root" data-chat-root={slug}>
      <button ref={btn} type="button" className="cc-chat-button" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls={`chat-drawer-${slug}`}
        onClick={() => { setLoaded(true); setOpen(!open); }}>
        {t('chat.button', locale)}
      </button>
      {loaded && (
        <div id={`chat-drawer-${slug}`} className="cc-chat-drawer" role="dialog" aria-modal="false" aria-label={t('chat.button', locale)} hidden={!open}>
          <Suspense fallback={<p>{t('chat.connecting', locale)}</p>}>
            <ChatPanel slug={slug} locale={locale} active={open} onClose={close} returnPath={returnPath} />
          </Suspense>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/islands/community/chat.css` (body font, neutral surface, dashed frame; none of the tokens Task 9 forbids):

```css
.cc-chat-button { font: inherit; min-height: 44px; padding: 0 16px; border: 1px dashed var(--border-light); border-radius: 6px;
  background: var(--bg-secondary); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; margin: 12px 0; }
.cc-chat-drawer { position: fixed; top: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; width: min(420px, 100vw); z-index: 900; overflow-y: auto;
  background: var(--bg-secondary); border-left: 1px dashed var(--border-light); box-shadow: -8px 0 24px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.35); }
.cc-chat { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; padding: 16px; font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
  color: var(--text-primary); border: 1px dashed var(--border-light); border-radius: 6px; background: var(--bg-card); min-height: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; }
.cc-chat__head { position: sticky; top: 0; background: var(--bg-card); padding-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border); }
.cc-chat__head h2 { font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 600; margin: 0 0 4px; }
.cc-chat__log { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; }
.cc-chat__msg { padding: 6px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.cc-chat__msg--held, .cc-chat__msg--pending { opacity: 0.75; }
.cc-chat__who { font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-secondary); }
.cc-chat__note, .cc-chat__status { color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem; }
.cc-chat__sr { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); }
.cc-chat button, .cc-chat select, .cc-chat__signin { min-height: 44px; font: inherit; }
.cc-chat__compose { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: flex-end; position: sticky; bottom: 0; background: var(--bg-card); }
.cc-chat__compose textarea { flex: 1; font: inherit; resize: vertical; max-width: 100%; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) { .cc-chat__msg { animation: cc-chat-in 120ms ease-out; } }
@keyframes cc-chat-in { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
@media (max-width: 768px) { .cc-chat-root { display: none; } }   /* phones use the mobile tab */
```

### Task 25: End-to-end — the rendered panel against a scripted room

No gate. Spec §11.3. Branch `feat/chat-panel`. The Worker itself is covered by Tasks 7-19 in workerd; here Playwright's `page.routeWebSocket` (Playwright ≥ 1.48; the repo has `^1.58.2`) plays the room so every state of spec §5.3 is reproducible. The real panel↔Worker path is exercised in the rehearsal (Task 26).

**Files:** Create `e2e/chat/with-chat-tracker.mjs`, `playwright.chat.config.ts`, `e2e/chat.spec.ts`. Modify `playwright.config.ts` (`testIgnore`), `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`.

- [ ] **25.1 A dev server with one chat-enabled tracker, restored on exit.** `e2e/chat/with-chat-tracker.mjs`:

```js
// Runs a command with trackers/cdmx/tracker.json temporarily carrying community.chat = true.
// Refuses to start on a dirty file; restores the exact original bytes on any exit.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { execFileSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';

const FILE = 'trackers/cdmx/tracker.json';
if (execFileSync('git', ['status', '--porcelain', '--', FILE], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim()) {
  console.error(`${FILE} has local changes; refusing to patch it`); process.exit(2);
}
const original = readFileSync(FILE, 'utf8');
writeFileSync(FILE, JSON.stringify({ ...JSON.parse(original), community: { chat: true } }, null, 2) + '\n');
let restored = false;
const restore = () => { if (!restored) { writeFileSync(FILE, original); restored = true; } };
const [cmd, ...args] = process.argv.slice(2);
const child = spawn(cmd, args, { stdio: 'inherit', env: { ...process.env, PUBLIC_CHAT_ENABLED: 'true' } });
for (const sig of ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM']) process.on(sig, () => { child.kill(sig); });
child.on('exit', (code) => { restore(); process.exit(code ?? 1); });
process.on('exit', restore);
```

- [ ] **25.2 The spec.** `e2e/chat.spec.ts` (written before running it once; every test must fail on the Task 24 code if its assertion is inverted — check two of them by inverting and reverting):

```ts
import { test, expect, type Page, type WebSocketRoute } from '@playwright/test';

const ROOM = 'wss://chat.watchboard.dev/room/cdmx';
const HEADERS = { en: 'Not a Watchboard source', es: 'No es una fuente de Watchboard', fr: 'Pas une source de Watchboard', pt: 'Não é uma fonte do Watchboard' };
type Frame = Record<string, unknown>;
async function room(page: Page, hello: Frame | null, onClient?: (ws: WebSocketRoute, f: Frame) => void) {
  await page.routeWebSocket(ROOM, (ws) => {
    if (hello === null) { ws.close({ code: 1011, reason: 'down' }); return; }
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'hello', v: 1, mode: 'open', history: [], ...hello }));
    ws.onMessage((m) => onClient?.(ws, JSON.parse(String(m))));
  });
}
const openPanel = async (page: Page, path = './cdmx/') => {
  await page.goto(path);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Community|Comunidad|Communauté|Comunidade/ }).click();
  return page.locator('.cc-chat');
};

test.describe('community chat panel', () => {
  test.use({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 } });

  test('no WebSocket before the panel opens; one after', async ({ page }) => {
    const sockets: string[] = [];
    page.on('websocket', (w) => { if (w.url().includes('chat.watchboard.dev')) sockets.push(w.url()); });
    await room(page, {});
    await page.goto('./cdmx/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Community' }).waitFor();
    await page.waitForTimeout(2_000);
    expect(sockets).toEqual([]);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Community' }).click();
    await expect.poll(() => sockets.length).toBe(1);
  });

  for (const [loc, text] of Object.entries(HEADERS)) {
    test(`header says "not a source" in ${loc}`, async ({ page }) => {
      await room(page, {});
      const panel = await openPanel(page, loc === 'en' ? './cdmx/' : `./${loc}/cdmx/`);
      await expect(panel.getByRole('heading', { level: 2 })).toContainText(text);
    });
  }

  test('an empty room says so', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, {});
    await expect(await openPanel(page)).toContainText('No messages yet. Be the first to comment.');
  });

  test('read-only shows its concrete reason, not an empty room', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, { mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget' });
    const panel = await openPanel(page);
    await expect(panel).toContainText('daily limit reached');
    await expect(panel).not.toContainText('No messages yet');
  });

  test('a dead room ends in "could not connect", never an empty room', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.clock.install();
    await room(page, null);
    const panel = await openPanel(page);
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) await page.clock.runFor(60_000);
    await expect(panel).toContainText('Could not connect to the chat');
    await expect(panel).not.toContainText('No messages yet');
  });

  test('a held message is labelled for its author only', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, { me: { handle: 'gh/me', canWrite: true, cooldownMs: 0 },
      history: [{ id: 1, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/me', body: 'mail me at a@b.org', state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii', mine: true }] });
    const panel = await openPanel(page);
    await expect(panel).toContainText('Pending review — only you can see it');
  });

  test('messages live only inside the panel, as text', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, { history: [{ id: 7, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/x', body: 'e2e-unique-7781 <b>bold</b>', state: 'visible' }] });
    await openPanel(page);
    await expect(page.getByText('e2e-unique-7781', { exact: false })).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(page.locator('.cc-chat').getByText('<b>bold</b>', { exact: false })).toHaveCount(1);   // shown literally
    await expect(page.locator('b', { hasText: 'bold' })).toHaveCount(0);
  });

  test('keyboard: focus goes to sign-in, Esc closes and returns focus, log role present', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, {});
    const panel = await openPanel(page);
    await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Sign in with GitHub to write' })).toBeFocused();
    await expect(panel.getByRole('log')).toBeVisible();
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Community' })).toBeFocused();
  });

  test('a send is acked and shows as mine; no CSP violations on the way', async ({ page }) => {
    const csp: string[] = [];
    page.on('console', (m) => { if (/Content Security Policy|Refused to connect/i.test(m.text())) csp.push(m.text()); });
    await room(page, { me: { handle: 'gh/me', canWrite: true, cooldownMs: 0 } }, (ws, f) => {
      if (f.t === 'send') ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'ack', nonce: f.nonce, id: 11, state: 'visible' }));
    });
    const panel = await openPanel(page);
    await panel.getByRole('textbox').fill('hello from e2e');
    await panel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
    await expect(panel.locator('.cc-chat__msg--visible')).toContainText('hello from e2e');
    expect(csp).toEqual([]);
  });
});

test.describe('mobile', () => {
  test.use({ viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 } });
  test('the chat tab exists after the four data tabs and the composer stays visible', async ({ page }) => {
    await room(page, { me: { handle: 'gh/me', canWrite: true, cooldownMs: 0 } });
    await page.goto('./cdmx/');
    const tabs = page.getByRole('tab');
    await expect(tabs).toHaveCount(5);
    await expect(tabs.nth(4)).toHaveText(/Chat/);
    await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { selected: true })).toHaveText(/Feed/);   // initial tab unchanged
    await tabs.nth(4).click();
    await expect(page.locator('#tabpanel-chat textarea')).toBeInViewport();
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **26.2 The CLI.** `worker/tools/chat-cli.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Drill client for the chat: connects as https://watchboard.dev with a session cookie taken
// from the browser after signing in at https://chat.watchboard.dev/auth/github/start (env CHAT_COOKIE,
// the value of __Host-wb_chat). Prints every server frame as one JSON line with a timestamp.
export function parseArgs(argv) {
  const o = { room: '_canary', host: 'chat.watchboard.dev', flood: 0, report: null, say: null };
  let meanIt = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const a = argv[i], v = argv[i + 1];
    if (a === '--room') { o.room = v; i++; }
    else if (a === '--host') { o.host = v; i++; }
    else if (a === '--flood') { o.flood = Number(v); i++; }
    else if (a === '--say') { o.say = v; i++; }
    else if (a === '--report') { const [id, category] = v.split(':'); o.report = { id: Number(id), category }; i++; }
    else if (a === '--i-mean-it') meanIt = true;
    else throw new Error(`unknown argument ${a}`);
  }
  if (o.room !== '_canary' && !meanIt) throw new Error('drills run in _canary; pass --i-mean-it for another room');
  return o;
}
async function main() {
  const o = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const headers = { Origin: 'https://watchboard.dev', ...(process.env.CHAT_COOKIE ? { Cookie: `__Host-wb_chat=${process.env.CHAT_COOKIE}` } : {}) };
  const ws = new WebSocket(`wss://${o.host}/room/${o.room}`, { headers });
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const nonce = () => crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 16);
  ws.onmessage = (e) => console.log(JSON.stringify({ ms: Date.now() - t0, ...JSON.parse(String(e.data)) }));
  ws.onclose = (e) => { console.log(JSON.stringify({ ms: Date.now() - t0, closed: e.code })); process.exit(0); };
  ws.onopen = async () => {
    if (o.say) ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'send', body: o.say, nonce: nonce() }));
    if (o.report) ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'report', id: o.report.id, category: o.report.category }));
    for (let i = 0; i < o.flood; i++) { ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'send', body: `flood ${i}`, nonce: nonce() })); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100)); }
  };
}
if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) main();
```

- [ ] **26.3 Timed kill-switch drill (owner, stopwatch; spec §8, §11.5.1).** Terminal A: `CHAT_COOKIE=<owner cookie> node worker/tools/chat-cli.mjs` (leave it open; it prints each `mode` frame with its `ms`). For each lever, note the wall-clock time from the action to the observable effect, then undo it:
  1. `/admin` → `_canary` → `readonly` → terminal A prints `{"t":"mode","mode":"readonly",...}`.
  2. `/admin` → global `closed` → terminal A prints the `mode` frame; `curl -s https://chat.watchboard.dev/health | jq -r .globalMode` → `closed`; a new `node worker/tools/chat-cli.mjs` gets its upgrade refused (prints `closed: 1006` without a `hello`).
  3. **From the phone**: GitHub app → Actions → "Chat kill switch" → `enabled=false` (Task 5.4); time until the run's "Verify by value" step is green and terminal A prints `{"closed":4003}` on reconnect. Confirm `/admin` still loads and `/notice` still answers while off. Put it back with `enabled=true`. Then repeat once from the laptop with `npx wrangler secret put CHAT_ENABLED --config ../wrangler.toml` as the fallback, and time that too.
  4. Time one `gh workflow run deploy.yml` from dispatch to "deployed" (the lever-4 latency; the flag itself is already off).
  5. `gh workflow disable deploy-worker.yml`, then Cloudflare dashboard → Workers → watchboard-push → Domains → remove `chat.watchboard.dev`; time until `curl -s https://chat.watchboard.dev/health` fails; re-add the domain, time until it answers again, and `gh workflow enable deploy-worker.yml` (runbook lever 5: without the first step a deploy re-attaches the domain).
  6. **TCO one-hour drill** (spec §6.2 bis): the owner starts a stopwatch, posts a test line in `_canary` from terminal A, and — acting as if a removal order had arrived by email — removes it from `/admin` on the phone. Record the time; anything over 15 minutes in attended hours is a finding for the runbook.

- [ ] **26.4 Red team (owner + 2-3 test GitHub accounts ≥ `CHAT_MIN_ACCOUNT_AGE_DAYS` old; 30 min; spec §11.5.2).** With one terminal per account (`CHAT_COOKIE=… node worker/tools/chat-cli.mjs --say '…'`), try: obfuscated slurs from the term list (spacing, leet, diacritics, zero-width characters), a phone number, an email, an IBAN, a street address, a link and its obfuscated forms (`hxxps://`, `t[.]me/x`, `example dot com`, `example . com`, a link after three harmless messages), a message in Cyrillic or Arabic script, `--flood 50`, the same text from three accounts, three accounts reporting one message (`--report <id>:harassment`), each reserved handle if an account with a similar login exists, and from a signed-out shell: 50 anonymous connections in a loop (the room must stay writable for a signed-in account; review #4), 10 `/notice` posts about one message and one about a non-existent id (the room mode must not change; review #15). For every attempt record: expected verdict, observed frame, pass/fail. Every **fail** becomes a test case in `worker/chat/filters.test.ts` (with a synthetic term standing in for a real slur — the real list never enters the repo) that fails first, then a fix in `filters.ts`/`normalize.ts`, then `npm --prefix worker test` green. - [ ] **26.6 The Free-plan rate-limiting rule (precondition of Task 27; review #5, #28).** Cloudflare Free allows exactly **one** rule, a **10 s** counting period, a **10 s** mitigation and only the **Path** and Verified Bot fields — no host, no 60 s block (checked 2026-09-26 at `https://developers.cloudflare.com/waf/rate-limiting-rules/`; Task 6.1 re-checks it). Dashboard → `watchboard.dev` zone → Security → WAF → Rate limiting rules → one rule, expression `starts_with(http.request.uri.path, "/room/") or starts_with(http.request.uri.path, "/notice") or starts_with(http.request.uri.path, "/auth/")`, characteristic IP, 20 requests / 10 s, action Block, duration 10 s. The expression is zone-wide by path: no page of the static site lives under `/room/`, `/notice` or `/auth/` (check with `ls dist | grep -E '^(room|notice|auth)$'` → nothing), and push paths (`/subscribe`, `/unsubscribe`, `/preferences`, `/newsletter/*`) are not matched. Verify with `for i in $(seq 1 40); do curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' "https://chat.watchboard.dev/notice?i=$i"; done | sort | uniq -c` → some `429`. Record in ADR-0003 that this slows a burst but does **not** stop a low-rate script (one request every 0.6 s passes forever); the residual risk to the shared account quota is accepted there (spec §7.2), and the only full isolation is a separate Cloudflare account. **Q8** now decides only whether Turnstile is added to `/notice` and `/auth/github/start` (worker-served pages, spec §6.5); record the answer in the runbook.

### Task 27: Closed pilot (1-2 low-polarisation trackers, 30 days)

**BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Q6, Q7, Q11** (Q1 and Q2 were answered before Task 7; Q5 through Task 15.5, which must be merged: spec §4.5 says a pilot room opens only with attended hours). Also required before 27.4: the Free-plan rate-limiting rule of 26.6 is live, the TCO drill of 26.3.6 is recorded, and Q6 names the contact point for TCO removal orders and the owner receives that mailbox on the phone (spec §6.2 bis). Nothing in this task starts until every one is answered in writing; the executor copies the answers verbatim into ADR-0003 first. Spec §12 steps 5-6. Branch `feat/chat-pilot`.

**What this pilot can and cannot prove** (spec §12, review #22): with 1-2 low-polarisation rooms and no announcement it measures whether anyone uses the chat at all (Q2's N) and what one person's moderation load is when the load is small (Q2's M). It says nothing about brigading, doxxing or terrorist content on a conflict tracker. A conflict room is therefore never an "expansion" of this pilot: it needs its own spec, with the preconditions of 27.7.

When the run is green: `curl -s https://watchboard.dev/<slug>/ | grep -o 'component-url="[^"]*ChatButton[^"]*"' | wc -l` → 1 for each pilot slug and 0 for `iran-conflict`; `npm run test:live -- tests/live/chat.live.test.ts` → 3 passed; open the pilot page in a phone browser, open the Chat tab, sign in, write one message, delete it. Announce nothing publicly until the owner says so.

- [ ] **27.6 Daily log for 30 days.** Create `docs/runbooks/chat-pilot-log.md` with this header and one row per day, filled by the owner from `/admin/health` (counters are per UTC day and reset at midnight, so copy them before 00:00 UTC) plus the minutes spent moderating:

```markdown
# Chat pilot log (spec §12 step 5)
Criteria (Q2): ≥ N accepted/week/room = <N>; ≤ M moderation min/day = <M>; zero grave reports unattended > 60 min.
| Date (UTC) | Room | Accepted | Held | Removed | Reports (grave) | Panel opens (conns) | Max budget ratio | Moderation min | Incidents |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
```

(`<N>` and `<M>` are the Q2 numbers written in 27.1.) Commit weekly: `git add docs/runbooks/chat-pilot-log.md && git commit -m "docs(chat): pilot log week <n>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>"`.

- [ ] **27.7 Day-30 decision (owner; spec §12 step 6).** Against the Q2 criteria: **retire** → levers 4 then 3 (runbook), `community.chat: false`, `CHAT_ROOMS = "_canary"`; retention empties the rooms within 30 days, or `/admin/purge` at once; **keep** → nothing changes; **a conflict tracker** → never an expansion of this pilot (see "What this pilot can and cannot prove"): it is a new spec + plan, and that spec must at least require slow mode by default in the room, attended hours with the room `closed` (no history served) outside them, zero unattended grave incidents in this pilot, a timed TCO one-hour removal drill passed from the phone, the tracker's regional languages (e.g. Arabic, Hebrew, Persian, Russian, Ukrainian) either excluded by rule and by the `language` hold or covered by term lists **and** a reviewer who reads them, and a time-boxed trial with its own success and abort criteria (reviews #17, #22, #23).

**Silent-success guards added by this plan** (each fails loudly when its work goes nowhere): the row cost per send is measured from `cursor.rowsWritten`, not estimated (Task 13); erasure answers 502 and names unreachable rooms instead of reporting a partial success (Tasks 14, 16); the canary fails on "disabled" unless the owner set `CHAT_EXPECTED_ENABLED=false`, on a rejected Worker Telegram token, a changed ban key, a retention alarm older than 2 h and a room that cannot reach ChatMod (Task 19); a failed deploy smoke rolls back (Task 5); Worker tests are part of `npm test` and CI (Task 2 guard test); the CSP test sees `push`, `wss://chat` and `https://chat` (Tasks 3, 22, each with a "confirm the failure message" step); `/newsletter/send` answers 501 (Task 4); deploys are red when the smoke fails (Tasks 5, 7.7); `ack` only after the SQL write; erasure and purge return re-read counts and throw if rows remain (Tasks 13.6, 17); undelivered alerts are counted and surface in `/health` and the canary (Tasks 13.6, 19); the canary checks values, not exit codes, and "disabled on purpose" is distinguished from "down" (Task 19.4); a tracker cannot enable the panel without a Worker room (Task 20); the dark build is compared markup-for-markup (Task 24.7); bracket paths are verified in the commit (Task 24.9); the e2e wrapper restores `tracker.json` and CI checks `git status` indirectly via `chat-rooms-consistent` (Task 25).

**Placeholder scan.** Values in angle brackets appear only inside steps marked `BLOCKED ON OWNER DECISION Qn` (`<Q3 answer>`, `<Q6 address>`, `<Q6 controller>`, `<Q7>`, `<Q1 slugs>`, `<N>`/`<M>` of Q2), where the owner's answer is the value and the code has no default for it, and in the ADR heading template `<fecha>`.

**Adversarial review (2026-09-26).** 45 findings, verified one by one; the log with verdicts is at the end of the spec ("Adversarial review log"). The plan changes they caused: pinned `@cloudflare/workers-types ^5.20260815.1` (Task 1); push regression for `POST /subscribe` and the cron path, auto-rollback, lever 3 from a phone (Tasks 1, 5); indexed schema, `WITHOUT ROWID` tables, measured row costs and ceilings (Tasks 7, 13); no daily cap on anonymous connections, anonymous concurrency cap (D10); no `await` before the INSERT (D12); room registry and pull-based sync (D4); message-level provisional holds, never room-level on one report (D13); evidence preservation, `moot`, `preserve` (Tasks 13-14); ban statements with ground and date, expired-hold stubs, redress text (Tasks 13-15, 23-24); links always held, non-Latin text held (Task 10); EU jurisdiction gate and `stubs.ts` (Task 7); cached `/health`, no auth-start writes (Tasks 16, 19); CHAT_ENABLED no longer blocks `/admin`, `/notice`, `/me` (Tasks 7, 16); ban-key fingerprint, Worker-side Telegram probe, retention freshness and expected-state canary (Tasks 13-15, 19); explained refusals with close codes, tolerant parsers, hello timeout, client cooldown (Tasks 7-8, 13, 23-24); localised notice form, notice status page, uncapped grave notice kinds (Task 18); TCO drill and conflict-room preconditions (Tasks 26-27); Free-plan WAF rule rewritten for what Free allows (Task 26.6); Q1/Q2 and EU jurisdiction moved before Task 7.

**Type consistency checked across tasks:** `modStub`/`roomStub` (`stubs.ts`, Task 7) and `identityFromCookie` (Task 13.4) are the only way `oauth.ts`, `me.ts`, `admin.ts`, `notice.ts`, `room.ts` and `mod.ts` reach another object; `ChatMod.health()` (admin, live) and `publicHealth()` (public, cached) are separate; `ChatMod.report()` takes a `source` (`reader` | `notice`) and can answer `{ error: 'no_such_message' }`, which `onReport` and `/notice` both handle; `openReports()` returns an explicit row type, which Task 17 reads; `ChatEnv` gains `CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL` in Task 18; `CHAT_MAX_ROOMS`, `MAX_BODY_CHARS`, `MAX_FRAME_BYTES`, `ACK_TIMEOUT_MS` live in `protocol.ts` and `limits.ts` re-exports the Worker-side ones; `MobileTab` gains `'chat'` and `MobileTabBar` takes `tabs` from the shell (Task 24.5).

**Known risks for the executor:** (1) if miniflare gives `wrangler.toml [vars]` precedence over test bindings, Task 7.6 stops and reports rather than restructuring environments; (2) GitHub may not support PKCE for OAuth apps — Task 16.1 verifies and records it instead of sending unsupported parameters; (3) Node's `WebSocket` `headers` option was verified on Node 26.7 only — on Node 22 in CI the live test would fail visibly (403), not pass silently; (4) the Cloudflare figures are re-checked in Task 6.1 and any change recomputes the budget before Task 7.
